Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [91] [Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Prediction from Video Game State with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21290)
*Jonas Peché,Aliaksei Tsishurou,Alexander Zap,Günter Wallner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多任务学习在基于视频游戏状态数据的预测任务中的应用，提出了一种结合光栅化视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和单位状态信息的多模态架构，并在《坦克世界》大规模数据集上验证了相比单任务模型，多任务训练能提升泛化能力并降低训练和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏遥测技术能够从相同的结构化观测数据中提供多个相关的监督信号，多任务学习有望利用这些信号提升模型泛化能力并降低计算成本。

Method: 采用多模态架构，将光栅化视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和单位状态信息通过图像编码器和基于注意力的交互建模整合，适配为通用的多任务端点预测设置。

Result: 在《坦克世界》大型数据集上，多任务训练相比单任务模型提升了泛化能力，同时降低了训练和推理成本；实验还比较了混合损失和冲突梯度的加权策略，测试了有限目标数据下的预训练/微调效果，并研究了游戏地图间的迁移性能。

Conclusion: 多任务学习在团队类多人游戏中能有效提升模型泛化性并降低计算开销，为游戏遥测数据的预测任务提供了实用的训练范式。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is a promising approach for prediction tasks derived from video game state data, as modern game telemetry provides multiple related supervision signals from the same structured observations. We study whether a shared model trained jointly across tasks in team-based multiplayer games can improve generalization while reducing training and inference cost compared to specialized single-task models. We adapt a multimodal architecture for endpoint prediction to a general multi-task setting that combines rasterized vision inputs, global match context, and per-unit state information through an image encoder and attention-based interaction modeling. Experiments on a large proprietary World of Tanks dataset compare single-task and multi-task training, evaluate weighting strategies for mixed losses and conflicting gradients, and test pre-training/fine-tuning under limited target-data regimes. We also examine within-game transfer across game maps under structured environment shift.

</details>


### [92] [AI Assistants Overassist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21306)
*Verona Teo,Raghav Jain,Tobias Gerstenberg,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Int-Bench，一个基于模拟的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在学习过程中的干预行为。研究发现，与人类教师相比，LLM倾向于更频繁、更早地干预，并且更倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示，这表明当前LLM助教更优化短期任务成功而非支持深度学习所需的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被用作导师和思维伙伴，但它们在问题解决过程中如何做出干预决策（何时、是否以及如何干预）仍未被充分理解。过度或过早的干预可能会阻碍真正的学习和认知参与，因此需要系统评估LLM的干预行为。

Method: 提出了Int-Bench基准，采用"学生-教师"模拟框架：模拟一个"学生"解决问题，同时一个"教师"（LLM）监控学生推理并决定是否、何时以及如何干预。在代码调试、数学和脑力急转弯三个领域进行评估，比较LLM与人类教师在干预频率、时机以及对即时任务成功和泛化能力的影响。

Result: LLM比人类教师干预更频繁、更早。与人类教师不同，LLM倾向于提供完整的解决方案而不是有针对性的提示。

Conclusion: 当前LLM助教往往优化短期任务成功，而非支持深度学习所需的推理过程，这限制了其在促进长期学习成功方面的效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as tutors and thought partners, helping users reason through problems. While guidance from AI assistants can scaffold thinking and foster learning, such benefits depend on how they help--for instance, intervening too early or too frequently may hinder true learning and cognitive engagement. Yet how AI systems navigate intervention decisions during problem-solving remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Int-Bench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating LLM interventions during learning. Int-Bench simulates a "student" solving a problem while a "teacher" monitors the student's reasoning and decides whether, when, and how to intervene. Across three domains--code debugging, mathematics, and brain teasers--we evaluate LLM teachers on the frequency and timing of interventions, as well as their impact on both immediate task success and generalization to new problems. We also compare LLMs to humans, finding that LLMs intervene more frequently and earlier than humans. Moreover, in contrast to humans, they tend to provide complete solutions rather than targeted hints. These findings suggest that current LLM assistants often optimize for short-term success rather than supporting the reasoning processes needed for deeper learning and long-term success.

</details>


### [93] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M³-Gen框架，通过条件生成对抗网络，利用组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，解决了基因表达数据获取成本高和隐私限制的问题，并在TCGA数据集上验证了其生成结果的真实性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质生物医学数据的整合对于全面理解疾病至关重要，但基因表达数据的获取受限于高昂成本和隐私问题，限制了其在多模态研究和AI驱动应用中的使用。

Method: 提出M³-Gen框架，将生成对抗网络以组织病理学图像和临床元数据为条件，通过对比学习学习统一潜在表示，利用两种模态的嵌入引导生成模型产生生物学上一致的基因表达谱，并采用注意力机制实现多模态融合和内在可解释性。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上的评估表明，M³-Gen能够生成真实且功能上有意义的基因表达数据，并能通过注意力机制识别组织病理学图像中影响特定基因表达生成的区域。

Conclusion: M³-Gen框架通过组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，在实现高质量生成的同时提供了模型决策的可解释性，为多模态生物医学研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [94] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过压缩分析测量低秩适配器（LoRA）在冻结基座模型上实际写入的比特数，发现其记忆容量远小于全参数微调，且取决于参数位置而非数量：MLP层的记忆效率是注意力层的两倍，而基座模型的结构完整性对适配器容量至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"LoRA适配器是技能而非数据记录"的普遍假设，量化低秩适配器在冻结模型上实际存储的信息量。

Method: 将压缩记忆化分析扩展到冻结基座设置，直接测量低秩适配器在不改变基座模型的情况下写入的比特数。

Result: 每个可训练参数仅存储几个比特，远低于完整模型的预算；MLP层的记忆容量约为注意力层的两倍；剥离基座模型结构后适配器容量几乎消失；隐私泄露与写入比特数相关而非参数数量；监督微调会逐字复制秘密，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习适配器不会记录隐私信息。

Conclusion: 将关于LoRA的民间经验转化为可量化的设计指标，为隐私保护微调提供理论依据。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [95] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验发现，基于显著性的权重选择在表示级机器遗忘中并无实质贡献，遗忘效果主要由梯度集中性和表征几何结构主导，而非具体选择的权重子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于梯度显著性的权重选择方法在机器遗忘中被广泛采用，但其对表示级遗忘的实际贡献尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对这类机制进行严格控制的消融分析。

Method: 采用匹配计算量的实验设计，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-18，比较基于显著性的掩码、等稀疏度随机掩码和无约束更新三种策略，保持遗忘目标、优化计划和计算预算不变。使用线性探测、原型恢复和层间CKA等多种表示级评估方法。

Result: 三种策略在表示级可恢复性上统计等价。遗忘梯度高度集中在最后几层（CIFAR-10上约92%的平方梯度能量）。显著性掩码的类别特异性极低（特异性指数0.09-0.11），不同遗忘类别选择的高度重叠的参数子集。

Conclusion: 表示级遗忘主要由梯度集中性和表征几何结构决定，而非具体显著性选择的权重子集。有效的表示级遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表示的学习目标，而非依赖日益复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [96] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比性教师强制方法从冻结的对齐指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct）中提取出领域个性子空间，发现窄域微调会激活预先存在的低秩个性核心，导致广泛失对齐。将该子空间从残差流中投影出去可完全阻止广泛失对齐（从27.7%降至0.0%），而注入该子空间可诱导失对齐至45.4%。后验权重编辑无法根除该结构，且坏数据跨领域分布会加剧失对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在对齐语言模型上进行窄域有害微调后，为何会产生超出训练数据范围的广泛失对齐（emergent misalignment）现象。

Method: 使用对比性教师强制从冻结的Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct中提取每个领域的个性子空间；通过投影操作残差流进行干预实验；进行后验权重编辑；比较跨领域坏数据分布效果。

Result: 4个无关领域共享一个低秩核心（随机子空间信噪比657倍），82%的核心位于风格核心之外；第一个优化步即出现失对齐信号；投影子空间可将失对齐从27.7%降至0.0%；注入子空间可诱导失对齐至45.4%；后验编辑无法根除该结构；跨领域分散坏数据产生更多失对齐。

Conclusion: 窄域微调通过招募模型中预先存在的个性结构导致广泛失对齐，该结构可被提取、转移和干预，但难以通过后验编辑根除。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [97] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE框架，将网络压缩从离散域转换到希尔伯特连续函数空间，通过将神经元建模为秩1希尔伯特-施密特算子，统一了剪枝和神经元合并为低秩子空间投影，并引入宏块驱逐以统一度量多层结构，实现跨层无偏架构决策，是一个无数据、无超参数的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂表征，但解构其内部知识仍具挑战。鉴于学习与压缩之间的联系，网络压缩为分析这类知识提供了有前景的视角，但标准压缩方法常受尺度对称性和架构偏差影响，需要一种更优的解决方案。

Method: HOPE将网络压缩从离散域转移到连续函数的希尔伯特空间；将单个神经元建模为秩1希尔伯特-施密特算子，统一剪枝和神经元合并为低秩子空间投影；引入宏块驱逐以在同一统一度量下涵盖多层结构（如完整残差路径）。

Result: 提出了概念验证实验，在模型压缩和微调中展示了该理论的实际潜力。HOPE是一个无数据且无超参数的框架。

Conclusion: HOPE通过希尔伯特空间建模实现了无偏的跨层架构决策，为网络压缩和分析提供了一种统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [98] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法，通过预测干净令牌的后验均值并经过精确的线性映射转换为分数，解决了离散扩散模型中分数比率违反贝叶斯可实现性的问题，在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText数据集上均取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD)方法虽然通过正数约束保证了反向跳转速率的非负性，但并未确保分数比率满足贝叶斯可实现性——即噪声状态下的分数比率不一定由任意干净令牌后验通过前向核诱导产生。实验发现，在训练好的纯均匀SEDD检查点中，约四分之一的分数向量违反坐标箱约束，超过一半虽然位于箱内但与任何有效后验不兼容，导致有限步采样中出现负的预归一化权重，损害生成质量。

Method: 提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法：首先预测干净令牌的后验均值，然后通过精确的、依赖于核的线性映射将其转换为分数。该方法适用于任何满足温和支撑条件的已知坐标连续时间马尔可夫链。对于均匀腐蚀，M2S将概率单纯形映射到桥接多面体上；对于吸收掩码腐蚀，恢复出的目标函数与MD4完全一致。

Result: 在CIFAR-10 (28.4M参数)上，M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著降低。在170M参数、约262B OpenWebText令牌槽训练的模型中，M2S在所有测试采样预算下均优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC检查点，128步时生成PPL达143.3，而最强纯均匀基线为183.6。

Conclusion: 通过投影原始分数到桥接多面体上可以消除所有观察到的负权重，并将外部生成PPL从203.6改进至175.1。M2S提供了一种 principled 的方式来保证离散扩散模型中的分数满足贝叶斯可实现性约束，在多个基准上实现了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [99] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JITAI-Twins方法，利用条件时间序列扩散模型为移动健康干预中的在线算法生成数字孪生体，用于在部署前模拟和评估算法效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中的在线学习算法若设计不当会加重用户负担、降低参与度，因此需要在部署前对算法设计进行充分验证，但目前缺乏能够真实模拟目标亚人群的数字孪生方法。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型构建数字孪生体，确保时间一致性（未来动作不影响已生成的历史）。支持三种信息源的迭代更新：大规模观测数据预训练、相关人群小规模干预数据微调、领域专家知识推理时校准。

Result: 在HeartSteps系列（v2到v4）中验证，该方法在再现目标亚人群的时间结构和参与者间结构方面优于简单模拟器。

Conclusion: 该方法可在真实部署前模拟目标干预场景，为测试和指导在线算法设计决策提供前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [100] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对合作式多任务语义通信（CMT-SemCom）中任务间语义关系可能导致的破坏性合作问题，提出一种语义感知的任务聚类方法，通过层次密度聚类将语义对齐的任务分组后进行端到端联合训练，有效缓解负迁移，提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式多任务语义通信中，任务间的语义关系可能产生破坏性合作（负迁移），影响整体执行性能。现有方法未对任务语义关系进行区分，需要一种机制确保只有语义对齐的任务之间进行合作。

Method: 提出语义感知的任务聚类框架：1）经过短时初始训练后，利用层次密度空间聚类（HDBSCAN）对任务进行语义聚类；2）在聚类形成的任务组内部进行端到端联合训练，不同组之间不共享表示，避免负迁移。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架能有效缓解破坏性合作与负迁移，相比未聚类的多任务学习和独立训练基线，取得了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过语义感知的聚类方法，可以将合作限制在语义对齐的任务组内，从而在CMT-SemCom中实现建设性合作而非破坏性合作，提升系统性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [101] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部上下文信息的离散流匹配改进方法，通过上下文加权采样器和缩放交叉熵损失函数，在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低高达63%，且无需额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的因子化训练目标使模型同时面对易预测和模糊高熵的令牌，导致训练信号质量不均。作者发现令牌的不确定性与其邻域上下文密度密切相关，因此希望通过引入局部上下文信息来改善生成质量。

Method: 1) 提出上下文加权采样器：修改底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC），融入局部上下文信息，在几乎无额外计算开销下提升生成质量。2) 提出缩放交叉熵损失函数：根据不同令牌的上下文信息重新加权训练信号。

Result: 在OpenWebText上生成困惑度降低高达63%；在质量上与强半自回归块扩散基线相当，同时保留任意顺序生成的能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的重要因素，简单的上下文感知改进即可显著提升采样和训练效率。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant是一种针对扩散Transformer的4-bit后训练量化方法，采用Kronecker结构可逆变换处理激活值异常点，以W4A4精度在保持图像质量的同时比SmoothQuant快14%，参数少于per-channel scaling，在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell上优于SVDQuant和LoRaQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiTs）在W4A4量化时，激活值中的异常点导致4-bit格式无法有效表示，严重降低输出质量。现有方案在量化质量与推理成本之间存在权衡：SmoothQuant计算廉价但量化精度差；固定Hadamard变换需要大块大小，在线开销高；学习全d维可逆变换校准效果好但需要密集矩阵乘法，成本过高。

Method: 提出KroQuant方法：1）对激活值每个32元素块应用学习得到的Kronecker结构可逆变换，参数少于per-channel scaling；2）块局部结构以小tensor-core GEMMs运行；3）离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收残差权重量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器内核比SmoothQuant内核快14%；在W4A4（MXFP4e2）精度下，对PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell的量化输出在MJHQ-30K和SDCI上比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，同时保持或改善图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构可逆变换在量化质量与推理成本之间取得更优平衡，以更少参数实现更好加速和量化精度，是DiTs后训练量化的有效方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [103] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTEL算法，通过令牌级错误定位（利用固定或环境反馈比较条件概率），在推理阶段截断错误轨迹并重生成，最大化复用有效前缀。在LiveCodeBench上，Qwen3-8B达到71.0% pass@64，生成令牌数仅为独立采样的约一半（360.4k vs 735.0k）；在数学基准AIME-2025和HMMT-2025上同样优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法（独立采样、多轮精炼）缺乏令牌级信用分配，有效推理前缀常被丢弃，导致计算效率低下。

Method: TTEL利用固定或环境反馈，通过比较知情反馈下的条件概率与空上下文基线，定位错误发生的令牌位置，截断轨迹并分支生成新内容，最大化复用有效前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上Qwen3-8B pass@64达71.0%，生成360.4k令牌（独立采样需735.0k）；在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025上，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均优于对比基线。

Conclusion: TTEL在顺序推理领域建立了严格帕累托主导的权衡曲线，验证了令牌级错误定位在提升推理时计算效率上的有效性。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [104] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性KV缓存驱逐无法感知其丢弃内容的重要性，会导致注意力输出误差无限增长；而随机化驱逐（泊松采样+哈杰克校正）可恢复可识别性，并提供每步误差认证（经验覆盖率达0.97）。预注册实验表明：基于问题的驱逐在25-50%预算下近乎免费；输出对数概率比认证更能预测失败；认证门控的预算扩展无效。核心发现是随机化带来归因能力（AUC 0.73-0.75），而非预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性KV缓存驱逐方法（保留top-k重要token）无法感知被丢弃内容的影响，导致注意力输出误差无法被准确估计，缺乏可证明的误差保证。

Method: 提出随机化驱逐机制：对尾部token进行泊松采样（已知包含概率），通过哈杰克校正（一个logit偏移量）修正softmax，并利用调查抽样方差估计器提供每步误差证书。在真实工作负载上进行预注册实验验证七个声明。

Result: 预注册七个声明中三个被证伪：基于问题的驱逐在25-50%预算下近乎免费；输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败；证书门控的预算扩展无效果。成功验证的归因能力：证书能区分缓存导致与固有的失败（AUC 0.73-0.75），优于输出置信度（AUC 0.47-0.54），且能比随机或置信度门控更好地调度重计算。

Conclusion: 随机化驱逐的核心价值在于归因（attribution）而非预测（prediction），即提供对缓存引入误差的可信估计能力，而非对模型整体失败的直接预测。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [105] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在经验管道的初始高召回阶段中，如何通过有限样本有效认证遗漏的相关项数量很小。核心发现是：仅使用候选集内部标签无法认证有意义的遗漏量下界，必须对排除池进行采样。作者证明了匹配的有限语料下界，并开发了基于二项和超几何分布反演的精确实用工具包，用于遗漏量认证、召回率转换、嵌套生成器族认证及压力测试认证，所有保证在候选生成器和审计规则预先固定的前提下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经验管道中，初始高召回阶段决定哪些项目进入后续审查、标注或建模，而遗漏的相关项将永远丢失。确保遗漏的相关项质量很小至关重要，但需要多少审计标签才能以有限样本有效性认证这一点尚不清楚。本文旨在刻画该问题的标签复杂度，并提供实用的认证工具。

Method: 首先证明仅使用候选集内部标签无法认证任何非平凡的遗漏量下界，必须对排除池进行采样。接着证明在零遗漏场景下，任何有效审计若要以高概率认证遗漏少于m个相关项，必须检查约N₀/m个排除池标签（N₀为排除池大小），表明排除池审计是极小极大速率最优的。基于此，开发了使用二项和超几何分布反演而非渐近近似的精确实用工具包。

Result: 证明了排除池审计是遗漏量认证在零遗漏场景下的极小极大速率最优策略；开发了完整的有限样本认证工具包，包括遗漏量认证、通过双池设计转换为召回率、预指定嵌套生成器族的同时认证，以及针对声明扰动机制的压力测试认证。

Conclusion: 本文为经验管道中遗漏相关项的认证问题提供了理论基础和实用工具。所有保证的核心条件是：候选生成器（或其预指定族）和审计规则必须在检查认证标签之前固定。这些证书可与可观测的审查负担配对，以选择满足遗漏量目标的最轻负担的预指定候选生成器。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [106] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS)，一种分层深度强化学习架构，通过中央Master生成全局协调嵌入（proto-plan），由分散Worker智能体执行车辆控制，在无信号交叉口实现零冲突导航并显著降低旅行时间，且展现良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无信号交叉口的自动驾驶车辆协调是多智能体强化学习中的关键挑战，现有方法难以处理组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或存在刚性智能体设计。

Method: 提出分层DRL架构MAPS：中央Master智能体生成紧凑连续嵌入（proto-plan）编码全局协调策略，分散Worker智能体结合该嵌入与局部观测执行车辆控制，实现策略意图与战术执行的解耦。

Result: 在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航，平均旅行时间显著优于基线；三智能体训练的系统零样本迁移至五智能体场景时成功率达94%。

Conclusion: 基于proto-plan的分层学习为多车协调提供了有前景的框架，证明该机制能有效解耦全局策略与局部执行，并展现良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [107] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对百万级长上下文场景中MTP草稿头因全注意力机制导致开销剧增的问题，提出Windowed-MTP方法：仅在草稿注意力上应用滑动窗口+注意力汇聚(StreamingLLM风格)，保留目标模型的全注意力验证。该方案无需训练、即插即用、无损（验证决定接受与否），将草稿KV工作集限定为常数，丢弃约99%的KV条目。在Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B和Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B上，单GPU 1M上下文下，每解码步成本降低28%-44%，端到端延迟改善相同幅度，且能回收7.7-11%的草稿KV内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码中，MTP草稿头在百万级上下文下执行全注意力导致KV读取成本随上下文线性增长，使得草稿不再廉价，甚至拖慢整体解码速度；该问题在混合/线性注意力目标模型中更为严重。

Method: 对草稿注意力单独应用StreamingLLM式滑动窗口+注意力汇聚（Windowed-MTP），限制其KV工作集为常数；目标模型保持全注意力验证不变，保证输出分布无损。

Result: 在三种架构（含MoE和Mamba2混合模型）1M上下文下，解码步成本降低28%-44%；可回收约7.7-11%的总KV缓存；端到端解码延迟同步改善，且保持目标模型输出分布不变。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP是一种训练无关、即插即用且无损的优化策略，有效解决了长上下文下MTP草稿头成本失控问题，显著提升了投机解码在实际长上下文场景中的效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [108] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零流差异（ZFD）的双样本检验方法ZF2ST，通过学习局部错位方向模式来检测分布差异，能在保持良好第一类错误控制的同时实现强检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双样本检验难以有效检测结构化分布变化，需要一种既能利用神经网络的灵活性又能保持统计有效性的方法。

Method: 基于零流准则构建统计差异度量ZFD，将证人学习与假设评估分离，开发了基于回归和功效最大化的两种证人学习方法。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上，ZF2ST对结构化分布变化具有强检验功效，且第一类错误控制良好。

Conclusion: ZF2ST是一种有效且校准良好的双样本检验方法，尤其适用于检测结构化分布差异。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [109] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架，将文本大模型的推理能力迁移至音频-语言模型，通过三类对称语料（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理）训练，显著提升音频推理与思维链质量，同时保留模型原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-语言模型在深度逻辑推理上落后于文本大模型，根本原因在于缺乏高质量音频推理数据。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架：学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；构建三类对称语料（文本推理转语音、复杂声学场景中的音频事件推理、涉及副语言线索的口语对话推理）。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio、MMAR四个基准上，X$^3$-OPD显著改善了音频推理和思维链质量，同时在域偏移下较好地保留了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态蒸馏可以从文本教师迁移推理能力到音频学生，且该方法能扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话语境等无法通过文本恢复的推理内容。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [110] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EnsembleEGNN，一种分子构象集成基础模型，通过对每个构象使用共享的等变图神经网络（EGNN）编码，再用集合注意力块池化，并在循环肽数据集CREMP上预训练。实验表明，单独训练效果极差（R²=0.005），但预训练后显著优于序列BERT基线（R²=0.477 vs 0.439），与BERT联合训练效果最佳（R²=0.538）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子性质预测通常使用单一代表性构象，但许多分子在溶液中以构象集合体形式存在，忽视构象多样性导致预测不准确。作者旨在利用构象集成信息提升性质预测性能。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN，首先用共享EGNN层编码每个构象，然后用集合注意力块（Set Attention Block）池化所有构象的表示。在CREMP循环肽构象数据集上采用多任务自监督预训练，包括掩码token恢复、带噪坐标重建和成对距离重建。进一步与BERT序列编码器联合端到端训练。

Result: 从零训练EnsembleEGNN表现极差（R²=0.005）；预训练后达R²=0.477，Pearson r=0.699，优于BERT基线（R²=0.439, r=0.667）；与BERT联合训练进一步提升至R²=0.538, r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集成编码为单一热力学信息嵌入能够改善环肽性质预测，混合结构-序列模型效果最优。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>

### [113] [An obstruction and residual-completion theory for Newman--Janis deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20565)
*Reggie C. Pantig,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个用于分析Newman-Janis型变形（包括原始算法和非复化变体）的阻碍与残差完备化理论框架，将NJ算法视为离壳映射，定义NJ阻碍张量编码场方程失败，分解为动力学、几何/坐标可容许性和源守恒三个通道，并通过Schwarzschild示例展示了该框架如何用可计算的标准替代对正确复化规则的搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Newman-Janis算法虽被广泛用于从静态种子构造旋转解，但其成功与否依赖于复化规则的选取，缺乏系统性的失败诊断和修正方法。本文旨在建立一个形式化的框架，量化NJ型变形偏离精确解的程度，并提供残差完备化的可计算判据。

Method: 将NJ算法视为离壳映射，定义NJ阻碍张量作为将试探配置代入目标场方程后的残差；将阻碍分解为动力学、几何/坐标可容许性和源守恒三个通道；在旋转参数的首阶非零阶转化为线性可解性问题，要求阻碍位于规范固定完备化算子的像空间中。

Result: 在真空GR中以Schwarzschild种子为例，采用非Kerr生成型的ONJA复化获得非Kerr旋转试探度规，计算得到n_*=2和ℓ_*=0，表明存在四极分量；通过最小偶宇称修正恢复Kerr复化可消除首阶残差。

Conclusion: 该框架用可计算的成功、完备化或阻碍判据替代了对正确复化规则的搜索，为系统评估和修正NJ型变形提供了形式化工具。

Abstract: We formulate an obstruction and residual-completion theory for Newman-Janis-type deformations of black hole seeds, where Newman-Janis-type includes both the original complex-coordinate Newman-Janis algorithm and modified prescriptions such as non-complexification variants. The Newman-Janis algorithm is treated as an off-shell map from a static seed to a stationary-axisymmetric trial geometry, rather than as a solution-generating theorem. Its failure is encoded in a Newman-Janis obstruction tensor, defined as the residual obtained after substituting the trial configuration into the intended field equations. We decompose this residual into dynamical, geometrical/coordinate-admissibility, and source-preservation channels, separating field-equation failure from circularity, Boyer-Lindquist integrability, stress-tensor type, equation-of-state preservation, and matter-model realizability. When the obstruction is nonzero, residual completion asks whether a minimal correction of the metric and matter fields can cancel it. At leading nonzero order in the rotation parameter, this becomes a linear solvability problem: the obstruction must lie in the image of a gauge-fixed completion operator subject to boundary and source-sector constraints, while the cokernel condition gives a no-go criterion in the chosen ansatz class. We illustrate the framework with a Schwarzschild example in vacuum GR. Using an ONJA-type complexification different from the Kerr-generating one, we obtain a non-Kerr rotating trial metric and compute its obstruction. The example gives $n_\star=2$ and $\ell_\star=0$, with an additional quadrupolar component at the same order, and shows how the leading residual is removed by the minimal even-parity correction restoring the Kerr complexification. The framework replaces the search for the correct complexification rule with computable criteria for success, completion, or obstruction.

</details>


### [114] [Eikonal Quality-Factor Parameterization of Model-Conditional Static Black-Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20573)
*Nikko John Leo S. Lobos,Emmanuel T. Rodulfo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用单个复准正态频率参数化静态球对称黑洞家族的几何与模型条件热力学。在eikonal极限下，标量场的比率χ=ω_Rτ=ω_R/ω_I=2Q可消除整体质量标度。若χ在某无量纲参数上单射，则可重建该参数与几何标度。该方法在RN形式族中可确定电荷参数u=q²但无法区分电荷符号；MOG静态度规在特定映射下与RN完全等价，导致标量谱完全相同但熵不同。通过Chebyshev和WKB-Padé方法量化了有限多极误差。该方法是一种模型依赖的反演，而非理论选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅通过单个复准正态频率（QNM）来重建静态球对称黑洞的几何结构和热力学性质，从而为引力波观测提供一种模型依赖的黑洞参数反演方法。

Method: 理论推导与数值计算相结合。在eikonal极限下推导标量场扰动χ比率与黑洞参数的关系；利用Chebyshev插值和WKB-Padé近似量化有限多极误差；通过RN和MOG度规族进行案例分析。

Result: χ比率可单射确定RN形式族中的电荷参数u=q²（但无法区分电荷符号）；MOG度规在特定参数映射下与RN完全等价，标量QNM谱完全相同但Wald熵不同；有限多极误差在l=2时最大为2.1×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种模型依赖的反演工具，能重建固定模型族内的几何参数和热力学量，但无法区分不同的引力理论（如RN vs MOG）。应用于引力波观测需考虑张量、矢量、标量扰动以及超出eikonal近似的修正。

Abstract: We study how one complex quasinormal frequency can parameterize the geometry and model-conditional thermodynamics of a prescribed family of static, spherically symmetric black holes. For a minimally coupled massless test scalar in the eikonal limit, the ratio $χ=ω_Rτ=ω_R/ω_I=2Q$ removes the overall mass scale. If $χ$ is injective in one dimensionless parameter, that parameter and the geometric scale can be reconstructed within the chosen family. The normalized metric then fixes the horizon and Hawking temperature, whereas Wald entropy requires the gravitational action. For the RN-form family, $χ$ determines $u=q^2$ but not the sign of charge. The constant-coupling static MOG metric is exactly RN under $\mathcal M=(1+α)m_{\rm MOG}$ and $u=α/(1+α)$; consequently, the complete minimally coupled scalar spectrum is identical on mapped backgrounds for every multipole and overtone. The two assignments have equal temperature but different action-dependent entropy. Chebyshev and WKB--Padé calculations quantify the finite-multipole error, with a maximum eikonal ratio error of $2.1\times10^{-3}$ on the tested $l=2$ RN grid. The construction is therefore a model-dependent inverse, not a theory selector. Applying it to gravitational-wave observations requires the appropriate tensor, vector, and scalar perturbation sectors beyond the test-field eikonal approximation.

</details>


### [115] [Unstable de Sitter inflationary solution in sixth-order gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20575)
*Tuan Q. Do*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种包含R□R和R_{μν}□R^{μν}项以及二次和三次曲率项的六阶引力理论，发现其存在精确的德西特解，并证明当3γ₁+γ₂<0时该解不稳定，表明该理论更适合描述早期宇宙暴胀；而二阶极限总能给出稳定的德西特解，适用于晚期宇宙加速膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究包含高阶曲率项（尤其是R□R和R_{μν}□R^{μν}项）的六阶引力理论是否存在精确且稳定的德西特解，以判断该理论更适合描述早期宇宙暴胀还是晚期宇宙加速膨胀。

Method: 在FLRW背景时空下，基于欧拉-拉格朗日方程的有效方法推导六阶微分场方程；通过解析求解得到精确德西特解（等价于动力系统不动点）；利用不等式条件分析稳定性，并通过数值计算验证。

Result: 得到了精确的德西特解，γ₁和γ₂不影响解的值但影响稳定性；3γ₁+γ₂<0时德西特解不稳定（数值验证为排斥子）；二阶极限总能给出稳定德西特解。

Conclusion: 六阶引力理论更适用于早期宇宙暴胀阶段（不稳定的德西特解），其二阶极限适用于晚期宇宙加速膨胀（稳定的德西特解）。

Abstract: A sixth-order gravity, which involves not only two leading terms, $γ_1 R\Box R$ and $γ_2 R_{μν} \Box R^{μν}$, but also quadratic curvature terms along with cubic curvature ones, will be investigated in this paper to see if it admits an exact stable de Sitter solution. First, we will derive sixth-order differential field equations of this gravity under the homogeneous and isotropic Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker background spacetime, using the effective method based on the Euler-Lagrange equations. Then, we will analytically solve these field equations to figure out an exact de Sitter solution, which turns out to be equivalent to a fixed point of the corresponding dynamical system of the studied gravity. Interestingly, two coefficients, $γ_1$ and $γ_2$, do not contribute to the value of the obtained de Sitter solution. However, they affect on the stability of the de Sitter solution. In particular, if these two coefficients obey the following inequality, $3γ_1+γ_2 <0$, then the de Sitter solution will always be unstable. Furthermore, numerical calculations will be performed to verify that the de Sitter fixed point is indeed a repeller of the dynamical system once this inequality is satisfied. All these results indicate that the sixth-order gravity is more suitable for an inflationary phase of early universe. To be complete, two special limits of the studied gravity model, in which field equations are reduced to second-order and fourth-order, respectively, will be investigated. As expected, only the second-order limit can always give raise a stable de Sitter solution, compatible with an accelerated expansion of late-time universe.

</details>


### [116] [Black hole-neutron star binaries with high spins and large mass asymmetries: III. Properties of the ejected material and its electromagnetic signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20609)
*Konrad Topolski,Samuel D. Tootle,Paramvir Singh,Mattia Bulla,Luciano Rezzolla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟，系统研究了黑洞-中子星双星系统的动力学抛射物几何与热力学性质，并结合核反应网络和辐射转移计算了r过程元素丰度及千新星光变曲线，结果与AT2017gfo和S190814bv观测数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞-中子星双星是重要的多信使天文源，但此前对高质量比、高自旋BHNS系统的动力学抛射物性质及相应电磁对应体的研究尚不充分，需要系统性的参数空间探索。

Method: 采用广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟对BHNS系统进行数值模拟，覆盖大质量比和高自旋参数空间；使用SkyNet核反应网络计算r过程元素丰度；利用POSSIS辐射转移代码计算千新星光变曲线，并与观测数据对比。

Result: 1. 揭示了高质量比、高自旋BHNS系统中潮汐瓦解和动力学质量抛射的能量角动量转移特性；2. 获得了抛射物的几何、热力学和运动学性质对双星参数的依赖关系；3. 计算了r过程元素丰度及其对前身星性质的依赖；4. 得到的千新星光变曲线与AT2017gfo观测数据及S190814bv上限保持一致性。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了对高质量比、高自旋BHNS系统动力学抛射物的理解，验证了理论模型与现有多信使观测的兼容性，为未来BHNS双星的多信使探测提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Compact binary systems consisting of a black hole (BH) and a neutron star (NS) are expected to provide detectable signals both in the gravitational wave and in the electromagnetic channels, thus representing excellent sources for multi-messenger astronomy. In this third paper in a series, we report on the geometric and thermodynamic properties of the purely dynamical ejecta component modeled in our general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamical simulations of BHNS systems over a comprehensive binary parameter space consisting of large mass ratio and high BH spin. The tidal disruption and dynamical mass ejection is studied by monitoring the transfer of energy and angular momentum to matter for the particular case of a highly unequal-mass binary with large BH spin, extending known results for low-mass-ratio, irrotational BHNS binaries. By varying the binary parameters, we investigate their influence on the geometric, thermodynamic, and kinematic properties of the ejected matter. Furthermore, we employ the nuclear-reaction network SkyNet to obtain the elemental abundances resulting from the r-process active in the ejecta and study how they depend on the properties of the progenitor objects. Finally, using the radiative-transfer code POSSIS, we compute the kilonova light-curves from the results of our simulations, discuss their bolometric and optical/near-infrared evolution, and compare them with the AT2017gfo data and upper-limits for S190814bv, finding overall consistency.

</details>


### [117] [Cosmological Inflation in f(R,T) Gravity with Chern-Simons Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20654)
*Maryam Shiravand,Saeed Fakhry,Mehrdad Farhoudi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R,T)线性引力框架下，引入由量子引力诱导的Chern-Simons修正及暴胀子标量场，研究宇宙暴胀。通过FLRW度规和慢滚近似推导修正Friedmann方程，采用三角函数和指数函数两种Chern-Simons耦合形式，计算标量谱指数、张量谱指数和张量标量比。模型预测与Planck 2018数据、Planck+BK15+BAO联合数据高度吻合，且指数耦合对张量标量比给出更强约束。对比分析表明，Chern-Simons修正能在f(R,T)线性引力背景下有效精化张量谱指数和张量标量比的预测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在f(R,T)线性引力框架下引入量子引力诱导的Chern-Simons修正，研究其对宇宙暴胀观测量的影响，并与现有宇宙学数据（Planck 2018、BK15、BAO）进行对比验证。

Method: 基于FLRW度规，在慢滚近似下推导f(R,T)线性引力加Chern-Simons修正的修正Friedmann方程；选择三角函数和指数函数两种Chern-Simons耦合形式配对相应暴胀势；定义并计算慢滚参数、标量谱指数、张量谱指数和张量标量比。

Result: 模型预测与Planck 2018数据吻合良好；指数型Chern-Simons耦合对张量标量比给出更强约束，与Planck、BK15和BAO联合数据一致；Chern-Simons修正可在f(R,T)线性引力框架下精化张量谱指数和张量标量比的预测值。

Conclusion: 在f(R,T)线性引力中引入Chern-Simons修正能有效改善暴胀观测量的理论预测，使其更好地符合当前宇宙学观测数据，特别是对张量标量比给出更严格的约束。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological inflation within the framework of a linear form of f (R, T ) gravity that incorporates an inflaton scalar field augmented by a Chern-Simons correction induced by aspects of quantum gravity. Utilizing the FLRW metric, we derive the modified Friedmann equations under the slow-roll approximations. We consider two specific forms of the Chern-Simons coupling function, trigonometric and exponential, each paired with the choice of an inflaton potential. Then, we define the essential slow-roll parameters and acquire their required expressions in the proposed model. Subsequently, we compute the scalar spectral index, the tensor spectral index, and the tensor-to- scalar ratio. By adequately constraining the free parameters, the proposed model provides accurate predictions for these inflationary observables that are in good agreement with the Planck 2018 data. Furthermore, the model predictions for the Chern-Simons exponential coupling function impose a stronger limit on the value of the tensor-to-scalar ratio and also provide good agreement with the joint Planck, BK15 and BAO data. Meanwhile, for comparative analysis and better comparison of the model motivations, we also examine the model without the Chern-Simons correction, without the linear form of f (R, T ) gravity, and with a non-linear form of f (R, T ) gravity. As a general conclusion, the obtained findings indicate that the inclusion of the Chern-Simons correction approximately refines the values of the tensor spectral index and tensor-to-scalar ratio in the context of the linear form of f (R, T ) gravity.

</details>


### [118] [A product template bank to search for compact binary coalescences microlensed by isolated point mass lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20661)
*Sudhir Gholap,Sanjeev V. Dhurandhar,Shasvath J. Kapadia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对微透镜引力波事件的模板库构建方法。研究发现，当引力波波长与透镜史瓦西半径相当时，标准搜索模板会损失高达30%的信噪比。作者利用几何光学近似（将微透镜信号建模为重叠信号），通过放大因子的相位计算度规元素，在原有参数空间基础上增加了两个透镜参数（时间延迟和相对放大率）。通过识别波光学效应导致拟合因子低于97%的参数区域，构建了约4000个模板的透镜模板库，该库可视为非透镜模板库与透镜参数模板库的笛卡尔积，将失配度降低了约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模板搜索未考虑波光学效应，导致微透镜引力波信号的信噪比损失高达30%，需要构建专门的模板库来有效探测这类信号。

Method: 使用几何光学近似将微透镜信号建模为重叠信号，利用放大因子相位计算度规元素；在原有致密双星并合参数基础上增加时间延迟(t_d)和相对放大率(μ_r)两个透镜参数；识别波光学效应导致拟合因子低于97%的参数区域，构建约4000个模板的透镜模板库（非透镜模板库与透镜参数模板库的笛卡尔积）。

Result: 新构建的乘积模板库相比非透镜模板库，失配度降低了约一个数量级（约10倍），且满足构建时设定的0.97最小匹配准则。

Conclusion: 所提出的微透镜引力波模板库构建方法有效可行，能够显著降低波光学效应导致的匹配损失，且透镜模板库可独立于非透镜参数构建。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GW) from stellar mass compact binary coalescences (CBCs) that encounter a lens whose Schwarzschild radius is comparable to the GW wavelength, will be microlensed, leading to frequency-dependent modulations in the observed signal. Neglecting such wave-optics effects in standard templated searches for CBCs can result in signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) losses as large as 30%. Here, we present a prescription for constructing a template bank targeted at microlensed GWs. We consider CBCs microlensed by isolated point mass lenses, whose frequency-dependent amplification factor is known to have a closed-form analytical expression. However, to construct the template bank, it suffices to use the geometric optics approximation, effectively modeling the microlensed signal as overlapping signals. Moreover, given that matched filtering is mostly sensitive to the phase of the GW signal, we use the phase of the amplification factor to compute the metric elements. The usual CBC parameter space is appended by two extra parameters: (i) the time-delay ($t_d$) between images and (ii) the relative-magnification ($μ_r$). By identifying the region of the lensing parameter space where the fitting factor (FF) falls below 97% due to wave-optics effects, we construct a template bank consisting of ~4000 templates covering the selected lensing region, independent of the values of the unlensed CBCs' parameters. We show that the bank targeting microlensed CBCs is, to a very good approximation, a Cartesian product of the unlensed CBC bank and the bank spanning the lensing parameters. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by evaluating fitting factors using both the unlensed CBC bank and the product template bank, finding a reduction of up to a factor of ~10 in the mismatch, as well as consistency with the prescribed minimal match criterion of 0.97 used in the construction of the banks.

</details>


### [119] [A First-Order Gauge Approach to de Sitter General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20687)
*P. Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种德西特相对论的一阶规范表述，将伪半径l(x)提升为时空场，局域破缺SO(4,1)到SO(3,1)。l(x)作为几何补偿场，dl(x)为序参量，修改了强等效原理，使局部真空跟随物质分布而非固定不变。通过Inonu-Wigner收缩，抽象生成元变为显式时空变换。广义Noether定理给出统一能量-动量流，无物质时恒为零，导致局域真空能量渐近消逝，背景衰变为闵氏时空。独立变分得到耦合的德西特场方程，并代数证明其与坐标流形上的张量分裂等价。规范Bianchi恒等式表明Lambda(x)是时空挠率的内禀来源。宇宙学常数问题被重新表述而非数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的德西特相对论框架中，伪半径l被固定为常数，无法描述时空对称性的局域破缺过程。本文旨在通过将l(x)提升为动力学场，建立一个规范理论框架，以统一处理引力与物质，并重新审视宇宙学常数问题。

Method: 采用一阶规范形式，将de Sitter群SO(4,1)局域破缺到Lorentz子群SO(3,1)，引入几何补偿场l(x)及其微分dl(x)作为序参量。利用Inonu-Wigner收缩连接抽象生成元与时空变换。通过广义Noether定理推导守恒流，独立变分得到场方程，并利用Bianchi恒等式分析Lambda(x)的挠率来源。

Result: 广义Noether定理给出统一能量-动量流，无物质时恒为零，导致局域真空能量渐近消逝，背景衰变为闵氏时空；独立变分得到耦合的德西特场方程，并代数证明其与坐标流形上的张量分裂等价；规范Bianchi恒等式表明Lambda(x)是时空挠率的内禀来源。

Conclusion: 宇宙学常数问题被重新表述为对称性破缺中序参量的动力学演化问题，而非数值求解。从早期宇宙密度推导Lambda(x)衰减至观测值仍是未来工作。

Abstract: We propose a first-order gauge formulation of de Sitter relativity in which the pseudo-radius l(x) is promoted to a spacetime field, locally breaking SO(4,1) to SO(3,1). Here, l(x) is a geometric compensator and dl(x) the associated order parameter. This modifies the Strong Equivalence Principle: the local vacuum follows the matter distribution instead of remaining fixed, while the propagating degrees of freedom of General Relativity are unchanged. At fixed l(x), the action is SO(3,1)-gauge invariant, but varying l(x) destroys covariance under the full translational sector of SO(4,1). Through the Inonu-Wigner contraction, abstract generators become explicit spacetime transformations, yielding nonlinear Killing vectors in both the macroscopic l tends to infinity and microscopic l tends to 0 limits. The generalized Noether theorem gives a unified energy-momentum current coupling the standard tensor to the proper conformal matter current. It vanishes identically without matter, causing the asymptotic extinction of local vacuum energy and the decay of the background to Minkowski spacetime. Independent variations yield the coupled de Sitter field equations, and we algebraically prove their equivalence to the tensorial splitting on the coordinate manifold. Gauge Bianchi identities further show that Lambda(x) is an intrinsic source of spacetime torsion, consistently identifying it with the local order parameter of the symmetry breaking. The cosmological constant problem is therefore recast, not numerically solved: deriving the decay of Lambda(x) from early-universe densities to its observed value remains future work.

</details>


### [120] [Gravitational Wave Birefringence in generalized Palatini Chern Simons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20689)
*Jose Perdiguero,Macarena Lagos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(R)引力与动力学Chern-Simons项耦合的模型中，引力波在宇称破缺下的宇宙学双折射现象。作者发现Palatini形式同时预言振幅和速度双折射，而度规形式仅预言振幅双折射；双折射效应可被f(R)相互作用增强或抑制，具体依赖于f(R)形式；相较于f(R)=R情况，三种常见f(R)模型（Hu-Sawicki、指数、双曲引力）使双折射增强1-10%（Palatini形式）或高达2倍（度规形式）；双折射效应随源红移呈多项式增长，而非线性距离标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在f(R)引力与动力学Chern-Simons项耦合的框架下，宇称破缺如何导致引力波传播中的偏振畸变（宇宙学双折射），并比较度规形式与Palatini形式在预言上的差异，同时评估不同f(R)模型对双折射效应的影响。

Method: 在f(R)引力与dCS项耦合的理论框架下，分别在度规形式和Palatini形式中推导引力波传播方程；选取三种常见的f(R)模型（Hu-Sawicki、指数、双曲引力）并利用最新宇宙学数据进行拟合，量化双折射效应；将现有引力波双折射观测约束转化为对模型参数的限定。

Result: Palatini形式同时预言振幅双折射和速度双折射，度规形式仅预言振幅双折射；双折射效应可被f(R)相互作用增强或抑制（取决于f(R)具体形式）；三种f(R)模型下双折射增强幅度在Palatini形式中为1-10%，在度规形式中可达2倍；双折射随红移呈多项式增长，而非常见的线性距离标度。

Conclusion: 该模型为引力波双折射提供了丰富的现象学特征，不同引力形式（度规vs Palatini）导致不同的双折射模式，f(R)相互作用可显著增强双折射效应，且双折射的红移依赖性与现有现象学模型不同，为未来引力波观测检验提供了可区分的理论预言。

Abstract: The cosmological propagation of gravitational waves (GWs) can exhibit amplitude and phase polarization distortions when parity symmetry is broken, a phenomenon known as cosmological birefringence. In this paper, we investigate the phenomenology of GW birefringence in a gravitational model $f(R)$ coupled to a dynamical Chern-Simons (dCS) term, analyzed in the metric and Palatini formalisms. At the background level, this model can lead to dynamical dark energy, while for GW propagation we find that the Palatini formalism predicts both amplitude and velocity birefringence, whereas the metric formalism predicts amplitude birefringence only. We also find that the birefringence effects can either be suppressed or enhanced by the $f(R)$ interactions, depending on the specific form of $f(R)$. Considering three common $f(R)$ models (Hu-Sawicki, Exponential, and Hyperbolic gravity) fit to recent cosmological data, we find that birefringence is enhanced relative to the $f(R) = R$ case, by $1-10\%$ in the Palatini formalism and up to a factor of 2 in the metric formalism. We also translate current GW birefringence constraints to bounds on the dCS coupling within our model. Finally, we show that in our model the birefringence effect grows polynomially with source redshift, in contrast to the linear-distance scaling commonly assumed in phenomenological models of GW birefringence in the current literature.

</details>


### [121] [Evolving Dark Energy from the Back-Reaction of Cosmological Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20715)
*Vincent Comeau*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证了宇宙学常数与不均匀性的反作用相结合时，会产生一个状态方程随时间演化的有效暗能量。通过梯度展开方法处理，这种时间依赖性不再简化为边界项，即使在全局平均或诱导空间曲率可忽略时也可能显著，且暗能量不会在phantom与非phantom行为之间交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不均匀性的反作用如何与宇宙学常数耦合，产生具有时间演化状态的暗能量，并解释近期数值模拟中发现的暗能量受反作用影响的观测结果。

Method: 采用两种扰动处理方法：(1) 将不均匀性视为均匀背景上的小扰动，在标量模式二次阶下暗能量时间依赖性简化为边界项；(2) 采用梯度展开对不均匀性的空间导数进行扰动处理，此时时间依赖性不再简化为边界项。

Result: 发现暗能量的时间依赖性在梯度展开下可显著存在，即使对于全局平均或可忽略的诱导空间曲率也是如此；该时间依赖在当今时期保持同一符号，使得暗能量要么始终是phantom要么始终不是phantom，不会出现行为交叉。

Conclusion: 不均匀性的反作用与宇宙学常数的结合可以产生状态方程随时间演化的有效暗能量，且分析结果与近期数值模拟一致，为解释当前宇宙加速膨胀提供了一种理论机制。

Abstract: We argue that the back-reaction of inhomogeneities, when combined with a pure cosmological constant, produces an effective dark energy whose equation of state can evolve with time. Our analytical computation agrees with recent numerical simulations, which have shown that the back-reaction of inhomogeneities can impact dark energy around the present time. Here, its equation of state is derived from an average version of the Friedmann equations, for which the average is taken over a fixed volume of matter particles (not necessarily global). When treating the inhomogeneities as small perturbations around a homogeneous background, dark energy acquires a time dependence which reduces to a boundary term, at least at quadratic order in the scalar modes of the perturbations. Thus, it becomes negligible for a global average. On the other hand, when treating instead the spatial derivatives of the inhomogeneities perturbatively in a gradient expansion, dark energy gets the same time dependence, which no longer reduces to a boundary term. This time dependence can be significant even for a global average or when the constant spatial curvature induced by the inhomogeneities is negligible. It also keeps the same sign around the present time, thus causing dark energy to remain either always phantom or never phantom, not allowing for any crossing from one type of behaviour to the other.

</details>


### [122] [Thermodynamics of boosted Schwarzschild black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20746)
*Glenn Barnich*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
