Method: 采用简洁的数学推导，建立加速史瓦西黑洞的平衡热力学形式，并与相对论热力学进行比较验证。

Result: 加速史瓦西黑洞的热力学行为与相对论热力学的预期完全一致。

Conclusion: 加速黑洞的平衡热力学可以简洁地构建，并且自洽地嵌入相对论热力学的框架中。

Abstract: The equilibrium thermodynamics of boosted Schwarzschild black holes is worked out in a concise way and shown to agree with expectations from relativistic thermodynamics.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Spin Perturbations, Thermodynamics, and Observational Signatures of Reissner-Nordstrom Black Holes in Bumblebee Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20839)
*Jayasri Choudhury,Yenshembam Priyobarta Singh,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Telem Ibungochouba Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了蜂鸟引力框架下带电Reissner-Nordström黑洞的动力学与热力学性质，分析了洛伦兹对称性破缺参数和电荷对拟正则模、灰体因子、霍金温度、熵及相变的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹对称性破缺如何影响带电黑洞的动力学稳定性、波传播特性及热力学演化，为洛伦兹违背理论提供黑洞物理方面的理论检验。

Method: 采用纽曼-彭罗斯形式论下的统一Teukolsky主方程分析自旋s=0,1/2,1,3/2,2的无质量场扰动；使用Padé改进的6阶WKB近似和渐近迭代法计算QNM频率；评估灰体因子；计算霍金温度、含对数热修正的熵及热容。

Result: LSB参数L和电荷Q显著改变了时空的阻尼振荡和波传播特性；确定了LISA、Virgo和LIGO可探测的黑洞质量范围；热力学分析揭示了由背景电荷主导临界半径的二阶相变。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹违背对带电黑洞的物理稳定性、热演化和相结构具有重要影响，为引力波探测和量子引力现象提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a comprehensive investigation into the dynamical and thermodynamic properties of the charged Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole (BH) in the bumblebee gravity framework, where spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking (LSB) occurs. To analyze the dynamical behavior, we apply the unified Teukolsky master equation within the Newman-Penrose formalism to evaluate massless field perturbations of arbitrary spins ($s=0, 1/2, 1, 3/2, 2$). By deriving the corresponding effective potentials, we compute the quasinormal modes (QNMs) frequencies using the Padé-improved 6th-order WKB approximation and the Asymptotic Iteration Method (AIM) and evaluate the greybody factors for all perturbing fields, demonstrating how the LSB parameter $L$ and the BH charge $Q$ modify the spacetime's damped oscillations and wave propagation. We further assess the observational prospects of these QNMs by determining the black-hole mass ranges accessible to current and future gravitational-wave detectors, including LISA, Virgo, and LIGO. Moreover, we investigate the modified thermodynamic structure, calculating the Hawking temperature, entropy with logarithmic thermal corrections and heat capacity. Our thermodynamic analysis reveals a second-order phase transition whose critical radius is heavily governed by the background charge. These combined findings provide valuable theoretical insights into how Lorentz violation affects the physical stability, thermal evolution, and phase structure of charged BHs.

</details>


### [124] [Static spheres in black hole spacetimes: pairing, energy conditions, and an upper bound on the innermost radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20850)
*Yong Song*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般静态球对称渐近平坦黑洞时空中静态球的存在性、与能量条件的关系及径向边界。通过分析由质量和径向压力函数构造的径向函数的全局行为，证明静态球必然要求负径向压力（张力），且非退化静态球总是成对出现：内不稳定球和外稳定球。假设弱能量条件（WEC）始终成立，内静态球表现为强能量条件（SEC）不等式违反（ρ+p+2p_T<0），外球处SEC恢复（ρ+p+2p_T>0），退化边缘情形满足ρ+p+2p_T=0。进一步，考虑最内层静态球并假设WEC成立而SEC从事件视界到该球均匀违反，推导了其半径的严格上界。这些结果建立了能量条件与静态球存在性之间的直接解析联系，并为具有此类轨道的黑洞的物质环境提供了定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞时空中静态球（静态轨道/静止球）的存在性、与能量条件的关系及径向边界，建立能量条件与静态球存在性之间的直接解析联系，为检验广义相对论和修正引力理论中的黑洞解以及解释相关天文观测提供理论工具。

Method: 通过分析由质量和径向压力函数构造的径向函数的全局行为，结合弱能量条件（WEC）和强能量条件（SEC）的假设，利用数学分析证明静态球的存在性与性质，并推导半径上界。

Result: (1) 静态球必然需要负径向压力（张力）；(2) 非退化静态球总是成对出现：内不稳定球和外稳定球；(3) 假设WEC成立，内球处SEC违反（ρ+p+2p_T<0），外球处SEC恢复（ρ+p+2p_T>0），退化情形满足等式；(4) 推导了最内层静态球半径的严格上界，解析地联系了事件视界半径、视界处能量密度和SEC违反强度参数。

Conclusion: 建立了能量条件（WEC、SEC）与静态球存在性及性质之间的直接解析联系，提供了对黑洞周围物质环境的定量约束，可应用于检验广义相对论及修正引力理论中的黑洞解，并有助于解释相关天文观测。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the existence, relation to the energy conditions, and radial bounds of static spheres in general static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat black hole spacetimes. By analyzing the global behavior of a radial function constructed from the mass and radial pressure functions, we prove that a static sphere necessarily requires a negative radial pressure (tension). Furthermore, we show that non-degenerate static spheres must always appear in pairs: an inner unstable sphere and an outer stable one. Assuming the Weak Energy Condition (WEC) always holds, the inner static sphere is characterized by a violation of the strong energy condition (SEC) inequality $ρ+p+2p_T<0$, where $ρ$, $p$, and $p_T$ denote the energy density, radial pressure, and tangential pressure, respectively; the SEC inequality is restored ($ρ+p+2p_T> 0$) at the outer sphere; the degenerate marginal case satisfies $ρ+p+2p_T=0$. In addition, focusing on the innermost static sphere and assuming that the WEC holds while the SEC is uniformly violated between the event horizon and this sphere, we derive a rigorous upper bound on its radius,
  \[
  r^-_{\mathrm{sp}}\le \left[r_H^3+\frac{3r_H\bigl(1-8πr^2_Hρ(r_H)\bigr)}{8πκ}\right]^{1/3},
  \]
  where $r_H$ is the horizon radius, $ρ(r_H)$ the energy density at the horizon, and $κ$ characterizes the strength of the SEC violation. These results establish a direct, analytic link between the energy conditions and the existence of static spheres, and provide a quantitative constraint on the matter environment of black holes possessing such orbits. The findings have potential applications in testing black hole solutions in general relativity and modified theories of gravity, as well as in interpreting related astronomical observations.

</details>


### [125] [Graviton-induced which-path decoherence in matter-wave interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20867)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了线性量子引力中引力子导致的路径退相干，发现在真空中退相干指数正比于引力子辐射数，在压缩真空（如暴胀）中可被增强或抑制，但总之在现有物质波干涉仪中可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索物质波干涉中因引力子（线性量子引力）被追踪而导致的路径退相干效应，检验其在现有及未来实验中的可观测性。

Method: 将引力子视为被物质源线性驱动的量子场，精确求解分支依赖演化；退相干由初始引力子态的生成函数在分支诱导位移差处取值给出；针对真空态和一般压缩真空态分别推导了退相干指数的解析表达式。

Result: 真空退相干指数Γ_vac=(8/15)Gm²d⁴/(ℏc⁵τ⁴)，数值范围10⁻⁸⁹~10⁻⁶¹；暴胀压缩真空下Γ_inf=(π/20)Ω_inf(md²H₀/ℏ)²，最大约10⁻²⁷。

Conclusion: 引力子辐射导致的路径退相干在当前及可预见的物质波干涉实验中均可忽略不计。

Abstract: We derive which-path decoherence for matter-wave interferometry that arises from tracing out the gravitons of linearized quantum gravity. Because the gravitons are driven linearly by the matter source, the branch-dependent evolution can be solved exactly, and the reduced coherence is given by the characteristic function of the initial graviton state, evaluated at the difference of the branch-induced field displacements. In vacuum, the decoherence exponent equals half the mean number of gravitons radiated by the difference source. For a smooth Gaussian trajectory, it reduces to $Γ_{\rm vac}=(8/15)\,Gm^2d^4/(\hbar c^5τ^4)$, ranging from $10^{-89}$ to $10^{-61}$ across representative matter-wave platforms. For a general squeezed graviton vacuum, we obtain an exact expression in which squeezing either suppresses or enhances the vacuum response, depending on the squeezing phase. For the strongly squeezed state produced by inflation, this expression reduces to $Γ_{\rm inf}=(π/20)\,Ω_{\rm inf}(md^2H_0/\hbar)^2$ and reaches at most $\sim10^{-27}$ for optimistic parameters. Hence, the which-path decoherence induced by radiative gravitons is therefore negligible in current matter-wave interferometers.

</details>


### [126] [Using horizon shadows to distinguish a black hole and a white hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20945)
*Chengyu Bi,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用广义相对论射线追踪技术，计算了克尔黑洞及其经过量子反弹后演化为白洞阶段的吸积盘成像。结果显示黑洞呈现新月形发射环和中心阴影，而白洞由于参考系拖拽和透镜效应形成偏心不对称嵌套强度环结构。白洞偏振图像出现环间偏振不连续性，为未来VLBI区分黑洞与白洞提供了多波段和偏振干涉基线理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在圈量子引力等理论框架下，黑洞可能通过量子反弹演化为白洞。为了在观测上区分黑洞与白洞，需要计算并比较两种天体在成像上的差异特征，为未来VLBI观测提供理论基础。

Method: 采用广义相对论射线追踪技术，计算克尔黑洞和反弹后克尔白洞阶段的吸积盘射线追踪成像；引入偏振射线追踪计算，分析偏振矢量在引力场和时空自旋拖拽下的演化。

Result: 黑洞图像呈现新月形发射环和中心阴影；白洞图像形成偏心不对称嵌套强度环结构；白洞偏振图像呈现环间偏振不连续性和同心干涉条纹的空间旋转模式。

Conclusion: 嵌套强度环结构和环间偏振不连续性能为多波段和偏振干涉测量提供基线，克服形态观测简并，为未来VLBI区分黑洞与白洞提供理论指导。

Abstract: Within theoretical frameworks such as loop quantum gravity, black holes may evolve into white holes through a quantum bounce. This paper uses general relativistic ray-tracing techniques to calculate the ray-traced imaging of accretion disks from the previous cosmic stage during the Kerr black hole and post-bounce Kerr white hole phases. Calculations show that the black hole image presents a crescent emission ring and a central shadow. In contrast, after radiation from the previous universe penetrates the rotating white hole, eccentric and asymmetric nested intensity ring structures form in the synthetic image due to frame-dragging and lensing effects. We analyze the influence of spin parameters, observation inclinations, and accretion disk geometric configurations on the distribution of this nested ring structure using synthetic images and intensity profiles. Building upon this, we introduce polarized ray-tracing calculations for radiation across evolutionary stages. This process results in the polarization image features after the polarization vector is subjected to the gravitational field and spacetime spin dragging during the photon propagation through the white hole horizon and internal spacetime. The spatial rotation patterns and concentric interference fringes in the white hole polarization images exhibit a distinct inter-ring polarization discontinuity. This phenomenon differs from the polarization behavior of black holes. The intensity ring structures and polarization inter-ring discontinuity features provide multi-band and polarimetric interferometry baselines to overcome morphological observational degeneracies. This provides theoretical guidance for future very-long-baseline interferometry (VLBI) to distinguish black holes and white holes.

</details>


### [127] [New generalization of the Barboza-Alcaniz parametrization of Dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21064)
*Shahab Shahidi*

Method: 提出一个三参数的Barboza-Alcaniz参数化推广模型，通过宇宙学数据（具体数据未在摘要中详述）进行模型比较。

Result: 宇宙学数据支持新参数化模型优于Barboza-Alcaniz模型和ΛCDM模型；新模型的定性行为与原模型相似，但加速率略小。

Conclusion: 新提出的三参数推广模型能解决原Barboza-Alcaniz模型在未来时间的行为缺陷，并得到宇宙学数据的偏好。

Abstract: A generalization of the Barboza-Alcaniz parametrization of dark energy is proposed. This is a three-parameter model which can resolve the shortcomings of the Barboza-Alcaniz behavior at future times. We show that cosmological data favor the new parametrization over both the Barboza-Alcaniz model and $Λ$CDM. We also consider the cosmological implications of the model and show that the qualitative behavior mimics to the original Barboza-Alcaniz model, with a slightly smaller acceleration rate.

</details>


### [128] [The leading-soft cubic graviton self-interaction on the black-hole horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21066)
*Ayanendu Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在GGV近地平线框架的偶Regge-Wheeler规范下，将爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量展开到立方阶并导出引力子自耦合。核心结果是"消失定理"：在领头软阶，纯无迹纵向极化自耦合恒为零。通过IBM Qiskit/Aer模拟验证了纵向道在微扰意义下的刚性，并测量了残余修饰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Schwarzschild视界附近量子引力效应，特别是立方阶引力子自相互作用的性质，以及纵向极化模式在近地平线框架下的行为。

Method: 采用GGV框架，在偶Regge-Wheeler规范下将Einstein-Hilbert作用量展开至κ的立方阶；通过封闭形式约化证明消失定理；利用IBM Qiskit/Aer进行实时哈密顿量模拟，并与精确对角化交叉验证。

Result: (1) 证明领头软阶纯无迹纵向极化自耦合恒为零；(2) 发现守恒荷仅被立方顶点破坏，开辟φφ→hh→4φ通道；(3) 证明无共振的boost谱（能隙→1/2）；(4) 模拟确认纵向道微扰刚性，测得残余有效维度d_eff=1.06；(5) 首次给出扇区分辨的能级统计，显示中间行为。

Conclusion: 消失定理是框架特定的结论，非规范不变的一般相对论定理；纵向通道在微扰意义下刚性；所有数值结果均附带系统误差，硬件执行需在量化噪声预算下进行。

Abstract: We expand the Einstein-Hilbert action to cubic order about the Schwarzschild horizon, in the even Regge-Wheeler gauge of the Gaddam-Groenenboom-'t~Hooft (GGV) near-horizon framework, and derive the cubic graviton self-interaction. Our central result is a `vanishing theorem': at leading soft order the self-coupling of the purely traceless longitudinal polarizations is identically zero, because with the trace/transverse-scalar sector switched off the fluctuation reduces to a two-dimensional block whose $\sqrt{-g}\,R$ is a total (Euler) derivative at every order in $κ$. We prove this by an explicit closed-form reduction and exhibit the cancellation term by term. It is a `framework-specific' statement, even RW gauge, GGV sector, leading soft order, not a gauge-invariant theorem of general relativity. The theorem is exact, but the quantized vertex inherits a $\sim\!20\%$ soft/scheme systematic at the only simulable multiplet ($\ell=2$), which we state explicitly. We then simulate the real-time dynamics of the resulting Hamiltonian on IBM Qiskit/Aer with exact cross-checks. Two structural facts, a conserved charge that only the cubic vertex violates, opening $φφ\to hh\to4φ$, and a provably resonance-free boost spectrum (gap $\to1/2$), already predict that the longitudinal channel is perturbatively rigid; the simulation confirms this quantitatively and measures the residual dressing ($d_{\rm eff}=1.06$; multiplicity far from thermal, Poisson, and Haar references) rather than discovering it. A symmetry-exact total-occupation truncation yields the first sector-resolved level statistics, indicative of intermediate behaviour on Hilbert spaces too small to be decisive. All circuit results agree with exact diagonalization, every headline number carries a stated systematic, and hardware execution is deferred behind a quantified noise budget.

</details>


### [129] [Spin-mediated modulation of chaos bound violation in Lorentz-violating black hole spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21165)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang,Bingbing Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Bumblebee引力框架下洛伦兹破缺黑洞时空中粒子自旋如何调节混沌界违反，发现自旋通过修改临界角动量阈值提供精细调节机制，洛伦兹破缺参数抑制违反，而宇宙常数具有双重影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粒子自旋在洛伦兹破缺黑洞时空中对混沌界违反的调制作用，揭示自旋-曲率耦合、洛伦兹对称性破缺和宇宙常数在塑造混沌动力学中的角色。

Method: 分析静态球对称(A)dS黑洞中自旋带电粒子的动力学，研究自旋-曲率耦合、洛伦兹破缺参数和宇宙常数对混沌界违反的影响。

Result: 自旋通过修改临界角动量阈值提供精细调节机制，使高自旋粒子在更低角动量下进入违反区域；洛伦兹破缺参数通过收窄允许参数区域并降低临界电荷来抑制违反；宇宙常数具有双重影响：更负的宇宙常数减小违反参数区域但同时增强违反幅度。

Conclusion: 自旋和洛伦兹对称性破缺效应共同修改了轨道不稳定性与视界性质之间的关系，为理解黑洞混沌动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate how the particle spin modulates chaos bound violations in Lorentz-violating black hole spacetimes within the framework of Bumblebee gravity. By analyzing spinning charged particles in static spherically symmetric (A)dS black holes, we reveal the roles of spin--curvature coupling, Lorentz symmetry breaking, and the cosmological constant in shaping chaotic dynamics. The results show that the spin provides a fine-tuning mechanism for the chaos bound violation by modifying the critical angular momentum threshold, allowing particles with larger spin to access the violation regime at lower angular momenta. The Lorentz-violating parameter suppresses the violations by narrowing the allowed parameter region and reducing the critical charge. Meanwhile, the cosmological constant exhibits a dual influence: a more negative cosmological constant decreases the parameter region of violation while enhancing the violation magnitude for configurations that remain beyond the bound. These findings demonstrate that the spin and Lorentz symmetry breaking effects jointly modify the relationship between orbital instability and horizon properties.

</details>


### [130] [Zipoy--Voorhees spacetime with Maxwell and dilaton fields: exact solution and equatorial geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21283)
*Haryanto M. Siahaan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过将Zipoy-Voorhees真空种子解进行提升-加速-约化操作，导出了四维Kaluza-Klein理论的一个新精确解——Zipoy-Voorhees-dilaton时空。该静态轴对称渐近平直构型由种子长度尺度M、变形参数k和电荷控制参数α参数化。当k=1时对应规则黑洞；k≠1时在r=2M处继承裸奇点。文章提取了ADM质量、电荷和膨胀子荷，发现膨胀子荷服从与四极矩变形无关的代数约束。详细研究了中性测试粒子和光子的赤道测地线，发现外光子环阈值位于k=1/2（与α无关），推广了真空情况。给出了ISCO在(k,α)平面的分布图，并表明与Schwarzschild的比较取决于使用种子质量还是ADM质量单位。还计算了引力红移（在r=2M面发散使近奇点轨道不可见）和临界碰撞参数（决定阴影大小，在ADM质量单位下随电荷增加而小于Schwarzschild值）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获得Kaluza-Klein理论中新的精确解，研究Zipoy-Voorhees时空在膨胀子场存在下的性质，特别是其测地线结构、黑洞解释以及可观测效应（如阴影、红移等）。

Method: 采用"提升-加速-约化"（uplift-boost-reduction）程序，将Zipoy-Voorhees真空种子解变换为Kaluza-Klein理论中的解，通过引入膨胀子场和电场获得膨胀子-dilaton时空解。

Result: 得到了Zipoy-Voorhees-dilaton精确解，确定了其ADM质量、电荷和膨胀子荷的表达式；发现外光子环阈值在k=1/2处与α无关；绘制了ISCO在参数空间(k,α)的分布；发现引力红移在r=2M发散；阴影大小在ADM质量单位下随电荷增加而减小。

Conclusion: 该解为Kaluza-Klein理论提供了新的精确解系列，拓展了对变形膨胀子时空测地线结构和可观测特征的理解，外光子环阈值具有参数无关性等性质具有理论和观测意义。

Abstract: We derive a new exact solution of four-dimensional Kaluza-Klein theory by applying the uplift-boost-reduction procedure to the Zipoy-Voorhees vacuum seed. The resulting Zipoy-Voorhees-dilaton spacetime is a static, axisymmetric, asymptotically flat configuration parametrised by a seed length scale $M$, the Zipoy-Voorhees deformation parameter $k$, and a boost parameter $α$ that controls the electric charge. For $k=1$ it admits a regular black-hole interpretation; for $k \neq 1$ it inherits the naked curvature singularity of the seed at $r=2M$. We extract the ADM mass, electric charge, and dilaton charge, finding that the dilaton charge obeys an algebraic constraint independent of the quadrupole deformation. The equatorial geodesics of neutral test particles and photons are studied in detail. A compact algebraic identity locates the exterior-photon-ring threshold at $k=1/2$ for all $α$, generalising the known threshold of the vacuum Zipoy-Voorhees case. The innermost stable circular orbit is mapped over the $(k,α)$ plane, and the comparison with Schwarzschild is shown to depend qualitatively on whether seed-mass or ADM-mass units are used. We further compute the gravitational redshift, which diverges on the $r=2M$ surface and renders the near-singularity orbits effectively invisible, and the critical impact parameter setting the characteristic shadow size, which in ADM-mass units shrinks below the Schwarzschild value as the charge grows.

</details>


### [131] [Cosmological initial data without periodic boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21308)
*Károly Csukás*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用爱因斯坦约束条件的抛物-双曲形式生成宇宙学初始数据，以平坦FLRW为背景，包含局部各向异性完美流体扰动。该方法从原点向外演化约束方程，无需边界条件，避免了标准椭圓求解器的多解问题，并可用于研究周期性边界等常见边界条件引入的系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宇宙学初始数据生成方法通常依赖边界条件（如周期性边界），这可能引入系统偏差；此外，标准椭圆求解器在存在多解时可能失效。本文旨在提出一种无需边界条件且能保证解唯一性的替代方法。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦约束条件的抛物-双曲（parabolic-hyperbolic）形式，从原点处的规则数据向外演化约束方程，生成宇宙学初始数据。自由指定的几何数据对应平坦FLRW背景，物质部分包含局部各向异性完美流体扰动。

Result: 数值实现了该方法，成功生成了包含局部各向异性完美流体扰动的宇宙学初始数据。

Conclusion: 该方法无需边界条件，具有解唯一性优势，适合作为研究常见边界条件（如周期性边界）引入的系统偏差的起点。

Abstract: We apply the parabolic-hyperbolic formulation of the Einstein constraint equations to generate cosmological initial data. The freely specifiable geometric data correspond to flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background, while the matter sector contains localized anisotropic perturbations of a perfect fluid. Unlike the standard approach, our method evolves the constraints outward from regular data at the origin and therefore requires no boundary conditions. This makes our method well suited as a starting point in investigating systematic biases introduced by commonly adopted boundary conditions, such as periodic boundaries. A second advantage concerns uniqueness: standard elliptic solvers may fail when multiple solutions exist, whereas solving the constraints as a well-posed evolutionary system always yields a unique solution. To demonstrate our method, we implement it numerically and generate cosmological initial data with localized anisotropic perfect fluid perturbations.

</details>


### [132] [Octupole moments and the non-universality of free-fall in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21314)
*Abraham I. Harte,Paul Ramond*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究广义相对论中八极矩对自由落体非普适性的贡献，发现只有无迹部分影响运动，且在Petrov D型时空中能产生四极矩无法实现的力矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中不同多极矩如何导致不同物体即使初始条件相同也会表现出不同的自由落体加速度，特别是八极矩的作用尚未被充分研究。

Method: 首先证明在任意真空时空中只有八极矩的无迹分量影响物体运动；然后给出无迹八极矩的两种分解（类时标架和零标架）；最后将这些分解应用于牛顿时空和Petrov D型真空时空中的运动分析。

Result: 16个八极矩分量退耦；在牛顿时空中质量矩有经典效应而动量矩决定隐藏动量；在Petrov D型时空中八极矩能产生四极矩无法实现的力矩。

Conclusion: 八极矩具有与四极矩定性不同的效应，进一步加深了广义相对论中自由落体非普适性的理解。

Abstract: Extended bodies in general relativity do not necessarily fall along geodesics, but can be accelerated. These accelerations depend on an object's angular momentum, as well as on its quadrupole, octupole, and higher-order moments. However, these multipole moments can evolve differently from one body to another. Different bodies can thus fall differently, even with identical initial data. This paper examines how octupole moments contribute to the non-universality of free-fall in general relativity. We begin by showing that in arbitrary vacuum spacetimes, only the trace-free component of an octupole moment can affect an object's motion. It follows that at least 16 out of 40 octupole components decouple from the laws of motion. Then, we obtain two decompositions for trace-free octupole moments, one in terms of a timelike frame vector and the other in terms of a null tetrad. These decompositions are applied to motion both in generic Newtonian spacetimes and in fully-relativistic vacuum spacetimes that are of Petrov type D. In Newtonian spacetimes, the mass moments are shown to have their ordinary Newtonian effects, while the momentum moments determine a body's hidden momentum---the misalignment between its momentum and its velocity. In Petrov type D spacetimes (such as Kerr), we show that some torques that are impossible with quadrupole moments are possible with octupole moments. Octupole moments can thus have qualitatively-different effects from quadrupole moments.

</details>


### [133] [A story about a tipsy kangaroo: Reversible jump MCMC for model selection in the analysis of gravitational-wave signals from the coalescence of compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21484)
*Anna Puecher,Tim Dietrich,Hauke Koehn,Jonathan Gair,Gregory Ashton,Luca Negri,Anuradha Samajdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了t-roo，一种基于可逆跳转马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（RJMCMC）的跨维度引力波信号分析工具，能够在单次分析中同时提供模型赔率比和参数后验分布，显著提升计算效率，尤其适用于多模型比较和下一代探测器数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的引力波贝叶斯模型选择需要分别对不同模型进行单独分析并比较贝叶斯证据，计算成本高，尤其是在面对多模型或高信息量数据时效率低下。作者希望开发一种能够直接在模型空间采样的方法，以降低计算负担。

Method: 基于eryn采样器构建t-roo，采用可逆跳转马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（RJMCMC）方法，实现对双致密星并合引力波信号的跨维度推断，单次分析即可同时输出模型赔率比和参数后验。

Result: 在注入测试中，t-roo与嵌套采样器dynesty结果一致；成功分析了真实事件GW190425和GW230529，这些事件仅靠系统参数无法明确判断中子星成分的存在。

Conclusion: t-roo可用于任意模型比较场景，尤其对下一代引力波探测器（如需要同时比较多个竞争模型）具有重要实用价值，能大幅降低计算资源需求。

Abstract: Bayesian inference is commonly employed in the analysis of gravitational-wave signals not only to estimate the source parameters, but also for model selection. The latter provides insight into the physics of the source and has the potential to inform the direction for future model development. Although model comparison is usually performed by analyzing the data separately with different models and comparing the obtained Bayesian evidences, an alternative approach consists in sampling directly over the model itself. Here, we present t-roo, a reversible jump Markov chain Monte Carlo sampler capable of performing transdimensional inference on gravitational-wave signals from compact binary coalescences. Employing t-roo, a single analysis provides simultaneously the model odds ratio and the parameter posteriors for the favored models, hence yielding a potentially substantial computational advantage, particularly when comparing many models or analyzing highly informative data. t-roo is built on the sampler eryn and is specifically designed to compare models describing different kinds of sources, i.e., binary black hole, binary neutron star, or neutron star-black hole systems, as well as multiple models for the same source class. We validate the sampler on a set of injections, finding agreement with the results obtained with the nested sampler dynesty. We then use t-roo to analyze the real events GW190425 and GW230529, for which the system's parameters alone do not provide conclusive evidence of the presence of a neutron star component. t-roo can be adapted to any model-comparison scenario, thus providing a valuable tool in particular for next-generation detectors, where analyzing data separately with competing models becomes computationally even more demanding.

</details>

### [134] [Tailoring optical Schrödinger cat states via orientation-dependent high-harmonic generation in $\rm{H}_2^+$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21248)
*Ziyang Gan,Wei-Chao Jiang,Ahai Chen,Yuhai Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明，在H₂⁺分子中，分子取向角θ可作为高次谐波产生过程中调控光学薛定谔猫态的结构内禀、连续可调控制参数。基于两种条件性测量策略（共振增强低次谐波条件与平台谐波条件），系统展示了从kitten态到cat态的可控交叉行为，且该调控与激光强度、聚焦几何及分子密度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种无需依赖外部实验参数（如激光强度、聚焦几何等）的、结构内禀的连续调控方法，用于在分子体系中通过高次谐波产生工程化光学薛定谔猫态。

Method: 耦合含时薛定谔方程模拟与全量子化高次谐波产生框架，基于Wigner函数评估两种条件性测量策略下后选择谐波模的量子态：1) 低次谐波条件利用1σ_g→1σ_u和1σ_g→1π_u跃迁的互补偶极选择定则；2) 平台谐波条件利用双中心相消干涉效应。

Result: 1) 低次谐波条件下，两种跃迁通道随θ变化呈现方向相反的kitten-cat交叉；2) 平台谐波条件下，依赖阶次的双中心干涉角θ^*(q)产生可重入的cat→kitten→cat转变。

Conclusion: 分子取向角θ提供了一种连续可调、结构内禀的控制参数，可实现与激光强度、聚焦几何和分子密度解耦的光学薛定谔猫态调控，这是原子体系无法实现的。

Abstract: We theoretically demonstrate that the molecular orientation angle $θ$ provides a structurally intrinsic, continuously tunable control parameter for engineering optical Schrödinger cat states via high-harmonic generation (HHG) in H$_2^+$. Coupling time-dependent Schrödinger equation simulations to the fully quantized HHG framework, we evaluate the Wigner functions of the post-selected harmonic-mode states under two complementary conditioning strategies. Conditioning on resonance-enhanced low-order harmonics exploits the complementary dipole selection rules of the $1σ_g\to1σ_u$ and $1σ_g\to1π_u$ transitions, driving a kitten-cat crossover whose direction is opposite in the two channels as $θ$ is varied. Conditioning on plateau harmonics instead exploits two-center destructive interference, producing a reentrant cat$\to$kitten$\to$cat transition controlled by the order-dependent interference angle $θ^*(q)$. In both cases the crossover is decoupled from the laser intensity, focal geometry, and molecular density, offering a degree of control with no counterpart in atomic targets.

</details>


### [135] [Machine-Learned Compact Subspace Generation for Quantum Selected Configuration Interaction within Density Matrix Embedding Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20585)
*Ashish Kumar Patra,Anurag K. S. V.,Ruchika Bhat,Sai Shankar P.,Rahul Maitra,Jaiganesh G*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）的紧凑子空间生成协议QSCI-RBM，并将其集成到密度矩阵嵌入理论（DMET）框架中，用于计算分子基态能量。在SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶与抑制剂卡莫氟结合的蛋白质-配体复合物模拟中，DMET-QSCI-RBM仅访问约4%的配置子空间即达到化学精度，而标准DMET-SQD方法访问高达20%的子空间仍无法达到化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于量子采样的配置恢复技术主要通过施加对称性约束来构建子空间，但无法保证选择最物理相关的配置，导致子空间过大、经典对角化成本过高。本文旨在通过机器学习方法生成更紧凑的物理相关配置子空间，降低经典计算开销。

Method: 提出QSCI-RBM协议：使用受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）对量子采样得到的配置进行训练，学习主导行列式的概率分布，从而有针对性地生成高概率配置。将该协议集成到密度矩阵嵌入理论（DMET）框架中，形成DMET-QSCI-RBM方法。

Result: 在SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶与卡莫氟结合的蛋白质-配体复合物模拟中，DMET-QSCI-RBM仅访问约4%的配置子空间即达到化学精度；标准DMET-SQD方法访问高达20%的子空间仍无法达到化学精度。

Conclusion: RBM辅助的配置生成方法能够产生显著更紧凑的子空间，同时保持物理精度，从而减少经典计算开销，实现复杂生物系统的可扩展量子嵌入模拟。

Abstract: Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD), an extension of Quantum Selected Configuration Interaction (QSCI), has emerged as a promising hybrid quantum-classical paradigm for computing molecular ground state energies. By leveraging quantum sampling instead of variational optimization, QSCI avoids barren plateaus and enables direct reconstruction of correlated electronic wavefunctions. However, existing configuration recovery techniques primarily enforce symmetry constraints without guaranteeing optimal selection of the most physically relevant configurations, often leading to unnecessarily large subspaces and increased classical diagonalization costs. In this work, we introduce a machine-learned compact subspace generation protocol based on Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs), termed QSCI-RBM, and integrate it within the Density Matrix Embedding Theory (DMET) framework. The RBM is trained on quantum-sampled configurations to learn the underlying probability distribution of dominant determinants, enabling the targeted generation of high-probability configurations. We apply this framework to the simulation of a protein-ligand complex involving the inhibitor Carmofur bound to the SARS-CoV-2 main protease ($M^{\text{pro}}$). Our results demonstrate that DMET-QSCI-RBM achieves energies within the chemical accuracy threshold by accessing only approximately 4% of the configuration subspace. In contrast, standard DMET-SQD simulations failed to reach chemical accuracy while accessing up to 20% of the subspace, even as the chemical potential itself nearly converged. These findings highlight that RBM-assisted configuration generation produces significantly more compact subspaces while preserving physical accuracy, thereby reducing classical computational overhead and enabling the scalable quantum embedding simulation of complex biological systems.

</details>


### [136] [Autonomization of Quantum Systems and the Emergence of the Work Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20602)
*Carlo Cepollaro,Danial Chughtai,Alberto Spalvieri,Marcus Huber,Alberto Rolandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将受控动力学嵌入更大量子系统能量守恒演化中，将功定义为系统间转移的能量，解决了量子热力学中功的可观测性问题，推导出量子功算符及其涨落定理，并扩展到开放量子系统得到算符形式的热力学第一定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法假设对量子系统的经典控制（含时哈密顿量），但忽略了控制本身巨大的能量代价，使得功的数值缺乏操作意义。需要一种自洽的、考虑控制能量成本的功定义方法。

Method: 将受控动力学自主化（autonomizing），即把含时驱动哈密顿量嵌入到更大复合系统的能量守恒演化中；定义功算符为驱动系统与控制系统间的能量转移算子；推导量子涨落定理（恢复经典Jarzynski等式）；扩展至开放量子系统，得到功、热与内能变化的算符表达。

Result: 建立了量子功算符，规避了先前存在的不可行定理；推导出量子版本的功涨落定理，在经典极限下恢复Jarzynski等式；量化了非完美控制对功统计的修正；在开放量子系统中获得了算符形式的热力学第一定律。

Conclusion: 功在量子力学中是一个可观测量——前提是将控制系统纳入描述而非视作外部经典源。这一框架自洽地解决了关于功是否可观测的长期争论。

Abstract: Estimation of work at the quantum scale remains a central challenge in quantum thermodynamics. Canonical approaches have a fundamental drawback: they assume classical control of the quantum system, as implicit in a time-dependent Hamiltonian. Yet the energetic cost of implementing this control is omitted and can exceed the system's energy scale by orders of magnitude, calling into question the operational significance of work values. We address this by autonomizing the controlled energy transfer, embedding the driven dynamics into an energy-conserving evolution on a larger quantum system. Work is then unambiguously identified with the energy transferred between the two systems, singling out a unique observable on the driven system: the work operator. We show that the quantum work operator evades a no-go theorem by deriving a quantum fluctuation theorem that recovers the Jarzynski equality in classical scenarios. We incorporate imperfect control and quantify corrections to work statistics. Extending the framework to open quantum systems, we obtain an operatorial first law in which work, heat, and internal-energy changes are represented by distinct operators on the reduced system. Together, these results settle the long-standing debate over whether work is a quantum observable: it is, once the controlling system is included in the description rather than treated as external.

</details>


### [137] [Perturbatively Stable Self-Correcting Classical Memory from Gauge Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20605)
*Ryan Thorngren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明三维Wegner规范理论中的自校正记忆对任意足够小的哈密顿量扰动是稳定的，提出了一种称为"规范平均"的新方法，该方法给出了显式破缺的规范对称性通过涨落有效恢复的条件，并表明自校正记忆相是涨落稳定的，其抗扰动能力随温度升高而增强，直至临界温度Tc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维Wegner规范理论中自校正记忆在扰动下的稳定性，特别是探究温度如何影响该稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种名为"规范平均"（gauge averaging）的新方法，用于分析规范对称性在微扰下的有效恢复条件。

Result: 证明了三维Wegner规范理论的自校正记忆对任意足够小的哈密顿量扰动是稳定的；自校正记忆相是涨落稳定的，且其鲁棒性随温度升高而增强，直到临界温度Tc。

Conclusion: 规范平均方法为理解涨落诱导的对称性恢复提供了新工具，表明自校正量子记忆可以在有限温度下通过涨落维持稳定性。

Abstract: We show that the self-correcting memory in 3d Wegner gauge theory is stable to arbitrary small enough perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our proof relies on a new method we dub ``gauge averaging'', which gives conditions under which explicitly broken gauge symmetries are effectively restored by fluctuations. These conditions show that the self-correcting memory phase is fluctuation stabilized, with its robustness to perturbations increasing with increasing temperature, up to some $T_c > 0$.

</details>


### [138] [Enabling Neutral Atom Integration: Redesigning Device Models for Universal Quantum Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20616)
*Yannick Stade,Lukas Burgholzer,Ludwig Schmid,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出现有量子计算生态系统（如Qiskit、Cirq）的设备模型仅适用于超导量子硬件，无法表示中性原子设备特有的动态量子比特重排和分区操作等计算能力，导致该类设备难以集成。作者提出重新设计设备模型以忠实描述中性原子设备，并在QDMI框架中验证，结果显示对于16量子比特、600门的电路，路由开销保真度提升高达100,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子生态系统中的设备模型长期受超导硬件影响，采用静态量子比特位置和固定耦合映射，无法表示中性原子设备特有的动态量子比特重排和分区操作等计算能力，导致该类设备被排斥在主流生态系统之外，形成技术锁定。

Method: 重新设计设备模型以忠实表示中性原子设备的计算能力（如动态重排、分区操作），并在Quantum Device Management Interface (QDMI)框架中进行评估验证。

Result: 在16量子比特、600门的电路上，所提设备模型将路由开销保真度提升高达100,000倍。

Conclusion: 需重新思考当前设备模型以忠实表示中性原子设备，从而实现其无缝集成到通用量子生态系统中，释放该类新兴硬件的计算潜力。

Abstract: Quantum computing is transitioning from an academic idea to a practical technology, driven by recent hardware advancements and clear paths toward real-world applications. Universal quantum ecosystems (e.g., Qiskit, Cirq, PennyLane) facilitate this transition by providing a consistent interface to diverse quantum devices, abstracting hardware-specific details through a device model that captures each device's computational capabilities. However, these device models have historically been shaped by superconducting hardware, assuming static qubit positions and fixed coupling maps. This prevents them from representing the unique computational capabilities of emerging technologies such as neutral atoms, which feature dynamic qubit rearrangement and zoned operations. As a result, although numerous specialized compilers for neutral atom devices already exist, they cannot retrieve the hardware information they need through these ecosystems - creating a technology lock that hinders or even prevents the integration of neutral atom devices. In this work, we demonstrate how this limitation leads to suboptimal compilation results and can exclude certain devices entirely. Motivated by this, we propose rethinking current device models to faithfully represent neutral atom devices, enabling their seamless integration into universal quantum ecosystems. Evaluations conducted within the Quantum Device Management Interface (QDMI) demonstrate that the proposed device model unlocks a routing overhead fidelity improvement by a factor of up to 100,000 on a circuit with 16 qubits and 600 gates.

</details>


### [139] [Learning the closest Slater determinant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20623)
*Nisarga Paul,Haimeng Zhao,David D. Dai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了从任意费米子多体态中学习具有最大保真度的斯莱特行列式问题，提供了经典和量子算法（时间复杂度为 m^{poly(n,1/ε)}），证明了匹配的硬度下界，揭示了保真度 2/3 是优化景观的相变点，并应用于费米-哈伯德模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体态的紧凑可解释描述在量子科学中至关重要，该问题受到哈特里-福克方法和不可知层析成像的启发，旨在从任意费米子多体态中提取最接近的斯莱特行列式。

Method: 提出了经典和量子算法，返回保真度在 ε 以内的斯莱特行列式，时间复杂度为 m^{poly(n,1/ε)}；同时利用量子副本时仅需 poly(m,n,1/ε) 个副本。通过标准复杂性猜想证明了沿某些参数轴的匹配硬度下界。分析了优化景观，发现保真度 2/3 以上时驻点为唯一全局最大值，而低于 2/3 则可能出现虚假驻点。

Result: 证明了保真度 2/3 是优化景观的相变点；提供了具有可证明保证的算法工具；在费米-哈伯德模型中从神经量子态解中提取了最接近的斯莱特行列式。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解和模拟费米子多体系统提供了具有可证明保证的算法工具，适用于经典或量子模拟场景。

Abstract: Learning compact, interpretable descriptions of quantum many-body states is an important task in quantum science. We study the task of learning the Slater determinant with maximum fidelity to an arbitrary fermionic many-body state, with motivation from both Hartree-Fock methods and agnostic tomography. Given an $n$-fermion wavefunction built from $m$ fermionic modes, we provide classical and quantum algorithms returning a Slater determinant with fidelity within $\varepsilon$ of maximal in time $m^{\text{poly}(n,1/\varepsilon)}$. We prove matching hardness lower bounds, assuming standard complexity conjectures, along some parameter axes. Given access to quantum copies, we prove this can be accomplished with $\text{poly}(m,n,1/\varepsilon)$ copies of $ρ$. We also show that above a fidelity of $2/3$ any stationary point is the unique global maximum while below $2/3$ the optimization landscape can have spurious stationary points, and hence $2/3$ marks a transition point in the optimization landscape for this problem. We apply the algorithm to the Fermi-Hubbard model, extracting the closest Slater determinant from neural quantum state solutions. Together, our results provide algorithmic tools with provable guarantees in understanding fermionic many-body systems with classical or quantum simulation.

</details>


### [140] [HI-QDC: An Isometric Modular Scalable Architecture for Quantum Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20633)
*Mohadeseh Azari,Anoosha Fayyaz,Amy Babay,David Tipper,Prashant Krishnamurthy,Kaushik Seshadreesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过端到端纯化机制将服务器中心量子数据中心的路径多样性转化为保真度恢复能力，并设计了HI-QDC架构实现递归可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务器中心量子数据中心架构虽然通过分布式通信提供可扩展性，但路径长度增加导致贝尔态测量次数增多和端到端保真度下降，亟需解决保真度与扩展性之间的矛盾。

Method: 1) 在黑盒模型中推导了恢复单拷贝原始链路所需的最小Werner态拷贝数，比较了递归2-to-1与优化嵌套r-to-1纯化方案；2) 在概率性BCube架构中利用边不相交路径多样性提供原始拷贝；3) 提出HI-QDC架构，通过等距模块化设计使纯化将模块内端到端纠缠转化为模块间链路的有效资源。

Result: 确定了BCube模块在端到端纯化下保持原始链路保真度和产率的参数区间；模块可隐藏内部跳数，充当高层网络的有效原始链路。

Conclusion: 拓扑提供纯化所需的路径多样性，纯化将其转化为保真度恢复，两者协同实现递归可扩展的量子数据中心网络。

Abstract: Server-centric quantum data-center architectures offer scalability by distributing communication tasks across QPUs rather than concentrating complexity in a centralized switching core. However, scaling such architectures increases the path length, the number of Bell-state measurements, and the loss of end-to-end fidelity. We ask whether the path diversity of a server-centric topology can be converted into a mechanism for preserving not only rate, but also fidelity. We study this through end-to-end purification as a fidelity-restoration mechanism. First, in a black-box model, we determine the minimum number of raw end-to-end Werner-state copies, each carrying the degraded Werner parameter of a distance-ell path, that purification must consume to recover a single copy matching an elementary link, comparing recursive 2-to-1 and optimized nested r-to-1 purification. Second, we instantiate these requirements in a probabilistic BCube architecture, whose edge-disjoint path diversity supplies the raw copies. Because purification imposes a lower bound on input fidelity, no path redundancy can raise the output below this threshold, which limits scaling. To address it we present the Hop-Independent Quantum Data Center (HI-QDC), an isometric, modular, scalable architecture in which purification transforms end-to-end entanglement across a module into an effective link-level resource for the inter-module topology. Our results identify the regimes in which a BCube module, under end-to-end purification, preserves both the fidelity and the yield of an elementary link. The module then hides its internal hop count and acts as an effective elementary link for a higher-level network. Thus topology supplies the path multiplicity purification requires, while purification converts it into fidelity recovery, enabling recursively scalable quantum data-center networks.

</details>


### [141] [Unitary $k$-designs without Hamiltonian quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20640)
*Pratik Nandy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出2PK协议，仅需单一混沌哈密顿量和两次泡利算符插入即可生成近似酉k-设计，无需多次独立哈密顿淬火，并通过框架势验证了其在随机矩阵、SYK模型及其简化模型中的有效性，同时建立了有限温框架势与配分函数的联系并给出了全息解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上生成酉设计需要指数级资源或多重独立哈密顿淬火，本文旨在证明单一混沌哈密顿量足以生成近似酉k-设计，从而大幅降低资源需求。

Method: 提出2PK协议：在固定哈密顿量演化过程中插入两次泡利算符（kick），通过评估框架势来检验生成的系综是否收敛到Haar值，以此证明近似酉设计的出现。在Gaussian随机矩阵系综、SYK模型和自旋（玻色）SYK模型上进行了验证。

Result: 2PK协议生成的系综框架势收敛到Haar值，表明生成了近似酉k-设计；建立了有限温框架势的定义及其与配分函数的关系；给出了该协议在引力对偶图像中的全息解释。

Conclusion: 单一混沌哈密顿量配合两次泡利踢即可生成近似酉设计，无需多重淬火；该协议天然适用于有限温情形，且具有全息对偶的潜在解释。

Abstract: Unitary designs provide an efficient means of emulating Haar randomness without the requirement of exponential resources. Hamiltonian quench protocols have recently been shown to generate such designs, but achieving this typically requires multiple independent Hamiltonian quenches, even via temporal ensembles. Here, we show that no Hamiltonian quenches are required: a single chaotic Hamiltonian is sufficient to generate approximate unitary $k$-designs. We introduce a two-Pauli-kick (2PK) protocol, where unitary evolution under a fixed Hamiltonian is interspersed with two Pauli operator insertions (kicks). By evaluating the frame potential, we demonstrate that the resulting ensemble converges to the Haar value, indicating the emergence of approximate unitary designs. We verify this protocol for Gaussian random matrix ensembles, the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model, and simplified spin (bosonic) SYK models. Furthermore, our protocol admits a canonical definition of finite-temperature frame potentials and establishes the relation with the partition function. We outline a plausible holographic interpretation of our protocol from the dual gravity picture.

</details>


### [142] [Detecting Hidden Nonlinear High Frequency Modes Beyond Fundamental Minimal Temporal Resolution Using Weak Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20647)
*Igor Prlina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性量子力学效应能否通过强测量被检测，即使非线性效应仅存在于高频模式中。通过定义时间不可区分性公设并构建非线性玩具模型，研究表明即使是桌面级弱后选择测量也能约束高频非线性演化模式，即使其频率超出基本时间分辨率极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性量子力学效应是否可能通过仅存在于高频模式中而逃避强测量的检测，检验是否存在隐藏非线性效应的物理过程。

Method: 定义了时间不可区分性公设来描述平均过程，手工构建了适合逃避检测的非线性玩具模型，并利用桌面级弱后选择测量进行检测。

Result: 即使在理想的逃避检测设置下，桌面级弱后选择测量仍能检测到非线性行为，并对超出基本时间分辨率频率的高频非线性演化模式施加约束。

Conclusion: 非线性量子力学效应无法通过仅存在于高频模式来完全逃避检测，弱后选择测量是一种有效的检测手段。

Abstract: In this work we study whether nonlinear models of quantum mechanics can avoid detection by strong measurements, if the nonlinear effects only exist as high frequency modes. A potential physical process which could hide such modes would be amplitude level time averaging over fundamentally indistinguishable times. To that end, we have defined a temporal indistinguishability postulate which describes the averaging process, and handcrafted a nonlinear toy model well suited to avoid detection. We have demonstrated that even in such an ideal setup, tabletop weak postselected measurements can still detect nonlinear behavior and put constraints on nonlinear high frequency modes of evolution, even at frequencies beyond fundamental minimal temporal resolution.

</details>


### [143] [Finite Key Underwater Quantum Key Distribution: Performance Analysis and Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20664)
*Trevor Thomas,Michael Warnock,Sarah Previte,Benjamin Drozdenko,Walter O. Krawec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子密钥分发（QKD）在水下场景中的性能，通过模拟两种不同诱骗态协议在有限密钥场景下的表现，发现某些水下信道中“更简单”的QKD协议优于复杂协议，并验证了经典优势蒸馏可显著提升QKD在水下的最大传输距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然QKD在光纤和自由空间中的研究已很充分，但水下场景的研究尚处于起步阶段，需要系统评估QKD在水下环境中的实际性能。

Method: 考虑实际有限密钥场景，模拟评估两种不同诱骗态协议在水下信道中的性能，并引入经典优势蒸馏方法进行优化。

Result: 在某些水下信道中，简单QKD协议的性能优于复杂协议；经典优势蒸馏可大幅提升QKD在水下的最大传输距离。

Conclusion: 水下QKD的性能受信道特性影响，协议选择需因地制宜，且经典优势蒸馏是提升水下QKD性能的有效手段。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) allows for the establishment of a secret key between two parties, secure against computationally unbounded adversaries. Most experimental and theoretical research in this area, investigates the performance of QKD over fiber or free space channels. A growing body of work, however, has begun to investigate its performance in underwater scenarios. Here, we consider the realistic finite key scenario, and evaluate the simulated performance of two different decoy state protocols. We show that there are certain underwater channels where "simpler" QKD protocols outperform more complex ones. Finally, we also investigate methods to improve QKD performance underwater, specifically looking at classical advantage distillation, which we show can greatly improve the maximal distance supported by QKD underwater.

</details>


### [144] [Bistability of Exciton-Photon Microcavities in the Ultrastrong-Coupling Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20688)
*Rashed Aljasmi,Hisham Sati,Hichem Eleuch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超强耦合作用下具有Kerr非线性的激子-光子微腔，分析了低极化激元的稳态双稳性。结果表明，尽管双稳性判据形式上与强耦合情况类似，但超强耦合中的反旋转项和抗磁A²项会重整化极化激元谱和组分，导致有效失谐和非线性强度发生g依赖性变化，从而使双稳回滞窗口相对于强耦合预测发生偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超强耦合作用下激子-光子微腔中Kerr非线性的双稳特性，特别是超强耦合带来的反旋转项和抗磁A²项如何修正传统的强耦合图像。

Method: 从完整的Hopfield-Rabi-Kerr模型出发，利用下极化激元和上极化激元能量分离条件，约化为下极化激元的有效单模描述，进而分析稳态稳定性并推导双稳性判据。

Result: 超强耦合下双稳性定性上与强耦合类似，但反旋转项和抗磁A²项使得有效失谐和Kerr非线性强度U_LP呈现g依赖性，导致转变点和滞后窗口相对于强耦合（RWA）预测发生偏移。

Conclusion: 超强耦合中虽然半经典双稳性判据形式上保持标准Kerr振子形式，但实际物理参数的重整化使得系统行为偏离强耦合框架下的预期，需要更精确的模型来描述。

Abstract: We investigate a coherently driven exciton--photon microcavity with Kerr nonlinearity in the ultrastrong-coupling regime. When the lower and upper polariton branches are well separated in energy, the full Hopfield--Rabi--Kerr model reduces to an effective single-mode description of the lower polariton. We analyze the stability of the lower-polariton steady states. We show that the resulting bistability is qualitatively similar to that in the strong-coupling regime. However, in the ultrastrong-coupling regime counter-rotating processes and the diamagnetic $A^{2}$ term renormalize the polariton spectrum and composition, changing the effective detuning $\tildeΔ_{1}$ and nonlinearity $U_{\mathrm{LP}}$ $g$-dependency beyond the strong-coupling (RWA) picture. As a result, although the semiclassical bistability criterion keeps its standard Kerr--oscillator form, the turning points and hysteresis window are shifted relative to the strong-coupling prediction.

</details>


### [145] [Fermionic Genuine Multiparty Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20707)
*James Allen,Liuke Lyu,William Witczak-Krempa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可高效计算的费米子真正多体纠缠度量——费米子真正多体负性（fGMN），并通过半定规划实现。研究表明，许多态（包括费米子稳定子态）具有fGMN但无非费米子GME，且fGMN在分离度和温度上表现出有限突然死亡点等新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子系统中双体纠缠度量已有成熟研究，但真正多体纠缠（GME）的理解尚不充分，缺乏有效的度量工具。

Method: 通过半定规划（SDP）定义并计算费米子真正多体负性（fGMN），证明其为纠缠单调，并是费米子负性的自然多体推广。

Result: 发现大量态（包括费米子稳定子态）具有fGMN但无非费米子GME；fGMN在分离度和温度上表现出有限突然死亡点，与双体费米子负性有显著现象差异。

Conclusion: fGMN是有效的费米子真正多体纠缠度量，揭示了费米子系统多体纠缠的独特行为。

Abstract: Entanglement can show fundamentally different behavior in fermionic systems. However, while bipartite measures of fermionic entanglement have been established, genuine multiparty entanglement (GME) in fermionic systems is much less understood. We introduce an efficiently computable measure (via semi-definite programming) of fermionic GME, fermion genuine multiparty negativity (fGMN), and show that it is an entanglement monotone and a natural multipartite extension of the fermionic negativity. Using this measure, we find many states which have fGMN but no non-fermionic GME, including large classes of fermionic stabilizer states. The fGMN also displays some major phenomenological differences to the bipartite fermionic negativity, such as finite sudden death points over separation and temperature.

</details>


### [146] [Suppressing errors in analog logical rotation gates via balanced fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20756)
*Sam McArdle,Alexander M. Dalzell,Fernando G. S. L. Brandão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"平衡融合"技术，将模拟逻辑旋转的误差缩放从O(pφ)改进到O(pφ^1.5)，无需回退合成，提高了小角度模拟逻辑旋转的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期容错量子算法中使用的模拟逻辑旋转现有方案误差为O(pφ)，虽然对小角度φ可压制错误率，但误差缩放仍有改进空间，需要更高效的技术来提升小角度旋转的保真度。

Method: 提出"平衡融合"技术，通过对模拟逻辑旋转中相干误差项累积的改进分析，实现误差缩放从O(pφ)到O(pφ^1.5)的优化，无需依赖回退合成。

Result: 将模拟逻辑旋转的逻辑错误率从O(pφ)降低到O(pφ^1.5)，显著改善了小角度旋转的误差表现。

Conclusion: 平衡融合技术提升了模拟逻辑旋转在小角度场景下的实用性，可作为 cultivation 驱动的旋转合成的替代方案。

Abstract: There have been a number of recent proposals to use analog logical rotations in early fault-tolerant quantum algorithms. Existing proposals implement a logical rotation by angle $φ$, with error $O(pφ)$, where $p$ is the physical error rate. While this is not fault-tolerant, if $φ$ is sufficiently small the logical error rate can be suppressed. In this work, we introduce and analyze the `balanced fusion' technique for improving the error scaling of analog logical rotations to $O(p φ^{1.5})$, without having to resort to fallback synthesis. Balanced fusion is enabled by an improved analysis of the accumulation of coherent error terms in analog logical rotations. Our techniques improve the viability of analog logical rotations for small rotation angles as an alternative to cultivation-powered rotation synthesis.

</details>


### [147] [Component-Level Inverse Design of Transmon Qubits Using Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20795)
*Olivia Seidel,Firas Abouzahr,Abhishek Chakraborty,Sadman Ahmed Shanto,Saikat Das,Daniel Baxter,Jonathan Asaadi,Nicola Pancotti,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Sara Sussman,Enectali Figueroa-Feliciano,Eli M Levenson-Falk,Taylor L. Patti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于神经网络的反向设计工作流，能够将目标哈密顿参数直接映射到超导量子比特的布局几何参数。在平面transmon布局上验证，97%的设计几何可用，频率误差0.73%，非谐性误差1.58%，单次查询56ms（CPU），比传统电磁仿真快2100倍以上，批量推理可实现5×10^5至4.8×10^7倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超导量子比特设计需要迭代式前向循环（选择几何→仿真→提取电路参数→优化几何），耗时且计算密集。作者希望研究反向设计方法，将目标哈密顿参数直接映射到组件级布局参数，以加速设计流程。

Method: 采用神经网络工作流：训练时将反向模型与冻结的前向替代模型配对，在哈密顿参数空间（而非布局参数空间）中评估损失。在平面transmon布局上验证，与常规电磁求解器对比。支持批处理推理，可在CPU和GPU上运行。

Result: 97%生成设计产生可用几何；频率平均百分比误差0.73%，非谐性1.58%；单次CPU查询56ms vs 传统EM仿真约2min（加速>2100倍）；CPU批处理0.24ms/样本，GPU批处理2.5μs/样本，相对单次CPU EM提取分别加速5.0×10^5和4.8×10^7倍。

Conclusion: 组件级反向设计有效扩展和补充了传统电磁仿真，即使对于约1000样本的小数据集也能取得良好效果。

Abstract: Designing a superconducting qubit to realize specific Hamiltonian parameters typically requires iterating through a time and compute-intensive forward loop in which the designer chooses a layout geometry, simulates it, extracts circuit parameters such as capacitances, and refines the geometry. We study the inverse version of this task using a neural-network workflow that maps target Hamiltonian parameters directly to component-level layout parameters, which we subsequently demonstrate on a planar transmon layout. During training, we pair the inverse model with a frozen forward surrogate model and evaluate the loss in Hamiltonian space rather than in layout-parameter space. In validation against a conventional EM solver, 97% of generated designs produce usable geometries, and the inverse-plus-surrogate pipeline reaches mean percent errors of 0.73% for qubit frequency and 1.58% for anharmonicity, comparable to or below the fabrication and simulation-to-measurement uncertainty expected for academic-process transmon devices of this type. A single pipeline query takes approximately 56 ms on CPU, versus approximately 2 min for a conventional EM capacitance extraction on the same hardware, a speedup of more than 2,100 times. Batching minimizes the AI model inference overhead, reducing the runtime to 0.24 ms per sample on CPU and 2.5 microseconds per sample on GPU, resulting in speedups of 5.0 x 10^5 and 4.8 x 10^7, respectively, relative to a single conventional CPU EM extraction. Our results indicate that component-level inverse design usefully extends and complements conventional EM simulation, including for small datasets on the order of 1,000 samples.

</details>


### [148] [Hardness and Complexity Transition of Noisy Random Circuit Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20804)
*Byeongseon Go,Changhun Oh,Hyunseok Jeong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机电路采样（RCS）在噪声环境下的经典可模拟性边界。针对局部去极化噪声强度γ，作者证明了在标准平均情况#P-硬度猜想下，当γ=O(log n/(nd))时，噪声RCS仍然难以被经典模拟（除非多项式层级崩溃）。同时，结合收敛到均匀性结果和单调性约简，当γ=ω(log n/(nd))时，噪声RCS可被高效经典模拟。因此，γ=Θ(log n/(nd))被确定为渐近复杂性转变尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机电路采样是展示量子优势的主要候选方案，但实际噪声不可避免。核心问题在于确定噪声强度边界，区分经典可模拟与经典困难区间。现有缺乏架构通用的严格硬度边界，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 结合低次多项式外推法与单调性约简。首先证明在某一去极化噪声强度下存在高效经典模拟，则对所有更大噪声强度都存在高效模拟（单调性）。再利用低次多项式外推将理想RCS硬度猜想迁移到指定噪声强度。最后结合Dalzell等人的收敛到均匀性结果与单调性约简，得到上界。

Result: 在标准平均情况#P-硬度猜想下，任何满足该猜想的电路架构中，当γ=O(log n/(nd))时，噪声RCS在逆多项式全变差距离内仍不可被经典模拟；当γ=ω(log n/(nd))时，对分层规则连接架构可高效经典模拟。因此γ=Θ(log n/(nd))是渐近复杂度转变尺度。

Conclusion: 建立了架构通用的噪声RCS硬度下界，无需引入理想RCS硬度框架之外的额外猜想。同时确定了噪声强度转变的渐近标度，为实验实现量子优势提供了噪声容忍度的理论指导。

Abstract: Random circuit sampling (RCS) is a leading candidate for demonstrating quantum advantage, supported by strong complexity-theoretic evidence of hardness in the ideal setting and by rapid experimental progress to date. In practice, however, noise is unavoidable, and a central problem is to identify the noise-strength boundary between classically simulable and classically hard regimes. In this work, we establish an architecture-general hardness bound for this boundary for the standard local depolarizing noise of strength $γ$. Assuming the standard average-case #P-hardness conjecture for ideal RCS, we show that, for any circuit architecture satisfying this conjecture, noisy RCS on the same architecture remains hard to simulate classically within any inverse-polynomial total variation distance whenever $γ=O(\log n/(nd))$ for $n$-qubit circuits of depth $d$, unless the polynomial hierarchy collapses. Crucially, noisy-RCS hardness follows without any additional conjectural or architecture-specific assumption beyond those already entering the ideal-RCS hardness framework. Our proof combines a low-degree polynomial extrapolation with a monotonicity reduction showing that efficient classical simulation at one depolarizing noise strength implies efficient simulation at every larger strength. Together, these ingredients transfer the standard ideal-RCS hardness conjecture to sampling hardness at a prespecified noise strength. Finally, combining the convergence-to-uniformity result of Dalzell et al. [Commun. Math. Phys. 405, 78 (2024)] with our monotonicity reduction yields efficient classical simulation for $γ=ω(\log n/(nd))$ on layered, regularly connected architectures. Thus, wherever the two architectural settings overlap, this identifies $γ=Θ(\log n/(nd))$ as the asymptotic complexity-transition scale.

</details>


### [149] [An Operational Resolution of the Third-Particle Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20815)
*Časlav Brukner,Esteban Castro-Ruiz,Marius Krumm*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子参考系理论中的"第三粒子悖论"给出了操作性的解决方案，指出该悖论源于将概率的操作性陈述错误地替换为关于约化密度算符相等的更强、依赖于表示的陈述。如果第三粒子在原参考系中确实不可观测，则在变换后的参考系中通过适当的可观测量约束仍可保持其不可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子参考系理论中出现的"第三粒子悖论"——即一个系统在一个量子参考系描述中无关紧要，但在变换到另一个量子参考系后似乎变得相关，因为变换后再取迹与先取迹再变换的结果不一致。

Method: 通过将可观测量与态一同变换，或等价地，正确识别需要丢弃的子系统来解决悖论。论证这种比较只有在对可观测量也进行相应变换时才具有操作性意义。

Result: 如果第三粒子在原始参考系中对于所有实际可用的测量都是无关的，则变换后的测量在新参考系中形成一个受限的代数，使得第三粒子仍然无关。悖论源于用约化密度算符的相等性取代了关于概率的操作性陈述。

Conclusion: 将何时可以丢弃自由度的问题与可观测量诱导的、操作性的子系统结构方法联系起来。

Abstract: We give an operational resolution of the third-particle paradox, relevant in the theory of quantum reference frames. The apparent paradox is that a system which is irrelevant in one quantum-reference-frame description can seem to become relevant after changing to another quantum reference frame, because the reduced state obtained after transforming a larger system need not agree with the state obtained by first discarding the extra system and then transforming. We argue that this comparison is not operationally meaningful unless the observables are transformed together with the states, or equivalently, unless the subsystem that needs to be discarded is properly identified. If the third particle is irrelevant for all measurements actually available in the original frame, then the transformed measurements form a restricted algebra in the new frame for which the third particle remains irrelevant. The paradox therefore results from replacing an operational statement about probabilities by a stronger, representation-dependent statement about equality of reduced density operators. We close by relating the question of when degrees of freedom may be discarded to the observable-induced, operational approach to subsystem structure.

</details>


### [150] [Discrete-modulated continuous-variable quantum key distribution with uncertainty principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20840)
*Jiale Mi,Yiming Bian,Song Yu,Zhengyu Li,Yichen Zhang,Hong Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于海森堡不确定性原理的连续变量量子密钥分发安全分析框架，通过多模纠缠源模型建立星座几何与密钥率的显式映射，避免了传统半定规划方法的计算瓶颈。实验验证了256点QAM星座可渐近逼近高斯容量极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散调制连续变量量子密钥分发现有的安全证明依赖半定规划，该方法对高阶星座计算开销巨大且缺乏对非高斯调制的物理洞察，存在理论与实际硬件之间的根本性差距。

Method: 提出基于海森堡不确定性原理的安全分析框架，引入多模纠缠源模型刻画非高斯态制备，建立星座几何与密钥率间的显式映射，支持高阶星座下的数值与解析安全分析。

Result: 在分立元件与集成光子平台上实验验证了该方法，256点QAM调制格式的密钥率可渐近逼近高斯容量极限。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了离散调制CV-QKD的安全分析瓶颈，还通过源模扩展收紧不确定性约束界的原理，为探索复杂非高斯系统的信息论特性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution is a compelling framework for scalable quantum networks due to its seamless integration with existing optical communication infrastructure. However, a fundamental gap persists between theoretical protocols requiring ideal Gaussian modulation and the constrained, discrete-modulated signals dictated by practical high-speed hardware. Current security proofs for discrete modulation rely on semidefinite programming, which suffers from prohibitive computational overhead for high-order constellations and lacks direct physical insight into non-Gaussian modulation.In this Letter, we overcome this limitation by developing a security framework that obviates semidefinite programming in favor of an approach grounded fundamentally in the Heisenberg uncertainty principle. By introducing a multi-mode entanglement-source model to characterize non-Gaussian state preparation, we establish an explicit mapping between constellation geometry and the secret key rate. This framework effectively quantifies the security implications of hardware-limited, finite state preparation, enabling both numerical and analytical security analysis under high-order constellations. We experimentally validate our method on both discrete-component and integrated photonic platforms, demonstrating that a quadrature amplitude modulation format with 256 constellation points can asymptotically approach the Gaussian capacity limit. Beyond quantum key distribution, the principle of tightening uncertainty-constrained bounds via source-mode expansion offers a paradigm for exploring the information-theoretic properties of complex non-Gaussian systems.

</details>


### [151] [Heralded high-dimensional module-based quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20895)
*Xiao Zhang,Wen-Qiang Liu,Hai-Rui Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种高维广义奇偶校验模块，并基于此构建了模块化的受控非门。研究表明在(d-1)!个广义奇偶校验模块中，只有模加和模减两种模块可用于高维量子计算，并提出了基于量子非破坏测量的光学非破坏性实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇偶校验测量是制备和区分纠缠态以及实现量子计算的基本构建块，探索其在高维系统中的推广和应用具有重要意义。

Method: 开发了两种交替的高维广义奇偶校验模块，并基于模块化方法构建高维受控非门。通过分析(d-1)!种广义奇偶校验模块，筛选出可用于高维量子计算的模块。提出了基于量子非破坏测量的光学非破坏性实现方案，利用光子数分辨探测器和单光子探测器进行宣告。

Result: 在(d-1)!个广义奇偶校验模块中，仅模加和模减两种模块可作为高维量子计算的构建块。所提出的模块化量子计算方案是确定性的、可宣告的、对计算基维度不敏感，且无需后选择技术。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块化量子计算方案具有确定性、可宣告性、维度不敏感性和无需后选择等优势，为高维量子计算提供了可行的构建路径。

Abstract: Parity measurements have been explored as building blocks for preparing and discriminating entangled states, as well as for implementing quantum computation. We first develop two alternative high-dimensional generalized parity modules, and then propose a procedure for constructing high-dimensional generalized module-based controlled-NOT gate. The construction of module-based quantum computing introduced here is deterministic, heralded, insensitive to the dimensionality of the computing basis, and postselection technique is not required. The result shows that out of $(d-1)!$ generalized parity modules, only modules $ \mathcal{P}=(j\ominus i)\bmod d$ and $ \mathcal{P}=(j \oplus i)\bmod d$ can be used as building blocks for high-dimensional quantum computing. Furthermore, we proposed an optical nondestructive scheme for implementing generalized parity module through quantum nondemolition measurements, and the success of the parity module is heralded by photon-number-resolving detectors and single-photon detectors.

</details>


### [152] [Multicritical dissipative phase transitions manipulated by dipole--dipole interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20905)
*Jia-Xin Wang,Qian Bin,Jing-Jing Cao,Xin-You Lü*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个耗散型双里德伯原子腔QED系统中的多临界现象，发现原子间偶极-偶极相互作用可重构能级结构，显著改变超辐射相变边界，强相互作用下连续相变消失，仅存一级相变，使超辐射相可在极弱耦合下出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确控制超辐射相变中的临界性对于量子态工程和非平衡相变模拟至关重要，因此需要深入理解多临界现象及其调控机制。

Method: 从理论上研究了一个耗散型双里德伯原子腔QED系统，利用原子间固有的偶极-偶极相互作用来调控能级结构，分析其对超辐射相变边界和多临界点的影响。

Result: 偶极-偶极相互作用将连续二阶超辐射相变的边界和多临界点推向更弱的原子-腔耦合强度；当相互作用足够强时，连续二阶相变和多临界点消失，仅剩不连续的一级相变，使得超辐射相可在任意弱耦合下出现。

Conclusion: 该工作对研究耗散量子相变具有基础意义，并为量子精密测量和量子传感提供了潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Precise control of criticality in superradiant phase transitions is essential for quantum state engineering and the simulation of nonequilibrium phase transitions. Here, we investigate theoretically multicritical phenomena in a dissipative two-Rydberg-atom cavity--QED system. The intrinisic dipole--dipole interaction between the two Rydberg atoms restructures the energy-level landscape of the atomic subsystem, thereby significantly modifying the boundary of the continuous second-order superradiant phase transition, and shifting both the phase boundary and the multicritical point toward weaker atom--cavity strengths. For sufficiently strong dipole--dipole interactions, the continuous second-order phase transition and the multicritical point both disappear, leaving only a discontinuous first--order phase transition that enables the emergence of a superradiant phase even at arbitrarily weak atom--cavity coupling. This work is of fundamental interest for studying dissipative quantum phase transitions, with potential implications for quantum precision measurement and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [153] [Random unitary circuits with constant spectral gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20919)
*Tim Baer,Jeongwan Haah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了两种随机行走（随机Pauli旋转和砖墙随机酉电路）在SU(2^n)上的谱隙常数下界，该下界与量子比特数n无关，并统一适用于所有有限维酉表示，包括酉t-设计中的表示。同时给出了Clifford酉算子的类似常数间隙结果，这是砖墙随机酉电路论证的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机量子电路和随机酉算子的混合速率，特别是谱隙的下界，这对于理解量子电路的随机化能力、酉t-设计的生成效率以及量子混沌等基本问题至关重要。

Method: 通过谱隙的常数下界分析，分别对两种随机行走（随机Pauli旋转和砖墙随机酉电路）进行严格证明。研究涵盖SU(2^n)的所有有限维酉表示，并特别处理了Clifford酉算子的情况。

Result: 两种随机行走的谱隙均存在与n无关的常数下界；该结果统一适用于所有有限维酉表示；Clifford酉算子也具有类似的常数间隙结果。

Conclusion: 随机Pauli旋转和砖墙随机酉电路在SU(2^n)上具有与系统规模无关的恒定谱隙下界，这保证了其随机化效率不随量子比特数增加而退化。Clifford酉算子的常数间隙结果对整体论证构成了关键支撑。

Abstract: We prove constant lower bounds for the spectral gap of the following random walks on unitary groups $\mathsf{SU}(2^n)$ on $n$ qubits. (i) Random Pauli Rotation: choose an $n$-qubit Pauli operator $P$ and an angle $θ\in \mathbb R / 2π\mathbb Z$, both uniformly at random, and apply $e^{\mathrm i θP}$. (ii) Brickwork Random Unitary Circuit: choose $n-1$ unitaries $U_{i}$ uniformly at random from $\mathsf{SU}(4)$ independently, and apply $U_{2j-1}$ on two qubits $2j-1, 2j$ and then $U_{2j}$ on two qubits $2j, 2j+1$. Importantly, the spectral gaps are independent of $n$ and apply for all finite dimensional unitary representations of $\mathsf{SU}(2^n)$ uniformly, including those that appear in unitary $t$-designs. We also prove analogous constant gap results for Clifford unitaries, which are indispensable for our result on Brickwork Random Unitary Circuit.

</details>


### [154] [Generation of bright quantum high-order harmonic driven by combined coherent and bright squeezed vacuum light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20941)
*Wentao Wang,Yaoshun Sun,Liyuan Wang,Lingrui Hu,Dajun Ding,Xiangyu Tang,Mingxuan Li,Jianmin Yuan,Sizuo Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强相干光与弱明亮压缩真空（BSV）激光在原子气体中相互作用产生的量子高次谐波的宏观传播效应。结果表明，BSV光子吸收或发射引起的谐波强度随压力变化与仅用强相干脉冲时不同，BSV脉冲扰动谐波作用相位，调制亚周期阿秒脉冲的相位失配并影响其量子特性，为阿秒量子光谱学奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由强量子光驱动的高次谐波叠加形成的阿秒量子光，以开辟探测物质中量子力学关联的新途径。具体动机是研究量子高次谐波在宏观传播中的效应，特别是弱BSV激光对谐波产生过程的影响。

Method: 将强相干脉冲与弱明亮压缩真空（BSV）激光结合，作用于原子气体，通过宏观传播模拟，分析压力依赖的谐波强度变化、作用相位扰动以及相位失配的调制效应。

Result: 1）BSV光子吸收或发射产生的谐波强度随气压变化与仅用强相干脉冲时不同；2）弱BSV脉冲扰动谐波的作用相位；3）该扰动调制了亚周期阿秒脉冲的相位失配，并在气压变化时影响其量子特性。

Conclusion: 产生明亮量子高次谐波的能力为建立和应用阿秒量子光谱学奠定了基础。

Abstract: Attosecond quantum light, formed by the superposition of high-order harmonics driven by intense quantum light, opens new routes to probe quantum-mechanical correlations in matter. In this study, we have investigated the macroscopic propagation effects of quantum high-order harmonics generated by the combination of strong coherent and weak bright squeezed vacuum (BSV) lasers interacting with atomic gas. Our results reveal that the pressure-dependent intensity of harmonics arising from absorbing or emitting BSV photons differs from that of harmonics generated using only strong coherent pulses. Macroscopic propagation simulations indicate that the action phase of harmonics is perturbed by the weak BSV pulses. This perturbation modulates the phase mismatch of sub-cycle attosecond bursts and affects their quantum properties when the gas pressure varies. The ability to generate bright quantum high-order harmonics lays a foundation for the establishment and application of attosecond quantum spectroscopy.

</details>


### [155] [An Analytically Trained Variational Surrogate for Quantum Phase Estimation on NISQ Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20943)
*Mousumi Kundu,Ashish Kumar Patra,Anurag K. S. V.,Ruchika Bhat,Sai Shankar P.,Alok Shukla,Jaiganesh G*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分量子电路（VQC）的解析代理框架，无需量子电路模拟即可重现量子相位估计（QPE）的测量分布。该框架通过Dirichlet核经典计算训练目标，避免了指数级模拟瓶颈。在IBM Quantum硬件上对H₂分子进行四阶段实验验证，采用线性纠缠器拓扑和单层VQC，结合动态解耦技术，成功在化学精度阈值内恢复基态能量，为NISQ设备上可扩展的QPE分子能量估计提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPE是分子基态能量估计的基础算法，但其深度电路要求在NISQ设备上无法直接执行。现有代理方法存在指数级模拟瓶颈，亟需一种可扩展、硬件高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出解析变分代理框架：通过Dirichlet核直接从FCI基态能量、辅助量子比特数和时间演化参数经典计算训练目标，训练浅层VQC以匹配QPE测量分布。采用RY-RZ-CZ ansatz，在IBM硬件上进行四阶段实验：比较拓扑结构、优化层数、验证降阶ansatz，并辅以噪声分析。

Result: 线性纠缠器拓扑优于全纠缠器；单层VQC在硬件噪声下最优；线性纠缠器+单层配置在化学精度（1 kcal/mol）内恢复基态能量；深度依赖的DD有效性分析揭示了电路深度与解耦效果的权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了QPE的忠实模仿，VQC复杂度线性扩展，避免了指数级模拟开销，为NISQ设备上可扩展的分子能量估计提供了硬件高效的新范式。

Abstract: Quantum Phase Estimation (QPE) is a foundational algorithm for molecular ground-state energy estimation, but its deep circuit requirements make direct hardware execution impractical on Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. We present an analytically grounded variational surrogate framework in which a shallow Variational Quantum Circuit (VQC) is trained to reproduce the QPE measurement distribution without any quantum circuit simulation. The training target is computed entirely classically via the Dirichlet kernel, evaluated directly from the Full Configuration Interaction (FCI) ground-state energy, the ancilla qubit count, and the time evolution parameter, eliminating the exponentially scaling simulation bottleneck of prior surrogate approaches. We apply this framework to the hydrogen molecule (H$_2$) with a symmetry-tapered Hamiltonian, conducting a four-stage experimental investigation on IBM Quantum hardware. Stage 1 compares linear and full entangler topologies for the $R_Y$-$R_Z$-$CZ$ ansatz, with and without XpXm Dynamical Decoupling (DD), across four distributional metrics (Hellinger distance, fidelity error, total variation distance, Jensen-Shannon divergence), identifying the linear entangler as optimal. Stage 2 varies VQC layers ($p=1$ to $5$) for the linear-entangler ansatz, identifying single-layer depth as optimal under hardware noise. Stage 3 applies this configuration to the reduced $R_Y$-$CZ$ ansatz, comparing ideal and noisy simulator-trained parameters. A supplementary noise analysis at $p \in \{8,64\}$ characterizes the depth-dependent interplay between circuit depth and DD effectiveness. The framework enables faithful QPE mimicry using a linearly scaling VQC, recovering the ground-state energy within the chemical accuracy threshold (1 kcal/mol), constituting a scalable, hardware-efficient paradigm for QPE-based molecular energy estimation on NISQ devices.

</details>


### [156] [Large deviations in quantum dynamics and complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20959)
*Xiangyu Cao,Jorge Kurchan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究多体量子动力学中大偏差的三种定义，发现无守恒律的通用系统中，定义(i)在O(1)时间内达到长期极限，而(ii)和(iii)分别在指数和双指数时间内达到极限，且存在探索与未探索结果之间的尖锐前沿，其漂移速度可作为量子复杂度的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体量子动力学中不同大偏差定义下的弛豫时间尺度，以及这些时间尺度与系统规模、量子复杂度的关系。

Method: 通过对比三种大偏差定义的长时间极限行为：(i)可观测量的完整分布，(ii)连续监测下测量结果的分布，(iii)期望值的分布。分析无守恒律通用系统中的时间标度行为。

Result: (i)在O(1)时间内达到长期极限，与系统大小N无关；(ii)需要指数时间t~e^N；(iii)需要双指数时间t~exp(e^N)。在达到极限前，分布存在尖锐前沿，将已探索与未探索区域分开。

Conclusion: 不同大偏差定义下的收敛时间标度差异巨大，未探索区域边界随时间的演化可作为量子复杂度的度量。

Abstract: We study three definitions of large deviation in many-body quantum dynamics: (i) via the full distribution of an extensive observable, (ii) via the distribution of measurement outcomes (from a continuous monitoring of the observable) over a time interval $t \le t_{\max}$, and (iii) via the distribution of expectation values over $t \le t_{\max}$. In generic systems without conservation laws, the large deviation function (i) reaches its longtime limit at $t \sim \mathcal{O}(1)$, independently of system size $N$. (ii) and (iii) reach their longtime limit at $t \sim e^{ N}$ and $t \sim \exp(e^{N})$, respectively. Before that, there is a {\it sharp} frontier between the explored and unexplored outcomes/expectation values; their distribution equals the longtime limit truncated at values that drift with $t_{\max}$. We propose that the evolution of these values with $t_{\max}$ provides a measure of quantum complexity.

</details>


### [157] [Compact deterministic liquid-crystal polarization controller with single-measurement direct control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20987)
*Ren-De Liu,Jie Wang,Dong-Dong Li,Xian-Chang Du,Dao-Zhong Xiao,shuai Li,Chuan-Yang Ding,Yan-Lin Tang,Yang Li,Sheng-Kai Liao,Teng-Yun Chen,Shi-Biao Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于液晶的紧凑型确定性偏振控制器（LC-PC），具有低插入损耗（<1 dB）、低驱动电压（<6 V）和全覆盖庞加莱球能力，并配合单次测量直接计算的控制算法，角误差低于2°，为偏振敏感光子系统提供了高效实用的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏振控制器在不同性能指标之间存在权衡，且控制算法要么收敛效率低，要么需要多次偏振态测量，缺乏兼具优良综合性能与高效控制方案的偏振管理器件。

Method: 设计了一种紧凑型确定性液晶偏振控制器，其偏振调制具有高度可预测和可重复的特性，表现为与偏振态无关的轨迹法向量；基于此特性提出了单次测量直接计算的控制算法。

Result: 该LC-PC实现了插入损耗<1 dB、驱动电压<6 V、全覆盖庞加莱球；控制算法角误差低于2°。

Conclusion: 该紧凑型确定性LC-PC及其高效控制方案为量子密钥分发、光学传感及先进光子系统中的偏振管理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Polarization controllers (PCs) and their control algorithms are critical for compensating environmental disturbances and maintaining signal fidelity in polarization-sensitive photonic systems. However, existing controllers often involve trade-offs among key performance metrics, while associated control algorithms either converge inefficiently or require multiple state-of-polarization (SOP) measurements. This work presents a compact deterministic liquid-crystal polarization controller (LC-PC) with balanced overall performance, featuring low insertion loss (<1 dB), low driving voltage (<6 V), and full Poincaré sphere coverage. The device exhibits highly predictable and repeatable polarization modulation characterized by SOP-independent trajectory normal vectors, enabling a single-measurement direct-calculation algorithm with an angular error below 2°. The combination of this compact deterministic LC-PC and its efficient control scheme provides a practical solution for polarization management in quantum key distribution, optical sensing, and advanced photonic systems.

</details>


### [158] [A Quantum Interface Between Neutral-Atoms and Trapped-Ions Quantum Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20998)
*Ayelet Hasson,Gal Dekel,Ehud Shahar,Nitzan Akerman,Roee Ozeri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种基于中性原子（88Sr）与 trapped 离子（88Sr+）晶体的混合量子界面方案，通过将原子激发到里德堡态增强原子-离子极化相互作用，实现离子集体运动模式的状态依赖调制，进而使中性原子作为控制量子比特对离子间的MS门进行条件控制。研究分析了里德堡态在光镊和Paul阱组合势中的俘获稳定性、寿命及相干性要求，表明该方案在现实实验参数下可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将中性原子阵列的可扩展性与 trapped 离子平台的高保真度控制能力相结合，构建混合量子系统，实现确定性的原子-离子混合量子门和量子接口。

Method: 提出中性原子与离子晶体的量子接口方案：将光镊中的88Sr中性原子激发到里德堡态，增强原子-离子极化相互作用，从而状态依赖地调制 trapped 离子晶体的集体运动模式，实现对MS门的条件控制。分析了负极化率里德堡态在光镊与Paul阱组合势中的稳定俘获条件、里德堡态寿命及相干性要求。

Result: 该方案能在现实实验参数下实现中性原子作为控制量子比特、离子作为目标量子比特的纠缠操作；负极化率里德堡态在组合势中具有稳定的约束特性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了实现确定性的原子-离子混合量子门和量子接口的实用路径，为结合两类量子平台优势提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum systems combining neutral atoms and trapped ions offer the prospect of integrating the scalability of atom arrays with the high-fidelity control available in trapped-ion platforms. Here we propose and analyze a quantum interface between individually trapped neutral atoms and a trapped-ion crystal. In our scheme, a neutral 88Sr atom trapped in optical tweezers interacts with a small 88Sr+ ion crystal, and by exciting the atom to a Rydberg state, the atom-ion polarization interaction is strongly enhanced, resulting in a state-dependent modification of the ions' collective motional modes. We show that this shift enables conditional control of a MS gate, allowing the neutral atom to act as a control qubit for an entangling operation between two ions. We investigate the feasibility of the scheme by analyzing Rydberg trapping in the combined optical tweezers and Paul trap potentials, identifying negative-polarizability Rydberg states as particularly favorable for stable confinement. We further evaluate the relevant trapping conditions, Rydberg lifetimes, and coherence requirements, and show that the proposed interface is compatible with realistic experimental parameters. These results establish a practical route toward deterministic atom-ion hybrid quantum gates and quantum interfaces.

</details>


### [159] [On-chip Radio Frequency Maser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21002)
*Hongliang Wu,Zhengtao Wang,Yuchen Han,Liu Yang,Zhiwei Wang,Yeliang Wang,Dezhi Zheng,Bo Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在室温下实现了片上射频激射器，利用光泵浦并五苯的三重态，在106.62 MHz产生受激发射，实现约10 fT/√Hz的超灵敏微波磁场探测，同时作为本地振荡器和传感器，通过主动控制微波耗散实现输出调控，为便携式量子器件提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统室温固态激射器依赖笨重的金属谐振器，严重阻碍了集成化、小型化以及向更低频率的扩展。

Method: 利用光泵浦并五苯的三重态，采用片上集成架构，在无腔开放体系中通过主动控制微波耗散来调控激射输出。

Result: 在106.62 MHz实现受激发射，微波磁场探测灵敏度达到约10 fT/√Hz，同时具备本地振荡器和传感器功能。

Conclusion: 将并五苯基激射器扩展到射频波段，建立了高度集成的室温激射器片上架构，为便携式量子器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Room-temperature solid-state masers offer exceptional frequency selectivity and ultra-low noise for weak-signal detection. However, their reliance on bulky metallic resonators has significantly hindered integration, miniaturization, and extension to lower frequencies. Here, we demonstrate the first on-chip radio-frequency maser operating at room temperature, exploiting optically pumped triplet states of pentacene. The device produces stimulated emission at 106.62 MHz and enables ultra-sensitive microwave magnetic-field detection with a sensitivity of ($\sim 10\,\rm{fT/\sqrt{Hz}}$), functioning simultaneously as a local oscillator and a sensor. By actively controlling microwave dissipation, we achieve efficient regulation of the maser output, revealing a key mechanism for tuning emission in open cavity-free systems. This work extends pentacene-based masers into the radio-frequency regime and establishes a highly integrated on-chip architecture for room-temperature masers, offering a new pathway toward portable quantum devices.

</details>


### [160] [Vacuum-induced interference in light scattering by multilevel atomic chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21073)
*Aleksei Konovalov,Giovanna Morigi,Nicola Piovella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了具有平行偶极矩的双共振多能级原子链中的合作光散射，揭示了交叉阻尼和交叉位移对相干和非相干动力学的影响，以及多能级干涉与超辐射的相互作用在碱金属原子链中的可观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多能级原子链中真空诱导的偶极跃迁干涉（交叉阻尼和交叉位移）如何影响合作光散射的谱学特性，特别是在碱金属原子链中是否产生可测量的效应。

Method: 采用谐振子模型描述偶极跃迁，在相干散射极限下分析外加激光驱动的有序原子链的激发谱，研究多能级干涉与合作效应的结合。

Result: 多能级干涉与超辐射的相互作用在碱金属原子链中能够产生可测量的谱学效应。

Conclusion: 多能级原子链中的交叉阻尼和交叉位移显著影响光散射的相干与非相干动力学，并在碱金属原子体系中具有实验可观测性。

Abstract: We investigate cooperative light scattering by an ordered chain of multilevel atoms, which possess two quasi-resonant transitions with parallel dipole moments. Interference between dipole transitions induced by coupling with the vacuum gives rise to so called cross-damping and cross-shifts, that modify the incoherent and coherent dynamics and can be manifest in the spectroscopic properties of the emitted light. We determine the excitation spectrum when the atomic chain is driven by an external laser in the limit in which the dipolar transitions can be described by harmonic oscillators and the scattering is coherent. We show that the interplay of multilevel interference and superradiance can give rise to measurable effects in chains of alkali-metal atoms.

</details>


### [161] [Picosecond-resolved entanglement distribution over an urban free-space channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21093)
*Alessandro Laneve,Fabrizio Cienzo,Santiago Gomez,Paolo Barigelli,Philip Menz,Mattia Beccaceci,Giuseppe Ronco,Giorgia Grossi,Ievgen Brytavskyi,Thomas Oberleitner,Markus Wiener,Christian Weidinger,Tobias M. Krieger,Ailton Garcia,Saimon Filipe Covre da Silva,Michele B. Rota,Nicolò Spagnolo,Henning Weier,Fabio Sciarrino,Armando Rastelli,Rinaldo Trotta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在罗马市中心270米自由空间信道上成功演示了快速演化纠缠态的高保真分发，开发了同步精度优于50皮秒的系统，验证了噪声城市信道中时间演化纠缠的可靠传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间演化纠缠态的动力学时标常与同步精度相当，尤其在噪声城市信道分发纠缠时，精确计时是忠实观测和利用量子关联的基本要求，但目前这一潜力受到限制。

Method: 在罗马市中心两栋建筑间建立270米自由空间链路，集成同步装置实现亚50皮秒定时精度，同时支持信道稳定化。

Result: 成功演示了快速演化纠缠态在噪声城市自由空间信道上的可靠传输，实现了亚50皮秒的同步精度。

Conclusion: 该工作为长距离自由空间量子通信和未来基于卫星的量子网络中时间关联纠缠态的实现树立了重要基准。

Abstract: Time-evolving entangled states describe quantum particles whose correlations evolve in time according to a well-defined dynamics. Such states can be generated in a variety of physical systems and are promising resources for several quantum technologies, ranging from quantum clock synchronization to quantum communication. However, their full potential is currently limited by the fact that the entanglement dynamics often occur on timescales comparable to the achievable synchronization precision, especially in experiments aimed at distributing entanglement through noisy urban channels. In this context, accurate timing is not merely a technical detail, but a fundamental requirement for faithfully observing and exploiting the underlying quantum correlations. Here, we demonstrate the faithful distribution of a fast-evolving entangled state over a 270 m free-space channel connecting two buildings in the center of Rome. The developed system incorporates a synchronization device capable of achieving sub-50 ps timing accuracy between the two ends of the link while simultaneously supporting channel stabilization. Our results demonstrate that time-evolving entanglement can be reliably transmitted through a noisy urban free-space channel, representing an important benchmark toward long-distance free-space quantum communication and the future implementation of time-correlated entangled states in demanding scenarios such as satellite-based quantum networks.

</details>


### [162] [Conditional probabilities in quantum-optical settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21100)
*Alfredo Luis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个互补可观测量联合噪声测量导出的条件概率，发现条件化可以在一个变量上消除另一变量的不确定性，且条件分布通常无法用Born规则或标准态约简表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究从互补可观测量联合噪声测量中导出的条件概率的性质，特别是条件化对不确定性的影响，以及这些条件分布能否用标准的量子力学规则（Born规则、态约简）来描述。

Method: 从两个互补可观测量（如位置和动量）的联合噪声测量导出条件概率，分析其不确定性特征；检验条件分布能否用边缘POVM和系统态以Born形式表示；在双零差探测和量子比特测量两个基本量子光学方案中进行验证。
