Result: 条件化可以降低甚至完全消除另一变量的不确定性；条件分布一般无法用Born规则（边缘POVM与系统态）表示，也无法由标准态约简规则推导。

Conclusion: 互补可观测量联合噪声测量下的条件概率具有非经典特征，突破了传统Born规则和态约简的适用范围。

Abstract: We examine conditional probabilities derived from the joint noisy measurement of two complementary observables. We show that conditioning on one variable can lead to reduction of uncertainty in the other one, even completely eliminating uncertainty. We show that the conditional distribution cannot, in general, be represented in Born form using the marginal positive operator valued measure and any physical system state, nor can it be derived from typical rules of state reduction. We examine these issues in two basic quantum-optical schemes. These are double homodyne detection and a qubit measurement.

</details>


### [163] [Entanglement, Evolutionary Stability, and Strategy-Space Dependence in an EWL Quantum Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21103)
*Azhar Iqbal,Derek Abbott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究EWL量子化如何影响对称2×2博弈中的演化稳定性。在受限双参数EWL策略空间中，量子均衡成为严格对称纳什均衡（ESS）；但扩展到完整SU(2)三参数策略空间后，均衡失去严格性，ESS条件不成立。纠缠仅在受限策略空间内稳定均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨EWL量子化是否能使原本非演化稳定的混合纳什均衡变得演化稳定，并检验该效应在不同策略空间下的鲁棒性。

Method: 在对称2×2博弈中，分别考虑受限双参数EWL策略空间和完整三参数SU(2)策略空间，分析量子均衡的纳什均衡性质和演化稳定策略（ESS）条件。

Result: 在受限双参数EWL空间中，量子均衡s*=(π/2,π/4)对所有γ>0都是严格对称纳什均衡，因而是ESS。扩展到完整SU(2)空间后，该均衡仍是纳什均衡，但出现连续统的收益中性突变，导致严格性和第二个ESS条件失效。

Conclusion: 纠缠仅在受限EWL策略空间内能稳定均衡，该效应不能推广到完整SU(2)策略空间。

Abstract: We examine how Eisert--Wilkens--Lewenstein (EWL) quantization affects evolutionary stability in a symmetric $2\times2$ game whose interior mixed Nash equilibrium is not evolutionarily stable. In the restricted two-parameter EWL strategy space, the quantum equilibrium $s^*=(π/2,π/4)$ becomes a strict symmetric Nash equilibrium, and hence an evolutionarily stable strategy, for every $γ>0$. Extending the admissible pure strategies to the full three-parameter $SU(2)$ family preserves the Nash equilibrium but introduces a continuum of payoff-neutral mutants, causing strictness and the second ESS condition to fail. Thus, entanglement stabilizes the equilibrium only within the restricted EWL strategy space; the effect does not survive enlargement to full $SU(2)$.

</details>


### [164] [Approximate Quantum State Preparation Through Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21121)
*Marco Mordacci,Michele Amoretti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（PPO）的量子架构搜索框架，用于近似量子态制备（QSP），能在2-5量子比特上以$10^{-14}$的近似误差制备Bell、GHZ、W、Dicke及完全随机态，同时最小化门数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备（QSP）中，搜索空间随量子比特数指数增长，难以识别最优电路，亟需高效的自动化搜索方法。

Method: 采用基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习智能体，在每一步向电路中添加新门并重新计算近似态与目标态之间的保真度，目标是在最小化门数量的同时最大化近似精度。

Result: 在2-5量子比特上对Bell、GHZ、W、Dicke等预定义态及完全随机态进行实验，实现了$10^{-14}$的近似误差。

Conclusion: 该量子架构搜索框架能够高效自动地近似制备目标量子态，在精度和电路复杂度之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: In this work, a quantum architecture search framework for approximate quantum state preparation (QSP) is proposed. QSP is a challenging task, since the search space grows exponentially with the number of qubits, making the identification of the optimal circuit non-trivial. To address this problem, deep reinforcement learning is employed through an agent based on proximal policy optimization. The objective of the agent is to identify the best possible approximation of the target state while simultaneously minimizing the number of gates used. At each step, the agent appends a new gate to the circuit and recomputes the fidelity between the approximated state and the target states. Various experiments have been performed from 2 to 5 qubits. Both predefined states, such as Bell, GHZ, W, and Dicke states, and completely random states are considered. The proposed framework is able to achieve approximation errors of $10^{-14}$.

</details>


### [165] [Duality constrains optimal thresholds in quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21160)
*Lucas H. English,Haoyuan Luo,Yangming Wang,Basudha Srivastava,Stephen D. Bartlett,Dominic J. Williamson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在副本极限下，许多常见量子纠错码的最优纠错阈值被约束为单一的普适值。通过统计力学映射，展示了em对称CSS码（X和Z型奇偶校验矩阵在行列置换下等价）具有相同的零率最优码容量阈值。这种自对偶性固定了清洁临界点并约束了无序相边界，且在同级联码的推广中保持成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠错阈值常被视为比较不同量子纠错码家族的首要指标，但不同码族阈值的本质关系尚不明确。本文旨在揭示看似不同的码族在最优阈值上的深层统一性。

Method: 利用统计力学映射，将量子纠错问题转化为统计力学模型；通过广义Kramers-Wannier对偶性证明em对称CSS码是自对偶的；在零率码族极限下分析热力学体自由能的自对偶性；证明自对偶性在同级联码的编码构造中保持不变。

Result: 零率em对称CSS码在副本极限下具有相同的普适最优码容量阈值；自对偶性将清洁临界点固定至唯一值并约束了无序相边界；同级联码的最优解码可重构为分层晶格上的重正化群流。

Conclusion: 为拓扑码、级联码和更广泛的量子LDPC码家族提供了一个统一的分析框架，涵盖最优码容量阈值和亚阈值逻辑错误抑制。

Abstract: Error correction thresholds are often treated as the primary figure of merit for comparing quantum error-correcting code families. We show that the optimal error correction threshold for many commonly considered codes is constrained to a single universal value at leading order in a replica limit. Through a statistical mechanical mapping, we demonstrate that duality constrains all zero-rate em-symmetric CSS codes to have the same optimal code capacity threshold. Here, em symmetry means that the X- and Z-type parity-check matrices are equivalent up to row and column permutations. Under this statistical mechanical mapping, em-symmetric CSS codes are self-dual under a generalized Kramers-Wannier duality up to a mixing of logical sectors. For zero-rate code families, this mixing contributes only subextensive corrections, so the thermodynamic bulk free energy is self-dual in the trivial logical sector. This self-duality fixes the clean critical point and constrains the disordered phase boundary. We also show that self-duality is preserved under code concatenation, and that optimal decoding of concatenated codes can be reformulated as a renormalization group flow on a hierarchical lattice. Our results provide a common framework for analyzing topological, concatenated, and more general quantum low-density parity-check code families, including both their optimal code capacity thresholds and their sub-threshold logical error suppression.

</details>


### [166] [Characterising extremal decoherence by quantum measurement incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21169)
*Daniel McNulty,William Townsend,Jukka Kiukas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Heisenberg绘景下研究退相干如何将不可兼容测量转化为可联合测量，通过定义噪声信道的"兼容区域"来量化不可兼容性的丧失，并发现对称信息完全测量（SIC）能够最大化兼容体积，从而在联合可测量性框架下为SIC的特殊角色提供了新的操作特征化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究通常从态绘景（密度矩阵阻尼）或相干性量化角度描述退相干，缺乏从观测层面（可观测量的联合可测量性）对退相干导致经典涌现的精细化描述。本文旨在建立一种基于联合可测量性的、更精细的操作框架来比较不同噪声信道。

Method: 将退相干信道建模为与单位对角正半定矩阵凸集极值点的Schur乘积；利用极值点消去凸混合，揭示相位相关退相干效应；通过秩一算子框架将联合可测量性简化为正定性检验，从而解析求解兼容区域并计算其体积；将框架与MIC POVM和SIC POVM关联，在概率表示（Qbism框架）下赋予几何解释。

Result: 极值退相干信道具有刚性的膨胀结构，由秩一算子框架描述，兼容区域可通过框架的Gram矩阵解析计算；在最大秩极值信道中，SIC POVM对应的信道最大化兼容体积，即破坏最多不可兼容性；该问题可转化为噪声互 unbiased 基的联合可测量性问题，从而将SIC存在性问题重新表述。

Conclusion: 建立了基于联合可测量性的退相干信道比较新框架，给出了SIC POVM在操作层面的新特征化——最大程度破坏不可兼容性，并将著名的SIC存在性问题转化为联合可测量性问题，为量子基础中的经典涌现和测量兼容性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Decoherence, when viewed in the Heisenberg picture, can turn incompatible measurements into jointly measurable ones. This provides an observable-level description of emergent classicality in terms of the incompatibility destroyed by a noise channel. The corresponding loss of incompatibility can be captured by the channel's \emph{compatibility region}---the observables in a probe class that become jointly measurable with every noisy observable. The geometry and volume of this region provide a refined operational way to compare noise channels. We apply this framework to decoherence channels, each of which acts by Schur multiplication with extreme points of the convex set of unit-diagonal positive semidefinite matrices. Restricting to extremals eliminates convex mixing and reveals subtle phase-dependent decoherence effects thar are not captured by damping rates or conventional coherence quantifiers. Such channels have a rigid dilation structure described by a rank-one operator frame, which reduces joint measurability to a positivity test, making the compatibility region analytically tractable, and its volume computable from the frame's Gram matrix. If the frame can be normalised to a minimal informationally complete (MIC) POVM, the compatibility region acquires a geometric interpretation in the associated probability representation of the quantum state space, familiar with Qbism. Among maximal-rank extremals, those associated with symmetric informationally complete (SIC) POVMs maximise the compatibility volume and hence destroy the greatest amount of incompatibility, providing an operational characterisation of the special role of SICs in terms of joint measurability. This leads to a new formulation of the well-known SIC existence problem as a joint measurability question for noisy mutually unbiased bases.

</details>


### [167] [Basis-independent coherence and quantum correlations in two dipole-dipole-coupled electrons in double quantum-dot molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21175)
*Zakaria Bouafia,Neha Pathania,Mostafa Mansour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个偶极-偶极耦合电子在空间分离的量子点分子中的热动力学，发现偶极耦合强度K是最有效的保护参数，可延长纠缠突然死亡温度、增强集体相干性；库仑排斥J通过将热态投影到纠缠子空间来增强这种保护；能量失谐ε增强局域相干但加速纠缠死亡；量子点间隧穿Γ增强低温局域叠加但削弱集体相干和纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于量子点分子中偶极-偶极耦合电子的热动力学行为，分析不同形式的量子相干性和量子关联在温度影响下的表现，寻找保护量子资源的最佳参数条件。

Method: 采用局域相干性和集体相干性表征单点量子叠加和子系统间相干性；使用Bures距离纠缠、局域量子不确定度(LQU)和局域量子Fisher信息(LQFI)量化两个双量子点之间的量子关联；通过改变偶极耦合强度K、库仑排斥J、能量失谐ε和量子点间隧穿Γ等参数进行热动力学分析。

Result: 偶极-偶极耦合K是最有效的保护参数，可延长纠缠突然死亡温度、增强集体相干性；库仑排斥J通过将热态投影到纠缠子空间来增强保护；能量失谐ε增强局域相干但加速纠缠死亡并抑制LQU和LQFI；量子点间隧穿Γ增强低温局域叠加但削弱集体相干性和纠缠。

Conclusion: 偶极-偶极耦合和库仑排斥的联合作用对保护量子资源最为有效，而能量失谐和量子点间隧穿对量子关联有不同程度的有害影响，为量子点量子信息处理中的热噪声管理提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This work examines the thermal dynamics of basis-independent quantum coherence and correlation-based quantum resources for two dipole-dipole-coupled electrons confined in spatially separated quantum-dot (QD) molecules. Single-dot quantum superpositions and inter-subsystem coherence are characterized by using localized and collective coherence. Quantum correlations between the two double quantum dots are quantified by employing Bures distance entanglement, Local quantum uncertainty (LQU), and local quantum Fisher Information (LQFI). The findings show that dipole-dipole coupling $K$ is the most effective protective parameter, extending the entanglement sudden death temperature, diminishing the local quantum superpositions and enhancing the collective coherence. The dipole-dipole interaction has also a crucial impact on protecting LQU and LQFI beyond the entanglement sudden death temperature. Coulomb repulsion $J$ reinforces this protection through an independent channel, projecting the thermal state onto the entangled $\{|0_A 1_B\rangle,|1_A 0_B\rangle\}$ subspace; their combined action is required to approach the entanglement maximum, and it enhances collective coherence and extends the temperature range over which LQU and LQFI remain appreciable. Energy detuning $\varepsilon$ can enhance localized coherence but paradoxically accelerates the entanglement sudden death and quenches LQU and LQFI by weakening two-body correlations. Inter-dot tunneling $Γ$ enhances local superpositions at low temperature, but it reduces collective coherence, lowers the entanglement sudden death temperature, and disrupts other nonclassical correlations.

</details>


### [168] [Do emulated quantum circuits change what CNNs look at? Performance and explainability comparison in medical image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21186)
*Guillermo Rubiños Rodríguez,Martín Ottavianelli,Mateo Alonso,Gonzalo Blázquez Gil,Boris-Stephan Rauchmann,Pablo Díez-Valle,Sergio Altares-López*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了混合量子启发式卷积神经网络（HQiCNN）与参数匹配的传统CNN在医学图像分类任务上的表现。结果显示，没有一种架构持续占优：HQiCNN在中等数据规模下表现更好，而CNN在最大训练集下精度更高。去除纠缠能提升量子模拟的可扩展性且性能相当，丰富可观测量集仅在数据充足时有益。此外，提出了两种基于SHAP的可解释性工具来比较模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究小规模、经典模拟的量子电路组件能否在复杂模型中发挥有意义的作用，作为纯经典卷积架构的替代方案。

Method: 提出混合量子启发式卷积神经网络（HQiCNN），与参数匹配的传统CNN进行系统比较，在两种真实医学数据集上变化多种超参数，确保比较的数据集独立性和超参数独立性。同时提出两种基于SHAP的可解释性指标（|SHAP|IoU和EMD_pos）用于比较模型预测。

Result: 1）无架构持续占优；2）HQiCNN在中等数据规模下增益最大，CNN在大训练集下精度最高；3）去除纠缠后性能相当但量子模拟可扩展性显著提升；4）丰富可观测量集仅在训练数据充足时有益；5）两种模型均能关注到解剖学合理区域。

Conclusion: 在特定条件下，混合量子启发式模型可作为传统CNN的有效替代方案，在某些实际任务（如医学图像分类）中能提供益处。

Abstract: Numerous studies have analyzed the use of hybrid quantum-classical convolutional neural networks as a promising alternative to classical deep learning. However, network components on quantum hardware impose fundamental limitations, while the scalability of quantum circuits leads to trainability issues. In this work, we investigate whether small, classically-emulated quantum circuit components can play a meaningful role within complex models, offering an alternative to purely classical convolutional architectures. To this end, we present a systematic study of the effectiveness of a Hybrid Quantum-inspired Convolutional Neural Network (HQiCNN) compared with a parameter-matched classical Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) that differs only in an intermediate dense neural layer. Both models are evaluated on two real-world medical datasets while systematically varying the different hyperparameters, ensuring a fair model comparison that is both dataset and hyperparameter independent. The results show that no architecture consistently dominates the other: the HQiCNN achieves its largest gains in intermediate-data regimes, whereas the CNN reaches the highest accuracies for the largest training sets in both datasets. Furthermore, removing entanglement produces comparable performance while enabling substantially better scalability of quantum simulations, and richer observable sets become beneficial only when sufficient training data are available. Finally, we propose two SHAP-based explainability tools for comparing the predictions between both models, $|SHAP|$IoU and $EMD_{pos}$ metric, to demonstrate that both architectures consistently attend to anatomically plausible regions. Thus, we provide a comprehensive benchmark showing that, under certain conditions, hybrid quantum-inspired models are an alternative that can offer benefits in practical tasks such as medical image classification.

</details>


### [169] [Quantum Circuit Fragments and Link Products in Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21215)
*Amalina Lai,Graeme D. Berk,Minjeong Song,Mile Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子链接积（link product）框架从有限维度扩展到连续变量系统，建立了连续变量电路片段的组合理论，并展示了该形式体系在高斯框架下可利用协方差矩阵高效计算，为自适应传感、非马尔可夫噪声抑制和高阶量子电路设计提供了系统化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子电路通常被描述为完整过程，具有固定的输入和输出；然而许多量子信息任务中自然出现的是电路片段（如非马尔可夫噪声源、子程序或自适应策略代理），缺乏系统化的组合分析框架。有限维度下的链接积提供了分析电路片段交互与组合的手段，但连续变量系统尚缺乏相应框架。

Method: 1. 引入连续变量电路片段的概念及其对应的链接积，实现片段之间的拼接组合。2. 在高斯框架下，利用协方差矩阵表示进行高效计算，大幅简化形式体系。3. 利用该形式体系从模块化组件构建非马尔可夫过程与自适应代理-环境交互。

Result: 成功将量子组合（quantum comb）框架扩展到连续变量领域，建立了连续变量电路片段的链接积形式体系，并证明高斯框架下可通过协方差矩阵高效实现链接积计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续变量系统提供了类似于有限维度量子组合的系统化分析工具，适用于自适应传感、非马尔可夫噪声抑制和高阶量子电路设计等任务。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are often drawn as complete processes, with fixed inputs and outputs. In many quantum-information tasks, however, the natural object is only a fragment of such a circuit: an unknown source of non-Markovian noise to be probed, a subroutine to be inserted into a larger algorithm, or an agent implementing an adaptive strategy. In finite dimensions, the link product provides a systematic means to analyze how such circuit fragments interact and compose. Here, we develop the corresponding framework for continuous-variable systems. We introduce continuous-variable circuit fragments and associated link products that stitch such fragments together into larger processes. We show that the formalism simplifies substantially in the Gaussian regime, where link products can be evaluated efficiently using covariance-matrix representations. We use the formalism to construct non-Markovian processes and adaptive agent-environment interactions from modular components. This extends the quantum-comb toolkit to continuous variables, providing systematic methods for adaptive sensing, non-Markovian noise mitigation, and higher-order quantum circuit design.

</details>


### [170] [Why Some Quantum States Cannot Be Recovered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21240)
*Yuan Liu,Linhan Lin,Ke-Mi Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现量子信息不可恢复性的代数根源——"鬼信息"（全局态中编码但不在任何可访问子系统留下痕迹的关联），引入标量度量其大小，证明存在普适误差下界，并揭示采样成本中的谱相变，将所有多体量子态分为四类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子系统丢失后量子信息恢复是量子信息处理的核心挑战，但某些态超出了任何恢复策略的能力范围，需要从代数根源上理解不可恢复性。

Method: 通过识别"鬼信息"作为不可恢复性的代数起源，引入标量度量；分析虚拟恢复映射的误差下界；研究采样成本中的谱相变（有谱隙时的有界行为与无谱隙时的发散行为）。

Result: 证明存在普适误差下界，任何虚拟恢复映射无法突破；发现采样成本的谱相变，可量化不可恢复性，并将所有多体量子态分为四类；揭示条件互信息与虚拟可恢复性根本无关。

Conclusion: 该误差下界为容损量子计量学设定了探测阈值，对量子信息处理中恢复策略的极限给出了根本性约束。

Abstract: The recovery of quantum information after subsystem loss is a central challenge in quantum information processing. However, some states remain beyond the reach of any recovery strategies. Here we identify the algebraic origin of irrecoverability, the ghost information---correlations encoded in the global state that leave no trace on any accessible subsystem. We introduce a scalar measure quantifying its magnitude and prove a universal error floor below which no virtual recovery map can operate, irrespective of resource investment. We further uncover a spectral phase transition in the sampling cost: bounded when the underlying linear map exhibits a spectrum gap, and divergent with a universal exponent in the gapless regime. Together with the universal error floor, this dichotomy organizes all multipartite quantum states into four classes. Moreover, it is revealed that conditional mutual information---the standard entropic diagnostic---is fundamentally irrelevant to virtual recoverability. As an implication, we show that the error floor imposes a detection threshold for loss-tolerant quantum metrology.

</details>


### [171] [Space-division multiplexed quantum key distribution exploiting multi-plane light conversion for few-mode fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21256)
*Qian Zhang,Stefan Krause,Felix Kunzmann,Juergen W Czarske*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了基于多平面光转换（MPLC）的少模光纤（FMF）空间复用BBM92量子密钥分发方案，通过解复用纠缠光子实现并行量子信道，两个信道的量子比特误码率分别为1.9%和6.8%，为可扩展量子安全网络和多用户纠缠分发提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发从实验室走向实际部署，量子通信网络需要更高的密钥速率以及在多个用户和网络节点间分发独立秘密密钥的能力，现有方案在并行量子信道的实现上存在挑战。

Method: 采用多平面光转换（MPLC）技术对通过少模光纤（FMF）传输的纠缠光子进行解复用，通过独立的单模光纤选择性激发模式，再由MPLC分离至不同输出端口，实现空间复用的BBM92 QKD方案。

Result: 实验获得两个复用信道的量子比特误码率：信道1为1.9±0.4%，信道2为6.8±0.8%。

Conclusion: 该方案在保持QKD所需纠缠特性的前提下，利用可区分导模作为并行信道，适用于可扩展量子安全网络、空分复用QKD系统、多用户纠缠分发及未来量子互联网架构。

Abstract: As quantum key distribution (QKD) progresses from laboratory demonstrations toward practical deployment, quantum communi- cation networks increasingly require higher key rates and the ability to distribute independent secret keys among multiple users and network nodes. In this paper we present a promsing approach with multi-plane light conversion (MPLC) for demultiplexing entangled photons, transmitted through a few-mode fiber (FMF). We experimentally demonstrated a spatially multiplexed BBM92 QKD scheme. The modes are selectively excited through separate single-mode-fibers and subsequently separated by MPLC into distinct output ports. Unlike high-dimensional QKD based on coherent modal superpositions, our approach exploits distinguishable guided modes as parallel channels while preserving the entanglement required for QKD. For the multiplexed links, we obtain quantum bit error rates of $1.9 \pm 0.4\%$ for the channel 1 and $6.8 \pm 0.8\%$ for the channel 2. These results are relevant for scalable quantum-secured networks, space-division-multiplexed QKD systems, multi-user entanglement distribution, and future quantum internet architectures, where parallel quantum channels must be implemented without compromising the quantum correlations required for security.

</details>


### [172] [Mixed-Binary Quadratic Programming via QUBO Sampling without Continuous-Variable Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21286)
*Taisei Takabayashi,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对可分离混合二元二次规划(MBQP)的有限温度公式，通过解析积分连续变量，避免离散化，仅对原始二元变量求解QUBO问题，在二次p-中位问题上的实验表明该方法比基于惩罚的QUBO公式更可靠地生成可行解，且条件相对误差与局部搜索相当或更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火等组合优化方法通常以QUBO问题为输入，但实际模型常包含约束和连续变量。标准的QUBO转换需要离散化连续变量，这不仅增加了二元维度，还使得可行低能态更难采样。因此，需要一种避免离散化的方法来处理混合整数二次规划问题。

Method: 基于有限温度公式，针对可分离类混合二元二次规划(MBQP)，在固定拉格朗日乘子的条件下，对连续变量部分进行解析积分，将其仅纳入乘子更新过程，最终得到一个仅包含原始二元变量的QUBO问题。

Result: 在二次p-中位问题的连续松弛上，与基于惩罚的QUBO公式相比，该方法更可靠地生成可行解。在适当的逆温度下，其条件相对误差在小规模实例中与局部搜索相当，在较大规模实例中通常更低。在到达目标时间的实验中，该方法在测试范围的上端比商业混合整数优化求解器更快达到目标。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限温度公式能有效避免连续变量离散化带来的维度灾难，更可靠地生成可行解，在较大规模问题上具有速度和精度优势，为混合整数二次规划与量子退火的结合提供了一种新途径。

Abstract: Quantum annealing and related combinatorial optimization methods typically accept quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problems as input, whereas many practical models include constraints and continuous variables. Standard QUBO conversions discretize continuous variables, increasing the binary dimension and often making feasible low-energy states harder to sample. We develop a finite-temperature formulation for a separable class of mixed-binary quadratic programs (MBQPs) that avoids this discretization. At fixed Lagrange multipliers, the continuous sector is integrated out analytically and enters only the multiplier update, leaving a QUBO over the original binary variables. We evaluate the method on the continuous relaxation of the quadratic $p$-median problem. Compared with a penalty-based QUBO formulation, it generates feasible solutions more reliably. At an appropriate inverse temperature, its conditional relative error is comparable to that of local search for small instances and often lower for the larger tested instances. In the time-to-target experiment, it also reaches the target faster than a commercial mixed-integer optimization solver toward the upper end of the tested range.

</details>


### [173] [Device-Independent Self-Testing of the Three-Qubit CCZ Hypergraph State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21288)
*Yunguang Han,Xin Kuang,Xingyuan Bu,Yukun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提供了两种设备无关的自测试方案来认证三量子比特CCZ态及其局域测量：一是通过五个输入三元组中的二十个关联子确定态和Pauli X/Z测量（无需最大贝尔不等式违背），二是构造了一个新的贝尔不等式，其最大量子违背可自测试CCZ态及所有三个局域测量，并通过平方和分解给出了解析的SWAP提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCZ态是最小的秩三超图态，其广义稳定子不是Pauli字符串，因此标准图态自测试论证不直接适用。现有方法无法直接对CCZ态进行设备无关的自测试认证，需要发展新的自测试技术。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 利用三方两输入两输出场景中五个全局输入三元组的二十个关联子来固定八条等权重计算分支，通过传播条件X翻转关系恢复111振幅的负号，从而确定态和测量；(2) 引入第三个独立测量并构造新的贝尔不等式，利用平方和分解证明量子界，通过等式条件得到解析的SWAP提取。

Result: 展示了五个上下文的关联子看似非局域，但标准的Pauli测量在最大化贝尔表达式时其局域界与量子值相同；通过引入第三个独立测量实现了从最大贝尔违背进行自测试；构造的贝尔不等式的最大量子违背可以自测试CCZ态和所有三个局域测量。

Conclusion: 通过多个关联子等式确定态与测量，与通过单个贝尔不等式的最大违背来识别态与测量是两种不同的自测试路径，本文同时给出了CCZ态的这两种自测试方案。

Abstract: The three-qubit CCZ state is the smallest rank-three hypergraph state and an elementary entangled magic resource. Its cubic phase is governed by generalized stabilizers that are not Pauli strings, so standard graph-state self-testing arguments do not apply directly. We show that twenty correlators, all obtainable from five of the eight global input triples in the tripartite two-input, two-output scenario, determine this state and the action of the Pauli $X/Z$ measurements up to local isometries. The proof fixes eight equally weighted computational branches and propagates conditional $X$-flip relations across the branch cube, recovering the minus sign of the $111$ amplitude. These five-context correlations are nonlocal, but the canonical Pauli measurements cannot attain the largest quantum value of any Bell inequality that they violate: whenever they maximize a Bell expression, its local bound has the same value. Introducing an independent third measurement makes self-testing from maximal Bell violation possible. We construct an explicit Bell inequality whose maximal quantum violation self-tests the CCZ state and all three local measurements. An exact sum-of-squares decomposition proves the quantum bound, and its equality conditions yield an analytic SWAP extraction. Together, these results give two explicit device-independent self-tests of the CCZ state and demonstrate that determining a state and its measurements from several correlator equalities is distinct from identifying them through the maximal violation of a single Bell inequality.

</details>


### [174] [Classifying Topology via Edge-State Pure Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21299)
*Fatih Ozaydin,Haydar Sahin,Armando Perez-Leija,Azmi Ali Altintas,Cihan Bayindir,Ozgur E. Mustecaplioglu,Sahin K. Ozdemir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示拓扑边缘态可作为纯热化燃料，在重复交互体系中通过SSH链边缘态抑制位移和压缩，驱动腔体进入Gibbs态，而体态则激活相干通道产生热机械操作，建立拓扑-热力学关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拓扑结构能否控制重复交互机器中热类资源与功类资源的区分，即拓扑结构在热力学过程中的角色未知。

Method: 以开放Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链作为微脉泽的燃料源，分析边缘态与体态对腔体态的影响；并在超导系统中将16位SSH本征态压缩为4量子比特燃料寄存器。

Result: 边缘本征态抑制位移和压缩，驱动腔体到Gibbs态（纯热化）；体态激活相干通道产生热机械操作；该边缘-体态热力学二分法在退相干、腔损耗、键无序及中等在位无序下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 拓扑边缘态可作为纯热化燃料，腔体响应可作为无需输运的拓扑分类器，建立了拓扑-热力学联系，可推广至更广泛的重复交互场景。

Abstract: Repeated-interaction machines distinguish heat-like from work-like resources through the steady states they generate, but whether topology can control this distinction remains unknown. Here we reveal the role of topology in the process by showing that topological edge states can act as pure-thermalization fuels. For an open Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain used as the fuel source of a micromaser, edge eigenstates suppress both displacement and squeezing and drive the cavity to a Gibbs state, whereas bulk eigenstates activate coherent channels and yield thermo-mechanical operation. This edge-bulk thermodynamic dichotomy remains robust under realistic decoherence, cavity loss, bond disorder, and moderate onsite disorder. We further design a superconducting implementation in which a sixteen-site SSH eigenstate is deterministically compressed into a four-qubit fuel register. The resulting cavity response provides a transport-free classifier of topology and identifies a topology-thermodynamics link that extends beyond cavity-QED to repeated-interaction settings more generally.

</details>


### [175] [Quasiparticle-induced transitions in a fluxonium qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21329)
*Maksim Litskevich,Kesavan Manivannan,Benjamin Byrd,Pavel D. Kurilovich,Vladislav D. Kurilovich,Gianluigi Catelani,Ivan V. Pechenezhskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过测量通量量子比特在可控准粒子注入下的激发和退激发速率，发现需要计入约瑟夫森结两侧超导能隙不对称性才能准确建模准粒子诱导跃迁速率与外加磁通的关系，并由此约束了阵列结与小结中准粒子的相对贡献，解释了此前两类元件准粒子密度推断不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子是超导量子比特中重要的退相干源，但在通量量子比特中难以隔离其效应——通量量子比特不像transmon那样对偏移电荷敏感，因此无法通过电荷-宇称检测来探测准粒子隧穿。

Method: 在可控片上准粒子注入条件下，测量通量量子比特的激发和退激发速率；将准粒子诱导跃迁速率与外加磁通的关系与理论模型进行对比。

Result: 发现必须考虑约瑟夫森结两侧超导能隙的不对称性，才能准确建模准粒子诱导跃迁速率对磁通的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该模型约束了阵列结和小结中准粒子的相对贡献，帮助解释了此前这两种电路元件准粒子密度推断不一致的问题。

Abstract: Quasiparticles are a prominent decoherence source in superconducting qubits, but their effects are notoriously difficult to isolate in fluxonium. Unlike a transmon, fluxonium is insensitive to offset charge, precluding charge-parity detection of quasiparticle tunneling. We address this challenge by measuring the excitation and de-excitation rates in a fluxonium qubit under controlled on-chip quasiparticle injection. We show that to accurately model the external magnetic flux dependence of the quasiparticle-induced transition rates, it is necessary to account for the superconducting gap asymmetry across the Josephson junctions. A comparison between theory and experiment constrains the relative quasiparticle contributions of the junction array and the small junction and helps explain previously reported discrepancies between the bounds on the quasiparticle densities inferred for these two circuit elements.

</details>


### [176] [Efficient classical simulation of large-scale unitary cluster Jastrow circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21337)
*Hrishikesh Belagali,Thomas Van Camp,R. Pradeep,Sourin Das,Namit Anand,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种经典多项式时间算法，能够计算任意单层 unitary cluster Jastrow 电路的能量，无需考虑量子硬件的局域性限制。该算法可在笔记本电脑上不到一分钟内复现 Sci. Adv. 11, 25 (2025) 中 77 量子比特、10,570 门的大型量子实验，并通过快速模拟进行电路优化，获得比原实验更低的基态能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子计算机在化学模拟领域取得了突破性进展，尤其是使用 unitary cluster Jastrow 电路在 77 量子比特规模上实现了超越 Hartree-Fock 的基态能量计算，但这些实验依赖昂贵的量子硬件和超算进行经典后处理。本文旨在证明该类量子优势可以被经典算法在多项式时间内复现甚至超越。

Method: 提出一种多项式时间经典算法，专门针对单层 unitary cluster Jastrow 电路进行能量计算，无需受量子硬件局域性约束。该算法利用电路结构的可化简性，实现高效经典模拟，并支持快速电路优化。

Result: 在笔记本电脑上不到一分钟即可复现原实验（77 量子比特、10,570 门）的结果；通过算法辅助的电路优化，获得了比原实验更低的基态能量。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，当前量子计算在化学模拟领域展示的某些"量子优势"可能并非真正无法被经典方法超越，经典算法通过针对性设计仍可高效模拟特定类型的量子电路。

Abstract: Recent experiments on quantum computers have challenged the limits of classical computation in chemistry, simulating ground states of strongly correlated molecules. Many of these experiments have utilized the unitary cluster Jastrow ansatz, a quantum circuit inspired by the unitary coupled cluster ansatz that can be tailored to current quantum hardware. Notably, the largest experiment in Sci. Adv. 11, 25 (2025) executed a quantum circuit with 77 qubits and 10,570 gates on an IBM quantum computer and performed classical post-processing with up to 6400 nodes on Fugaku to compute ground state energies better than Hartree-Fock. In this work, we present a polynomial time classical algorithm to compute the energy of any single-layer unitary cluster Jastrow circuit, independent of locality constraints for quantum hardware. Our algorithm can reproduce the largest experiment from Sci. Adv. 11, 25 (2025) in less than a minute on a laptop, and through circuit optimization enabled by fast simulation we achieve a lower ground state energy than the experiment.

</details>


### [177] [Floquet Reservoir Engineering for Remote Logical Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21360)
*Mingxing Yao,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出耗散Floquet协议，通过将连续耗散与周期性幺正门序列交错，实现对逻辑量子比特远程纠缠的稳定化，克服了标准时不变耗散方法的时间-纠缠限制，并实现了自主纠缠蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准时不变耗散动力学的限制，实现更强大的非幺正协议，以稳定远程纠缠并克服时间-纠缠极限。

Method: 引入耗散Floquet协议，将连续运行的耗散与周期性幺正门序列交错，利用现有实验能力实现自治纠缠蒸馏。

Result: 该协议增强了对波导损耗的防护能力，并基于超导电路中猫量子比特与transmon的特定实现进行了分析。

Conclusion: 耗散Floquet协议是一种更强大的非幺正方法，能够有效稳定逻辑量子比特的远程纠缠，并具备自主纠缠蒸馏能力。

Abstract: Implementing controlled dissipative dynamics is a powerful approach for state preparation in a variety of contexts, including the preparation of remote entangled states. Here, we show that by going beyond the standard setting of time-independent dissipative dynamics, one can realize even more powerful non-unitary protocols. We introduce dissipative Floquet protocols for stabilizing remote entanglement of logical qubits, where continuously-running dissipation is interleaved with a periodic sequence of unitary gates. These protocols harness existing experimental capabilities, and overcome time-entanglement limits that constrain standard approaches. They also implement an autonomous form of entanglement distillation. We show how these protocols give enhanced protection against waveguide loss, and as an example, analyze a specific implementation using cat-qubits and transmons in a superconducting circuit.

</details>


### [178] [A solution to 2-copy distillability of Werner states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21367)
*Jinshi Fu,Li Gao,Sang-Jun Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意维度的Werner态是2-拷贝可蒸馏的当且仅当它们是1-拷贝可蒸馏的，回答了Werner态2-拷贝可蒸馏性的长期开放问题，这对解决NPT态是否均可蒸馏的核心问题具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Werner态2-拷贝可蒸馏性的长期开放问题，推动判断是否每个非正部分转置（NPT）态都是可蒸馏的这一量子信息论核心问题的解决。

Method: 通过数学证明的方法，建立Werner态在任意维度下1-拷贝可蒸馏性与2-拷贝可蒸馏性之间的等价关系。

Result: 证明了任意维度的Werner态是2-拷贝可蒸馏的当且仅当它们是1-拷贝可蒸馏的。

Conclusion: 该结果为判断所有NPT态是否均可蒸馏这一中心开放问题提供了重要进展，有助于深化对纠缠蒸馏理论的理解。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation is a fundamental task in quantum information theory. In this work, we prove that Werner states in arbitrary dimension are 2-copy distillable if and only if they are 1-copy distillable. This answers the longstanding open question of the 2-copy distillability of Werner states. This is an important step on determining whether every non-positive partial transpose (NPT) state is distillable, which remains one of the central open problems in the field of entanglement distillation.

</details>


### [179] [Cautious optimism for deep parameterized quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21409)
*Marie Kempkes,Elies Gil-Fuster,Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Aroosa Ijaz,Alissa Wilms,Jens Eisert,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Vedran Dunjko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于梯度的参数化量子电路（PQC）在模型规模增大时，会在未见数据上表现出**双重下降（double descent）**现象，即性能先下降后提升，而非传统认为的过拟合导致泛化恶化。研究通过加一扰动技术和随机矩阵谱性质提供了理论分析，并在多种数据集和重上传PQC结构上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习中一个核心挑战是理解参数化量子电路（PQC）的缩放行为，特别是其未见数据性能随可训练参数数量增加如何变化。已有的泛化保证并不能充分描述实际中的泛化行为。

Method: 利用加一扰动（add-one-in perturbation）技术和随机矩阵的谱性质进行严格理论分析，并在重上传PQC（re-uploading PQCs）上开展数值实验，涵盖多个数据集和不同训练集规模。

Result: 梯度驱动的PQC在模型规模增大时，在未见数据上表现出双重下降现象：性能先恶化后改善。更深的参数化量子电路并不必然导致泛化性能退化。

Conclusion: 尽管实际量子机器学习仍面临其他障碍，但更深PQC不一定导致性能退化这一发现提供了审慎乐观的理由。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum machine learning is understanding the scaling behavior of parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs). In particular, it remains unclear how their performance on unseen data changes as the number of trainable parameters increases. Prior works have derived formal generalization guarantees for quantum models, but it is well-known that many such results do not fully characterize generalization behavior in practice. In this work, we show that gradient-based PQCs can exhibit improved performance on unseen data as model size increases, displaying the phenomenon of double descent. This contrasts with the traditional view that larger models lead to degraded generalization. We provide analytical results rigorously underpinning this behavior by leveraging add-one-in perturbation techniques and spectral properties of random matrices. We support these results with numerical experiments on re-uploading PQCs across several data sets and training set sizes, consistently observing the predicted double descent behavior. While other obstacles on the path toward practical quantum machine learning remain, our finding that deeper parameterized quantum circuits do not necessarily exhibit degraded performance provides reasons for cautious optimism.

</details>


### [180] [QSTAR: Quantum Selective Transfer with Adaptive Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21411)
*Saim Rehman,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QSTAR框架，通过选择性路由将量子网络作为低置信度样本的备用分支，而非直接替代经典分类器。实验表明，架构搜索得到的量子头在不确定样本上优于参数匹配的MLP，但固定量子头整体仍弱于经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子迁移学习常简单地将经典分类器替换为固定变分量子头，但未回答量子分支何时真正有用的问题。

Method: 提出QSTAR（量子选择性传输与自适应路由）框架，保留高置信度经典预测，仅将低置信度样本路由到量子备用分支。使用冻结的ResNet18骨干网络，对比手工设计量子头、KetGPT设计量子头和参数匹配的经典基线。

Result: 标准QTL头最高57.0%准确率；最强KetGPT头在滤波扫描中达78.5%准确率和0.785 F1；最强固定经典头81.6%。在低置信度样本上，KetGPT #180比参数匹配的MLP备用分支在阈值0.70/0.80/0.90下分别提高6.82/4.31/3.03个百分点。自适应KetGPT-QTL达80.9%准确率和0.807 F1，优于自适应经典基线。KetGPT #160仅用10个量子参数和9个门达81.9%准确率。

Conclusion: 架构搜索得到的量子头更适合作为针对不确定输入的备用分支，而非直接替代经典分类器。

Abstract: Quantum transfer learning (QTL) is often evaluated by replacing a classical classifier with a fixed variational quantum head, but this hides a key question: when is the quantum branch actually useful? We propose QSTAR: Quantum Selective Transfer with Adaptive Routing, a selective QTL framework that keeps high-confidence classical predictions and routes only low-confidence samples to a fallback branch. Using a frozen ResNet18 backbone on Fashion-MNIST, we compare manually designed QTL heads, KetGPT-designed quantum heads, and parameter-matched classical baselines under a common data split and optimization schedule. Standard QTL heads reach at most 57.0% accuracy, while the strongest KetGPT head in the main filtered sweep reaches 78.5% accuracy and 0.785 F1-score. Although the strongest fixed classical head remains higher at 81.6%, selective routing gives the quantum branch a clearer role. On low-confidence samples, KetGPT #180 improves accuracy over a parameter-matched MLP fallback by 6.82, 4.31, and 3.03 percentage points at thresholds of 0.70, 0.80, and 0.90. At the full-system level, Adaptive KetGPT-QTL reaches 80.9% accuracy and 0.807 F1-score, outperforming the adaptive classical baseline. A separate compact-circuit ablation identifies KetGPT #160 as a stronger fixed-head candidate, reaching 81.9% accuracy with only 10 quantum parameters and 9 gates. These results suggest that architecture-searched quantum heads are most useful as targeted fallback branches for uncertain inputs rather than uniform replacements for classical classifiers.

</details>


### [181] [Exponentially enhanced two-mode multiboson entanglement via phase-modulated tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21441)
*Pritam Chattopadhyay,A. G. Kofman,Gershon Kurizki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过精确分析隧穿耦合玻色子场模式，证明了因式化的多玻色子双模态可以通过双模耦合的频闪符号翻转实现完全纠缠，且纠缠度随翻转次数呈指数增长，该效应普遍适用于双模态制备，为量子技术应用提供线性控制下的纠缠资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并证明通过简单的线性控制（耦合符号的频闪翻转）能否突破传统纠缠产生的限制，实现因式化多玻色子双模态到完全纠缠态的转化。

Method: 对隧穿耦合玻色子场模式进行精确解析分析，研究耦合符号的频闪翻转对多玻色子双模态纠缠特性的影响。

Result: 因式化多玻色子双模态通过耦合符号的频闪翻转可以变为完全纠缠态，纠缠度随翻转次数指数增长；除少数在符号翻转下保持不变的禁止态外，该效应普遍适用。

Conclusion: 这种线性控制方法为量子技术应用提供了易于实现的纠缠资源生成手段。

Abstract: The entanglement of quantum systems is commonly restricted by their coupling Hamiltonian and initial state properties. Here, we prove by exact analysis of tunnel-coupled bosonic field modes, that factorized multi-boson two-mode states can become fully entangled via stroboscopic sign flips of the two-mode coupling. Their entanglement can exponentially grow with the number of flips. With the exception of specific prohibitive states that are invariant under such sign-flip control, this effect universally applies to two-mode state preparation. Remarkably, this linear control may provide entanglement resources for diverse quantum technological applications by readily available means.

</details>


### [182] [QuantumChain: Blockchain-Backed Quantum Federated Learning for Financial Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21449)
*Epameinondas Douros,Konstantinos Dalampekis,Nouhaila Innan,Ioannis Theodonis,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuantumChain提出了一种结合量子-经典混合神经网络、同态加密、区块链审计和量子安全通信的联邦学习框架，用于解决金融欺诈检测中的数据分散、类别不平衡和隐私保护问题。实验表明，混合量子模型在欺诈召回率上达到94.6%（经典模型93.2%），联邦部署下全局准确率从97.7%提升至98.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融欺诈检测面临三大挑战：数据分散（无法集中训练）、严重的类别不平衡（欺诈样本极少）以及隐私约束。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题，需要一种兼顾隐私保护、安全通信和高检测性能的联邦学习框架。

Method: 提出QuantumChain框架，核心包括：(1) 混合量子-经典神经网络，将变分量子电路嵌入经典神经网络层之间；(2) 加密联邦聚合，使用同态加密、阈值秘密共享和QKD密钥分发保护模型更新；(3) 基于许可链的区块链记录聚合事件并支持声誉加权信任机制。

Result: (1) 混合量子神经网络在大多数设置下欺诈召回率优于经典模型，达到94.6%（经典93.2%）；(2) Deep QLayer在全数据设置下性能更优，表明增加电路深度有助于恢复浅层电路的表示能力；(3) 混合态模拟显示召回率优势在非理想量子演化下仍然保持；(4) 联邦部署（10个异构客户端）中全局准确率从97.7%提升至98.8%并稳定收敛。

Conclusion: QuantumChain成功将深度感知的混合量子模型集成到安全的联邦欺诈检测流水线中，在保持稳定全局收敛的同时提升了欺诈检测性能，验证了量子联邦学习在金融隐私保护场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Financial fraud detection is challenged by decentralized data, severe class imbalance, and privacy constraints. This paper presents QuantumChain, a secure Quantum Federated Learning (QFL) framework that combines hybrid quantum-classical neural networks, encrypted federated aggregation, blockchain-based auditability, and quantum-secure communication. Each client trains a local hybrid model in which a variational quantum circuit is embedded between classical neural layers, while model updates are protected through homomorphic encryption, threshold secret sharing, and QKD-based keying. A permissioned blockchain records aggregation events and supports reputation-weighted trust among participants. We evaluate QuantumChain on financial transaction data using a compact, size-matched classical baseline to isolate the effect of the quantum layer. Results show that the HQNN achieves comparable accuracy while improving fraud-class recall in most settings, reaching 94.6% recall compared with 93.2% for the classical model. The Deep QLayer improves performance in full-data settings, suggesting that added circuit depth helps recover representational capacity when the shallow circuit becomes limited. Mixed-state simulations further show that the recall trend persists under non-ideal quantum evolution. In federated deployment with 10 heterogeneous clients, global accuracy increases from 97.7% to 98.8% over five rounds before stabilizing. These results show that QuantumChain can integrate depth-aware hybrid quantum models into a secure federated fraud-detection pipeline while maintaining stable global convergence.

</details>


### [183] [Directional telecom photons from a chirally coupled quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21452)
*Kristina Bodiroga,Jacob Ewaniuk,Andrew N. Wakileh,Lucas Rantz,Ivanna M. Boras Vazquez,Dan Dalacu,Phillip J. Poole,Robin L. Williams,Xiao-Liu Chu,Nir Rotenberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种在原始电信波段（1260-1360 nm）工作的集成手性量子界面，通过将InAs量子点与波导耦合的InP微盘结合实现，展示了峰值腔增强3.3和发射方向性0.985的近理想手性量子耦合，为集成光子器件上的量子信息处理提供了关键构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚不存在工作在电信波段且与电信基础设施及硅光子学兼容的手性量子界面，这限制了非互易量子器件、确定性量子逻辑门和纠缠生成协议的实际应用。

Method: 将InAs量子点与波导耦合的InP微盘集成，通过强磁场调谐量子点跃迁通过光子腔，实现手性量子耦合。

Result: 峰值腔增强达3.3，发射方向性达0.985，展示了近理想的手性量子耦合。

Conclusion: 该工作在原始电信波段成功实现了集成手性量子界面，为量子信息处理在集成光子器件上的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Chiral quantum light-matter interfaces, where the internal spin state of a quantum emitter determines the direction in which it emits, are essential building blocks of non-reciprocal quantum devices, deterministic quantum logical gates and entanglement generation protocols. Yet, a chiral quantum interface that operates at telecom wavelengths, and is compatible with telecommunication infrastructure and silicon photonics, does not yet exist. Here, we report on an integrated chiral quantum interface in the original telecom band (1260-1360 nm), created by interfacing InAs quantum dots with a waveguide-coupled InP microdisk. We tune the quantum dot transitions through the photonic cavity using a strong magnetic field, observing a peak cavity enhancement of 3.3 and an emission directionality of 0.985, demonstrating the near-ideal chiral quantum coupling required for quantum information processing on integrated photonic devices.

</details>


### [184] [Rack-integrated quantum dot-based source of single and entangled photons at telecom C-band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21454)
*Michal Vyvlecka,Raphael Joos,Benjamin Breiholz,Emma Marmasse,Anna Friederike Köhler,Ponraj Vijayan,Tobias Huber-Loyola,Sven Höfling,Michael Jetter,Simone Luca Portalupi,Peter Michler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了基于半导体量子点的电信C波段纠缠光子源，实现了创纪录的符合计数率，并在优化的机架式设备中运行，传输效率超过50%，为量子互联网的实际部署迈出了关键一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子光源从实验室环境迁移到实际电信网络中，需要在技术相关的电信O波段和C波段运行确定性量子光源，这对于实现量子互联网至关重要。

Method: 采用外延生长量子点作为确定性量子光源，在优化的机架式设备中集成可调谐脉冲激光器（从准共振到全共振激发）、激发滤波光学元件以及单模光纤耦合系统，在电信C波段实现纠缠光子发射。

Result: 实现了电信C波段纠缠光子发射的创纪录符合计数率，激子和双激子光子的传输效率均超过50%。

Conclusion: 基于量子点的电信波段光源现已具备可运输性和与现有光纤基础设施的集成能力，为即将到来的量子互联网可行性验证迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: For quantum light sources in everyday telecommunication networks, quantum science needs to be fully transformed into quantum technology. The first necessary step to move outside a well-controlled lab environment requires the use of quantum light sources operating in the technologically relevant telecom O- and C-band. This can be provided using epitaxial quantum dots as deterministic sources of quantum light. Particularly intriguing is that emitters operating at telecom wavelengths are rapidly catching up with their short wavelength counterparts in terms of performances. Here, we make a decisive step forward in the development of quantum communication networks: a state-of-the-art source of quantum light, a semiconductor quantum dot (QD), with record coincidence rate for entangled photon emission in the telecom C-band, is operated inside an optimized rack-based setup. This setup includes a tunable pulsed laser for the QD excitation (from quasi- to fully resonant excitation), all optics for the excitation filtering, and QD signal coupling into single-mode fibers. Overall, the setup allows for above $50\%$ transmission for both exciton and biexciton photons. These results show that quantum dots-based telecom light sources can now be transported and integrated into existing fiber infrastructures, an important step to demonstrate the feasibility of the upcoming quantum internet.

</details>


### [185] [An Optimal Analysis of the Product Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21477)
*Jacob Beckey,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Pei Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了乘积检验（Product Test）在最坏情况下的接受概率曲线，得到了完全解析公式 PT_n(ω)=1/2(1+mω²+(1-mω)²)，其中 m=⌊1/ω⌋，解决了此前未覆盖的低重叠区域（ω<1/2）的开放问题，并改进了 QMA(k)到QMA(2)归约中的一次声音参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积检验是连接量子性质检验、非纠缠量子证明系统和张量优化的基本任务，但其精确的最坏情况接受概率曲线尚未完全确定，特别是低重叠区域（ω<1/2）的解析形式未知，且存在公开问题（ω→0时PT_n(ω)的极限行为）。

Method: 基于Soleimanifar和Wright的技术框架，通过构建和分析乘积检验在接受概率与乘积重叠之间的精确关系，利用离散化参数 m=⌊1/ω⌋ 处理不同重叠区间，结合代数推导得到封闭解析公式。

Result: 对所有 n≥2 和 ω∈(0,1]，证明了 PT_n(ω)=1/2(1+mω²+(1-mω)²)，其中 m=⌊1/ω⌋。该公式恢复了已知的 ω≥1/2 紧致区间，完全解决了 ω<1/2 的低重叠区域，并得出 ω→0 时 PT_n(ω)→1/2，回答了Soleimanifar和Wright的开放问题。在应用层面，改进了Harrow-Montanaro归约中QMA(k)到QMA(2)的一次声音参数。

Conclusion: 乘积检验的精确接受概率曲线已被完全表征，证明方法出乎意料地简洁。该结果不仅解决了量子性质检验领域的核心理论问题，还在量子复杂性理论中具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: Product testing, i.e., deciding whether a pure multipartite quantum state is fully unentangled across a specified tensor decomposition, serves as a bridge between quantum property testing, unentangled quantum proof systems, and tensor optimization. Despite being a fundamental property testing task and having many applications, the product test's exact (worst-case) acceptance probability curve has yet to be fully determined. In this work, we determine this curve exactly. Let $ω$ be the maximum squared overlap of the input with a product state, and let $\mathrm{PT}_n(ω)$ be the largest possible acceptance probability of the product test over all $n$-partite pure states with product overlap $ω$, allowing arbitrary finite local dimensions. We prove that, for every $n\ge 2 $ and every $ω\in(0,1] $, $$
  \mathrm{PT}_n(ω)=\frac12\left(1+mω^2+(1-mω)^2\right), $$ where $m=\lfloor1/ω\rfloor $. The formula recovers the previously known tight section of the curve for $ω\ge 1/2 $, resolves all low-overlap regimes $ω<1/2 $, and implies $\mathrm{PT}_n(ω)\to 1/2 $ as $ω\to 0$ answering an open problem in [Soleimanifar and Wright, SODA 2022]. As a complexity-theoretic application, our results improve the one-shot soundness parameter in the Harrow-Montanaro reduction from $\mathsf{QMA}(k)$ to $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$. Our techniques, built upon those of Soleimanifar and Wright, allow us to resolve these open questions while remaining surprisingly elementary.

</details>


### [186] [Flow of local sensitivity in a spin chain coupled to a bosonic bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21501)
*Marcin Płodzień*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究自旋链与玻色子浴（全量子多体系统）耦合时，量子Fisher信息（局部参数灵敏度）如何在自旋、浴及它们之间的量子关联中流动。通过对比Jaynes-Cummings耦合（激发数守恒）和Holstein耦合（自旋激发守恒），发现耦合对称性决定灵敏度去向：Holstein耦合将灵敏度完全存入自旋-浴关联中；Jaynes-Cummings耦合则将灵敏度传递给浴。浴的频谱决定灵敏度是否返回自旋——若频率有公周期则完全周期性恢复，否则因退相永不返回。研究还指出，传输的计量寄存器损失的灵敏度大于其到达保真度所暗示的值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子系统中参数灵敏度的流动机制，特别是超越Lindblad主方程近似下，自旋链与全量子浴耦合时量子Fisher信息的迁移规律，以及耦合对称性如何影响灵敏度分布。

Method: 通过分析两种不同的耦合模型（激发数交换的Jaynes-Cummings耦合与自旋激发守恒的Holstein耦合），在哈密顿量层面研究耦合对称性如何直接决定灵敏度（量子Fisher信息）在自旋、浴及量子关联中的流向，并分析浴频谱对灵敏度回复的影响。

Result: Holstein耦合条件下，自旋灵敏度损失完全由自旋-浴关联承载，浴本身不获得任何一阶相位信息；Jaynes-Cummings耦合则会将灵敏度传递给浴（单激发下完全传递）。浴频谱特性决定灵敏度能否返回自旋：频率有公周期时完全周期性恢复，否则因不同模态退相而永不恢复。此外，传输的计量寄存器灵敏度损失大于到达保真度所暗示的值。

Conclusion: 耦合对称性从根本上决定了量子参数灵敏度在多体系统中的流动路径与分布，浴的频谱结构对灵敏度恢复至关重要，且灵敏度损失不能仅用保真度衡量。

Abstract: We study how local sensitivity to an encoded parameter, captured by the quantum Fisher information, flows between a spin chain, a coupled bosonic bath, and their quantum correlations, where we treat the bath as a full many-body quantum system beyond the Lindbladian approximation. We analyze how the coupling symmetry determines the destination of the departed sensitivity directly at the level of the Hamiltonian. We consider an excitation-exchanging Jaynes--Cummings coupling, which preserves the total number of excitations, and a spin-excitation-conserving Holstein coupling. We find that the Holstein coupling leaves the bath with no first-order information about the phase and stores the lost sensitivity entirely in spin--bath correlations, whereas the Jaynes--Cummings coupling passes this sensitivity to the bath, in full for a single excitation. The bath spectrum then governs whether the sensitivity ever returns to the spins. It revives fully and periodically when the coupled-system frequencies share a common period, but when those frequencies disperse the departed share dephases across the bath modes and never returns. We note that a transported metrological register loses more sensitivity than its arrival fidelity implies.

</details>


### [187] [Extended Single-Atom Tweezer Arrays in High-Cooperativity Cavity-QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21515)
*Thomas Picot,Clément Raphin,Marcel Kern,Pierre-Antoine Bourdel,Théo Ahamdach,Jakob Reichel,Romain Long*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了将单个$^{87}$Rb原子的光镊阵列集成到光纤法布里-珀罗微腔中，实现了单原子协同性$\mathcal{C} \sim 90$，并能在平均原子数$\bar{N} \simeq 36$的阵列中同时实现无背景荧光成像和与公共腔模的集体耦合，为多体腔量子电动力学提供了高协同性平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔QED量子技术的核心挑战是使高协同性光学接口与可寻址的单原子阵列兼容。

Method: 将$^{87}$Rb原子的光镊阵列集成到光纤法布里-珀罗微腔中，结合无背景的位点分辨荧光成像技术与公共腔模的集体耦合。

Result: 实现了单原子协同性$\mathcal{C} \sim 90$，在平均原子数$\bar{N} \simeq 36$的阵列中实现了集体耦合与位点分辨成像。

Conclusion: 建立了具有位点分辨探测和控制能力的高协同性多体腔QED平台。

Abstract: A central challenge for cavity-QED-based quantum technologies is to make high-cooperativity optical interfaces compatible with site-resolved arrays of single atoms. Here, we demonstrate optical tweezer arrays of individual $^{87}$Rb atoms inside a fiber Fabry-Perot microcavity with single-atom cooperativity $\mathcal{C} \sim 90$. We combine background-free site-resolved fluorescence imaging of extended arrays with collective coupling to a common cavity mode for arrays with a mean atom number up to $\bar{N} \simeq 36$. These results establish a high-cooperativity platform for many-body cavity-QED with site-resolved detection and control.

</details>


### [188] [Unified theory of classical and quantum semiparametric efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21524)
*Mankei Tsang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了经典和量子半参数模型的统一统计效率理论，将Cramér-Rao界和Helstrom界推广到无限维参数，并应用于相干和非相干光学成像，证明了空间模式解复用优于直接成像，更接近量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维未知参数在经典和量子统计中普遍存在，传统方法通常对参数做过多假设。为了在最小假设下处理广泛的半参数问题，需要建立统一的理论框架，将统计效率分析从有限维推广到无限维参数空间。

Method: 采用抽象几何语言建立半参数模型的统一处理框架，推广Cramér-Rao界和Helstrom界；利用奇异值分解(SVD)揭示通道的统计效应；将理论应用于高斯场和泊松场等经典与量子模型，以及相干和非相干光学成像问题。

Result: 建立了无限维参数空间下经典和量子半参数模型的统计效率统一理论；对于亚衍射非相干成像，证明了空间模式解复用(Spatial-mode demultiplexing)在估计广义傅里叶系数方面远优于直接成像，更接近量子极限。

Conclusion: 该理论将成为经典和量子统计的重要工具，特别是在需要对高维参数做最小假设的传感和成像应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: In classical and quantum statistics, high-dimensional unknown parameters are abundant and it is often prudent to make minimal assumptions about them using so-called semiparametric models. To attack a wide range of semiparametric problems in one broad stroke, we present a unified treatment of statistical efficiency for classical and quantum semiparametric models, generalizing the Cramér-Rao and Helstrom bounds beyond finite-dimensional parameters. We introduce the fundamental concepts in abstract and geometric terms before applying them to many examples, covering general classical and quantum models as well as the paradigmatic special cases of Gaussian and Poisson fields. We give an in-depth treatment of channels in the semiparametric efficiency theory and advocate the use of the singular value decomposition to elucidate the statistical effects of channels. To demonstrate the utility of the formalism, we apply it to coherent and incoherent optical imaging problems, assuming an arbitrary field or intensity on the object plane without parametric assumptions. Our formalism enables us to compute classical and quantum limits to coherent and incoherent imaging resolution in statistical terms. For subdiffraction incoherent imaging, we demonstrate that spatial-mode demultiplexing can be far superior to direct imaging in estimating generalized Fourier coefficients and come closer to the quantum limits. We envision our theory becoming an essential tool for both classical and quantum statistics with useful applications to sensing and imaging, whenever minimal assumptions about a high-dimensional parameter should be made.

</details>


### [189] [Benchmarking Agents for Proving Theorems in Quantum Algorithms and Quantum Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21533)
*Lei Zhang,Yusheng Zhao,Yimeng Cao,Ranyiliu Chen,Mingrui Jing,Jizhe Lai,Ziao Tang,Jingu Xie,Hongshun Yao,Xuanqiang Zhao,Guocheng Zhen,Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个量子计算领域的Lean 4定理证明基准（Lean-QuantumAlg-Bench和Lean-QIT-Bench），包含36和40个任务，评估了四种AI模型在两种设置下的表现，最高加权得分为60.4和59.6，发现库增强策略能提升性能，并揭示了模型在量子模拟、学习、信息度量和纠缠理论等领域的常见弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证在量子计算中日益实用，但AI智能体在此领域中构建机器可验证证明的能力尚未被测量，因此需要建立标准化的基准来评估和推动这一方向的发展。

Method: 构建了量子算法和量子信息论两个Lean 4定理证明基准，共76个定理补全任务；采用确定性证明检查和针对性语义审查进行评估；在任务基线（task-only）和库增强演绎（LAD）两种设置下测试了GPT-5.5、Kimi K3、DeepSeek V4-Pro和MiniMax M3四个模型。

Result: 量子算法基准最高难度加权得分为60.4/100，量子信息基准为59.6/100；LAD在全部8个模型-基准对比中均提升了得分和完成率，最高提升15.9分；不同模型在得分、时间和金钱成本之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 验证过的领域库能增强特定领域的证明智能体能力；模型在量子模拟、量子学习、量子信息度量和纠缠理论等方面存在持续性弱点；该基准为开发更强大、更可靠的证明智能体提供了可复现基线，并为迈向自我进化的AI科学家奠定基础。

Abstract: Formal verification is becoming increasingly practical for quantum computing, yet the ability of AI agents to construct machine-checkable proofs in this domain remains unmeasured. We introduce Lean-QuantumAlg-Bench and Lean-QIT-Bench, two Lean 4 benchmarks containing 36 and 40 theorem-completion tasks for quantum algorithms and quantum information theory, respectively. Every task compiles in a fixed environment and is evaluated by deterministic proof checking and targeted semantic review, with difficulty weights assigned before model execution. We evaluate four models-GPT-5.5, Kimi K3, DeepSeek V4-Pro, and MiniMax M3-within a common theorem-proving framework under two settings: a task-only baseline and library-augmented deduction (LAD), which additionally provides access to a verified domain library. The highest difficulty-weighted scores are 60.4 out of 100 on the quantum-algorithm benchmark and 59.6 out of 100 on the quantum-information benchmark. LAD improves both score and completion rate in all eight model-benchmark comparisons, with gains of up to 15.9 points, providing evidence that verified libraries can strengthen domain-specific proof agents. The results reveal recurring weaknesses of agentic proving in areas such as quantum simulation, quantum learning, quantum information measures, and entanglement theory. Monetary and wall-clock costs per score point also vary substantially across models, highlighting important capability-efficiency trade-offs. We expect these benchmarks to establish a reproducible baseline for developing more capable and reliable proof agents, and to pave the way toward self-evolving AI scientists for advancing quantum information science.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Calabrese-Cardy Scaling: Exceptional-Point Sensitivity from the de Sitter RT Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21536)
*Kuang-Hung Chou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 非厄米临界链中，双正交熵在有限系统下对亚有限尺寸能隙Δ（即使Δ<1/L）表现出额外敏感性，产生与区间无关的残余熵S_res=log(ΔL)，这在厄米系统中不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米临界链在例外点附近的双正交熵行为，揭示其与厄米系统在纠缠熵标度上的本质差异。

Method: 通过非厄米连续多尺度纠缠重整化（cMERA）产生的de Sitter几何解释，有限环上的正则电路终止于纠缠的两体IR态，计算该IR态的纠缠得到log(ΔL)项。

Result: 发现双正交熵在标准Calabrese-Cardy项之外存在区间无关的残余项S_res=log(ΔL)，即使对于单比特子系统也可探测，且无厄米类比。

Conclusion: 非厄米临界链的纠缠熵能分辨亚有限尺寸能隙，源于非幺正cMERA中dS极端面到达IR端点，保留端点贡献；残余熵可解释为有限深度解纠缠后剩余的长程纠缠。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy at one-dimensional criticality typically follows the Calabrese-Cardy scaling. In non-Hermitian critical chains near exceptional points, however, we show that the biorthogonal entropy of a finite system retains an additional sensitivity to a small energy gap \(Δ\) even when \(Δ< 1/L\). On top of the usual Calabrese-Cardy term, we find an interval-independent contribution \(S_{\rm res}=\log(ΔL)\), visible as a vertical offset and detectable even for a one-site subsystem. This behavior has no Hermitian analogue: a sub-finite-size gap is effectively invisible to entanglement in unitary critical chains, whereas here the entropy continues to resolve such a gap through its dependence on \(ΔL\). We interpret the result within the de Sitter geometry generated by non-unitary continuous multiscale entanglement renormalization: because the dS extremal surface reaches the IR endpoint, entanglement necessarily retains the endpoint contribution. On a finite ring, a regular circuit cannot terminate at a one-site product state and instead leaves an entangled two-site IR state. Computing the entanglement of the IR state recovers the same \(\log(ΔL)\) term, identifying this additional long-range entanglement as the residual entropy left after finite-depth disentangling.

</details>


### [191] [The trainability of photonic quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21544)
*Alexander Makarovskiy,Adam Taylor,Zhenghao Li,Michael Hanks,Aubrey Clark,M. S. Kim,Ian Walmsley,William Clements*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无源线性光学量子电路的可训练性，提出了基于样本方差与电路方差比值的框架，发现固定阶光子数多项式只需多项式数量样本即可训练，而高阶多项式和输出概率可观测性则需要指数级样本，并在可训练区域内识别出多项式加速的可观测性类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法在近期量子计算中占据重要地位，但其可扩展性受到贫瘠高原和测量成本（尤其是分辨损失景观微小变化所需的采样成本）的限制。本文旨在系统研究无源线性光学量子电路的可训练性，并量化其采样复杂度。

Method: 引入样本方差与电路方差比值框架，用于确定达到比例精度所需的电路样本数；将该框架应用于光子数可观测性，通过解析分析和数值实验研究电路方差的多项式衰减行为。

Result: 固定阶光子数多项式只需多项式数量样本；高阶多项式和基于输出概率的可观测性通常需要指数级样本；在可训练区域中，某些可观测性类别（如神经网络可观测性）可实现量子估计相对于经典方法的多项式加速。

Conclusion: 光量子变分量子计算是近期应用的可行平台，在可训练性和量子加速方面具有优势。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms are a leading approach to near-term quantum computing, but their scalability can be limited by barren plateaus and the sampling cost of resolving small changes in the loss landscape. Here, we study the trainability of passive linear-optical quantum circuits and introduce a framework based on the ratio of sample variance to circuit variance. This ratio determines the number of circuit samples required to resolve local loss differences and gradients to proportional accuracy. We apply this framework to photon-number observables and identify both trainable and non-trainable regimes. Supported by analytic results and a numerically observed polynomial decay of the circuit variance, we find that fixed-order photon-number polynomials require only polynomially many samples as the system size grows, whereas high-order polynomials and observables based on output probabilities generally require exponentially many samples. Within the trainable regime, we further identify classes of observables in which quantum estimation achieves a polynomial speed-up over multiple classical methods. Within this family, neural network observables provide one practical construction that allow measurement outcomes to be efficiently processed into the desired polynomial. These results establish photonic variational quantum computing as a promising platform for near-term applications.

</details>


### [192] [Unconditional Unclonable Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21551)
*Prabhanjan Ananth,Amit Sahai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了一种针对单比特消息的、信息论安全的一次性私钥不可克隆加密方案的无条件构造，具有高效加解密和指数级小的不可克隆不可区分优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可克隆加密方案多依赖计算假设或量子资源，本文希望在信息论安全模型下，无条件构造一次性的私钥不可克隆加密方案，以实现更基础的安全性保证。

Method: 采用信息论安全的构造方法，设计了一次性私钥不可克隆加密方案，针对单比特消息，通过私钥机制实现加密与解密的高效性，并保证不可克隆的不可区分优势呈指数级小。

Result: 成功构造了信息论安全的不可克隆加密方案，实现了指数级小的不可克隆不可区分优势，且加密解密过程高效。

Conclusion: 本文的工作首次无条件实现了信息论安全的不可克隆加密，为相关密码学原语提供了更扎实的理论基础。

Abstract: We give an unconditional construction of information-theoretically secure one-time private-key unclonable encryption scheme for one-bit messages, with efficient encryption and decryption and exponentially small unclonable-indistinguishability advantage.

</details>


### [193] [Strategic Plan for Neutral Atom Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21554)
*Adrian J. Menssen,Tout Wang,Michael Gullans,Tom Manovitz,Jacob M. Taylor,Jason Cong,Josiah Sinclair,Ziv Aqua,Daniel J. Blumenthal,J. Pablo Bonilla Ataides,Johannes Borregaard,Antoine Browaeys,Paola Cappellaro,Soonwon Choi,Alexandre Cooper,Robin Côté,Jacob P. Covey,Alexandre Dauphin,Ivana Dimitrova,Matt Eichenfield,Dirk Englund,Jacob Freedman,Akihisa Goban,Brandon Grinkemeyer,Andi Gu,Ruonan Han,Dominik Hangleiter,Aram W. Harrow,Liang Jiang,Eun-ah Kim,Felix W. Knollmann,Aleksander Kubica,Thierry Lahaye,Lucas Lassabliere,Joonho Lee,Bingzhao Li,Mo Li,Wan-Hsuan Lin,Mikhail D. Lukin,Varun Menon,Thomas Propson,Akbar Safari,Mark Saffman,Pascal Scholl,Alexander Schuckert,Giulia Semeghini,Jonathan Simon,David Spierings,Daniel Bochen Tan,Shai Tsesses,Vladan Vuletic,Hanrui Wang,Hanyu Wang,Susanne Yelin,Johannes Zeiher,Hengyun Zhouckert,Giulia Semeghini,Jonathan Simon,David Spierings,Daniel Bochen Tan,Shai Tsesses,Vladan Vuletic,Hanrui Wang,Hanyu Wang,Susanne Yelin,Johannes Zeiher,Hengyun Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了中性原子量子计算的战略规划，结合硬件发展与理论进展，旨在实现实用量子优势。定义了实用量子优势及其验证方法，设计了相应的量子算法。描述了中性原子量子处理器硬件的发展方向：系统规模扩展、量子比特编码与原子平台、逻辑量子比特性能优化、量子比特连续重加载、快速读出，以及可扩展集成光子控制技术。同时提出了量子纠错和量子电路编译的新进展，并探讨了通过多处理器网络实现分布式量子计算的机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现中性原子量子计算的实用量子优势，提出从硬件到算法的全面战略规划，以克服当前量子计算面临的规模化与性能瓶颈。

Method: 结合中性原子量子处理器硬件升级（系统扩展、量子比特编码、连续重加载、快速读出、集成光子控制）与理论进展（量子纠错、电路编译优化），并通过多处理器网络实现分布式量子计算。

Result: 提出了中性原子量子计算实现实用量子优势的完整路线图，涵盖硬件规模化、逻辑量子比特性能提升、光子集成控制技术以及分布式计算架构等关键方向。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子计算通过协同推进硬件与理论发展，有望实现实用量子优势，而多处理器网络化分布式计算可进一步克服单系统局限性。

Abstract: We present a strategic plan for neutral atom quantum computation, bringing together hardware development and theory advancements to achieve the goal of practical quantum advantage. The concept of practical quantum advantage is defined, along with how to verify claims of advantage, and approaches to designing quantum algorithms that deliver practical advantage. Future directions for neutral atom quantum processor hardware are described: scaling-up system size, Qubit encodings and atomic platforms, going further below threshold with neutral-atom logical-qubit performance, continuous reloading of qubits, and fast readout. We also explore opportunities for scalable integrated photonic control technologies. Alongside hardware advancements, new developments in quantum error correction and compilation of quantum circuits are proposed. Finally, we examine the opportunity of networking multiple neutral atom quantum processors together to perform distributed quantum computing and overcome possible limitations of a single system.

</details>


### [194] [Enhancing Entanglement Purification with Shared Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21555)
*Allen Zang,Bikun Li,Xinan Chen,Eric Chitambar,Liang Jiang,Martin Suchara,Tian Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 当纠缠源标签不可用时，利用经典共享随机性和缓冲存储器对多个纠缠分发轮次进行累积和洗牌，可以在不进行状态表征或电路优化的情况下提升纠缠纯化协议的成功概率和输出保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际纠缠源往往是异构的，且源标签在EPP层可能不可用，这限制了纠缠纯化的性能。需要一种不依赖状态表征或电路优化的通用增强方法。

Method: 提出累积-洗牌策略：先累积多个纠缠分发轮次，然后利用经典共享随机性对存储的纠缠态进行洗牌，再打包输入到固定的双局域Clifford EPP中。理论上证明了对于任意n个Werner源和任意固定的n-to-1双局域Clifford EPP，该策略在期望成功概率和成功加权的输出贝尔保真度上均优于基线方法。

Result: 证明对于任意n、任意有限积累轮次以及渐近极限下，累积和洗牌策略都能提升期望成功概率和成功加权的输出贝尔保真度，且提升幅度随积累轮次单调增加。

Conclusion: 经典共享随机性和缓冲存储器可以在源标签不可知的异构纠缠源场景下，有效增强纠缠纯化性能，提供了一种无需状态表征或电路优化的实用方案。

Abstract: Entanglement purification protocols (EPPs) are essential for improving entanglement fidelity to support fault-tolerant distributed quantum information processing. Practical entanglement sources are often heterogeneous and source labels may be unavailable at the EPP layer. We show that classical shared randomness, together with buffer memories, can enhance entanglement purification when source labels are unavailable, without state characterization or EPP circuit optimization. The strategy is to accumulate multiple entanglement distribution rounds and then use shared randomness to shuffle all the stored entangled states before packaging them as inputs to the EPP. For any $n$ Werner sources and any fixed $n$-to-1 bilocal Clifford EPP, we prove that accumulating and shuffling improves the expected success probability and the success-weighted output Bell fidelity over the baseline without accumulating and shuffling, for every $n$, for every finite number of accumulation rounds and in the asymptotic limit, and the improvement increases monotonically with the number of accumulation rounds.

</details>


### [195] [Fault-tolerant quantum algorithms for simulating atomic nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21563)
*James Benstead,Michael Garn,Neil Gaspar,Sean Greenaway,Angus Kan,Lloyd La Ronde,Chandan Sarma,Paul Stevenson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建并编译了基于壳模型和无核壳模型哈密顿量的原子核结构量子算法，首次提供了容错量子计算模拟原子核的Toffoli门和量子比特资源估计，发现中等质量核的壳模型模拟资源需求与Femoco化学基准相当，而轻核的无核壳模型模拟则需更高资源，亟需定制化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展容错量子计算机在核物理领域的应用，弥补量子计算社区对原子核结构模拟关注不足的空白，推动核结构与电子结构问题之间的方法借鉴与跨领域合作。

Method: 基于有效壳模型哈密顿量和手征有效场论导出的含三体相互作用的无核壳模型哈密顿量，构造并编译量子算法，并估算Toffoli门数和量子比特数等资源需求。

Result: $^{32}$Mg和$^{219}$At的壳模型资源估计与Femoco模拟相当；无核壳模型用于轻核（约$^{40}$Ca及以下）时资源需求显著更高。

Conclusion: 壳模型核模拟在容错量子计算上具备可行性，但无核壳模型模拟需更专门化的策略方可实用化；作者希望促进核物理与量子计算社区间的长期合作。

Abstract: To maximize the value of fault-tolerant quantum computers, it is essential to develop concrete applications beyond well-established domains such as chemistry and condensed-matter physics. Here we construct and compile quantum algorithms to simulate the structure of atomic nuclei -- a topic that has received relatively little attention from the quantum computing community despite its similarities to the electronic structure problem in chemistry -- via effective shell-model Hamiltonians and no-core-shell-model Hamiltonians with three-body interactions derived from chiral effective field theory. Furthermore, we provide quantum resource estimates, in terms of Toffoli gate and qubit counts, for these algorithms, which, to our knowledge, are the first such estimates for fault-tolerant quantum simulation of atomic nuclei. Notably, the estimates for $^{32}$Mg and $^{219}$At shell-model Hamiltonians are comparable to recent estimates of Femoco simulations, a standard benchmark in chemistry. For no-core-shell-model Hamiltonians suitable for light nuclei (up to $^{40}$Ca or so), we find that resource requirements are significantly higher, suggesting that more bespoke strategies are required to make such simulations practicable. Throughout this work, we draw upon the similarities between nuclear and electronic structure problems, while also highlighting challenges that are specific to the former. We hope this work will spur long-term collaborations between the nuclear and quantum computing community with the ultimate goal of realizing useful nuclear simulations on quantum computers.

</details>


### [196] [A geometric framework for spin relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21569)
*Sophia N. Fricke,Kieren A. Harkins,Ashok Ajoy,Jeffrey A. Reimer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架，将自旋弛豫描述为单个协变弛豫张量，而非传统的纵向R1和横向R2两个独立标量率，并通过超极化13C金刚石NV色心实验验证了弛豫的方向性和张量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋弛豫理论中纵向(R1)和横向(R2)弛豫率被视为独立的现象学速率，这种分离掩盖了二者更深层的结构统一性。作者旨在建立一个几何框架，将弛豫统一为单个协变张量，揭示其内在几何结构。

Method: 开发了Liouville空间中单一协变弛豫张量的几何框架；使用超极化13C金刚石NV色心自旋系统进行实验；通过对比对易脉冲序列（产生近似对角化弛豫矩阵）与非对易脉冲序列（产生与发射机频率偏移和脉冲顺序相关的非对角分量）验证张量性质；通过几何相位测量分离非对易动力学中的序依赖效应与耗散效应。

Result: 对易脉冲序列产生近似对角的弛豫矩阵；非对易序列产生可观测的非对角分量，其大小随发射机频率偏移和脉冲顺序变化；几何相位测量显示非对易动力学引入的序依赖效应可与耗散分离。

Conclusion: 该几何框架统一了Bloch、Redfield和Lindblad描述于坐标无关的表述中，为驱动的、各向异性的和非平衡自旋系统的弛豫提供了自然语言，表明弛豫本质上是状态流形上的几何输运过程。

Abstract: Spin relaxation is conventionally described by two independent phenomenological rates - longitudinal ($R_1$) and transverse ($R_2$) - whose separation obscures a deeper structural unity. Here we develop a geometric framework in which dissipation is represented by a single covariant relaxation tensor acting in Liouville space, from which $R_1$ and $R_2$ emerge as complementary projections. This tensor structure is not merely formal but is experimentally accessible through pulse sequences that probe noncommuting directions in spin space. Using hyperpolarized $^{13}C$ spins in diamond with nitrogen-vacancy centers, we show that commuting pulse trains yield effective relaxation matrices that are approximately diagonal, while noncommuting sequences produce off-diagonal components that vary with transmitter frequency offset and pulse ordering, providing evidence that relaxation is a directional process governed by a tensor rather than a pair of scalar rates. Complementary measurements of geometric phase demonstrate that noncommuting dynamics introduce ordering-dependent effects that are separable from dissipation, consistent with the interpretation of relaxation as geometric transport on the state manifold. This framework unifies Bloch, Redfield, and Lindblad descriptions within a coordinate-independent formulation and provides a natural language for relaxation in driven, anisotropic, and non-equilibrium spin systems.

</details>


### [197] [Flow-based Phase-space Tomography of Continuous-variable Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21584)
*Owen Dugan,Rumen Dangovski,Peter Y. Lu,Di Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QST-Flow框架，通过基于流的生成模型对相空间准概率分布进行归一化、可采样的神经密度建模，实现连续变量量子态层析成像，在多种非高斯态上展现出比先前机器学习方法更优的重构性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子态层析成像受限于高维相空间中非高斯结构的分辨成本，现有方法依赖截断密度矩阵，无法高效处理非高斯态。

Method: 提出QST-Flow框架，包含两个变体：QST-QFlow用单一归一化流建模正定的Husimi-Q函数；QST-WFlow用两个归一化流的可训练差值建模变号的Wigner函数。该方法保持准概率归一化，支持精确密度评估、直接采样和基于重要性采样的有限测量学习，无需固定网格。

Result: 在非高斯猫态、二项式态、Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill态、数态和Fock态上实现精确单模重构，可扩展至多模态，对含噪Wigner数据具有鲁棒性，重构误差优于先前的机器学习层析方法。

Conclusion: QST-Flow为可扩展、测量高效的相空间层析成像开辟了新路径，适用于非经典玻色子系统。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum state tomography is limited by the cost of resolving non-Gaussian structure in high-dimensional phase space. We introduce QST-Flow, a quantum state tomography framework via flow-based generative modeling that represents experimentally accessible phase-space quasiprobability distributions with normalized, samplable neural densities rather than a truncated density matrix. The framework has two variants: QST-QFlow models the positive Husimi-$Q$ function with a single normalizing flow, while QST-WFlow models sign-changing Wigner functions as a trainable difference of two normalized flows. This construction preserves quasiprobability normalization and enables exact density evaluation, direct sampling, and importance-sampled learning from finite phase-space measurements without a fixed grid. Benchmarks on non-Gaussian cat, binomial, Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill, number, and Fock states show accurate single-mode reconstructions, extension to multimode states, robustness on noisy Wigner data, and improved reconstruction error compared with prior machine-learning tomography methods. QST-Flow opens a promising route toward scalable, measurement-efficient phase-space tomography of nonclassical bosonic systems.

</details>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [A Single-Trace Surface Integral Equation Solver for Simulation of Open Bianisotropic Metasurfaces Described by Generalized Sheet Transition Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20659)
*Sebastian Celis Sierra,Junze Shao,Ran Zhao,Rui Chen,Partha Mondal,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于单迹面积分方程和广义薄层跃迁条件的开源双各向异性超表面仿真求解器，相比多迹线方法使用更少未知数且误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超表面仿真方法通常使用多迹线公式（需要两组等效表面电流），计算复杂度较高；同时需要人工闭合结构处理开源几何体。因此需要一种更高效、可直接建模开源超表面的单迹线求解器。

Method: 采用单组等效表面电流代替传统的两组电流；将两侧散射场通过面积分算子表示并代入GSTCs进行约束；使用Rao-Wilton-Glisson基函数离散方程组；直接处理开源超表面，无需人工闭合。

Result: 在极化旋转和完美反射案例中与解析解吻合；成功建模宽频吸收器（磁化率张量从全波仿真数据提取）；单迹线公式相比多迹线公式使用更少未知数且误差更低。

Conclusion: 该单迹线GSTC-SIE求解器高效、精确，适用于平面和曲面开源双各向异性超表面仿真，优于传统多迹线方法。

Abstract: A single-trace surface integral equation (SIE) solver incorporating generalized sheet transition conditions (GSTCs) is presented for the simulation of three-dimensional (3D) open bianisotropic metasurfaces. The metasurface is modeled as an infinitesimally thin, non-enclosing sheet across which the GSTCs enforce the electromagnetic field discontinuities through four surface susceptibility tensors. The proposed solver uses a single set of equivalent surface currents on the sheet, in place of the two sets used by prior multi-trace formulations. The scattered fields on both faces of the sheet, expressed through SIE operators acting on these currents, are substituted into the GSTCs. The resulting system of equations is then discretized using Rao--Wilton--Glisson basis functions. This solver models an open metasurface directly, without an artificial closure, and applies to both planar and curved geometries. It is validated against analytical solutions for polarization rotation and perfect reflection, and is used to model a realistic broadband absorber whose susceptibility tensors are retrieved from full-wave simulation data. A direct comparison shows that the single-trace formulation attains lower error than a multi-trace formulation while using significantly fewer unknowns.

</details>


### [199] [Matrix-free phase-field modeling of fracture in micromechanical testing simulations of inelastic materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21150)
*Fabio Di Gioacchino,Rezgar Shakeri,Zachary Atkins,Karen Stengel,Layla Ghaffari,Jeremy Thompson,Jed Brown*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文在开源固体力学库中实现了面向粘弹塑性材料的相场断裂模型，采用高阶有限元与矩阵自由方法，在GPU上通过p-多重网格预处理器实现高性能计算。通过引入串联流变断裂单元，使非弹性和断裂特性仅在均匀化尺度上相互影响。在El Capitan原型机上对合成颗粒-基体微结构进行拉伸/压缩模拟，成功再现了特征非弹性响应与裂纹扩展模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂建模中，陡峭损伤梯度跨越弥散裂纹需要高阶有限元以获得精度，而矩阵自由方法可提供更优的性能和可扩展性，因此需要开发高效的高性能计算实现。

Method: 在开源固体力学库中实现Peric & Dettmer本构框架，采用支持GPU上p-多重网格预处理的高阶有限元矩阵自由算子；引入串联组装的流变断裂单元，使非弹性和断裂特性仅在均匀化长度尺度上相互影响。

Result: 在El Capitan高性能计算原型机上对合成颗粒-基体微结构进行拉伸和压缩数值模拟，成功再现了特征非弹性响应和裂纹扩展模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效模拟粘弹塑性材料的断裂行为，结合高阶有限元、矩阵自由方法和GPU计算，为大规模相场断裂模拟提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Resolving steep damage gradients across diffuse cracks in the phase-field modeling of fracture favors the use of high-order finite elements, for which matrix-free methods can provide superior performance and scalability. Here, we implement the Peric & Dettmer constitutive framework for visco-elastoplastic materials in an open source solid mechanics library supporting matrix-free operators for high-order finite elements with p-multigrid preconditioning on GPUs. We introduce a rheological fracture element assembled in series so that inelastic and fracture properties can appear to affect each other only at homogenization length scales. Numerical simulations of tensile and compressive tests are conducted for synthetic particle-matrix microstructures on an El Capitan high performance computing prototype. Results are shown to reproduce characteristic inelastic responses and crack propagation patterns.

</details>


### [200] [Cycle-Consistent and Uncertainty-Aware Neural Surrogates for Tokamak Edge Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21407)
*Abdourahmane Diaw,Sebastian De Pascuale,Jae-Sun Park,Ivan Paradela Perez,Jeremy D. Lore,Stefan Dasbach*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种循环一致性神经代理模型，将条件U-Net正向模型与基于优化逆方法相结合，用于托卡马克边界等离子体模拟。正向模型将五个控制参数映射到SOLPS-ITER网格上的二维等离子体状态场，逆方法通过循环一致性实现无监督质量检查。正向模型归一化RMSE低于2.6%，Pearson相关系数高于0.95；循环一致性正则化将平均循环R²从0.59提升至0.99，所有五个控制参数恢复的Pearson r≥0.97。模型仅约4×10⁶参数，可在毫秒级生成完整2D预测，比SOLPS-ITER快五到六个数量级，支持实时控制、参数扫描和数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界和偏滤器等离子体决定了托卡马克的功率和粒子排出，但传统边缘模拟（如SOLPS-ITER）速度太慢，无法用于参数扫描、优化或实时控制。现有机器学习代理模型多为前向模型，无法从观测数据恢复输入参数或评估预测可靠性。

Method: 结合条件U-Net正向模型与基于优化的逆方法（利用冻结的正向网络），通过循环一致性正则化实现正向与逆预测的相互约束；同时使用多层感知机集成预测电子温度和密度剖面并提供不确定性估计；采用k-d树暖启动策略提高逆求解的数据库补全率。

Result: 正向模型在所有场上归一化RMSE低于2.6%、Pearson相关系数高于0.95；循环一致性正则化使平均循环R²从0.59提升至0.99且不降低正向精度；五个控制参数恢复的Pearson r≥0.97；k-d树暖启动使数据库补全率超过95%（冷启动约30%完全失败）；模型参数约4×10⁶，预测速度比SOLPS-ITER快五到六个数量级。

Conclusion: 该循环一致性神经代理模型有效解决了边缘等离子体模拟中正向预测与参数恢复的双向问题，同时提供预测可靠性评估，计算速度极大提升，可支持实时控制、参数扫描、不确定性分析和数字孪生等应用。

Abstract: The boundary and divertor plasma govern how a tokamak exhausts power and particles, setting heat fluxes, target conditions, and the onset of detachment. Predicting these quantities is essential for operating current and future devices, but edge simulations that resolve them are too slow for parameter scans, optimization, or real-time control. Machine-learning surrogates offer a fast alternative, yet most are forward-only: they cannot recover input parameters from observations or assess the reliability of their predictions. We introduce a cycle-consistent neural surrogate for edge plasmas, combining a conditional U-Net forward model with an optimization-based inverse method built on the frozen forward network. The forward model maps five control parameters to two-dimensional plasma-state fields on the SOLPS-ITER mesh; the inverse method enforces consistency between forward and inverse predictions, a self-supervised quality check needing no ground-truth labels at inference. An ensemble of multilayer perceptrons also predicts electron temperature and density profiles at the outboard midplane and divertor targets, with uncertainty estimates that flag where more simulations are needed. The forward model achieves normalized root-mean-square errors below 2.6% and Pearson correlations above 0.95 for all fields. Cycle-consistency regularization raises the average cyclical $R^2$ from 0.59 to 0.99 without degrading forward accuracy and enables recovery of the core fueling rate; all five control parameters are recovered with Pearson $r\ge0.97$. A $k$-d tree warm start yields a database completion rate above 95%, versus roughly 30% outright failures when cold-started. With about $4\times10^6$ parameters, the model produces full 2D predictions in milliseconds, five to six orders of magnitude faster than SOLPS-ITER, enabling real-time control, parameter scans, uncertainty analysis, and digital twins.

</details>
